const errorDiv=document.getElementById('pdf-tool-error'),previewDiv=document.getElementById('pdf-tool-preview'),spinner=document.getElementById('pdf-tool-spinner'),progressBar=document.getElementById('pdf-tool-progressBar'),progressFill=document.getElementById('pdf-tool-progressFill');

//const converterQuality=document.getElementById('converterQuality'),converterQualityVal=document.getElementById('converterQualityVal');

function debounce(fn,d){let t;return function(...a){clearTimeout(t);t=setTimeout(()=>fn.apply(this,a),d)}}
/* Allow only digits, comma, hyphen and spaces in page-range inputs (Remove / Split) */
function restrictPageRangeInput(el){
  if(!el)return;
  const clean=()=>{const v=el.value;const cleaned=v.replace(/[^0-9,\-\s]/g,'');if(cleaned!==v)el.value=cleaned};
  el.addEventListener('input',clean);
  el.addEventListener('paste',function(e){
    e.preventDefault();
    const text=(e.clipboardData||window.clipboardData).getData('text')||'';
    const cleaned=text.replace(/[^0-9,\-\s]/g,'');
    const start=el.selectionStart, end=el.selectionEnd;
    el.value=el.value.slice(0,start)+cleaned+el.value.slice(end);
    try{el.setSelectionRange(start+cleaned.length, start+cleaned.length)}catch(_){}
    el.dispatchEvent(new Event('input',{bubbles:true}));
  });
  el.addEventListener('keypress',function(e){
    if(e.ctrlKey||e.metaKey||e.altKey)return;
    const ch=e.key;
    if(ch.length===1 && !/[0-9,\-\s]/.test(ch)) e.preventDefault();
  });
}
function formatSize(b){return b>=1048576?(b/1048576).toFixed(2)+' MB':(b/1024).toFixed(1)+' KB'}
            
             //download successfully
//function showError(m,e){errorDiv.style.display='block';errorDiv.className=e?'error':'success';errorDiv.innerHTML=m+'<span class="close">×</span>';errorDiv.querySelector('.close').onclick=()=>errorDiv.style.display='none';setTimeout(()=>{errorDiv.style.display='none'},4500);if(!e)showSuccessToast(m)} 
            
            
         
            
  function showError(m,e){
  /* Skip success / download messages (keep real errors only) */
  if(!e) return;
  errorDiv.style.display='block';
  errorDiv.className='error';
  errorDiv.innerHTML=m+'<span class="close">×</span>';
  errorDiv.querySelector('.close').onclick=()=>errorDiv.style.display='none';
  setTimeout(()=>{errorDiv.style.display='none'},4500);
}          
            
            
            
            
            
            
            
            
function showSuccessToast(msg){
  const t=document.getElementById('successToast');
  const m=document.getElementById('successToastMsg');
  if(!t)return;
  if(m)m.textContent=(msg||'Done!').replace(/<[^>]+>/g,'').slice(0,80);
  t.classList.add('show');
  setTimeout(()=>t.classList.remove('show'),2800);
}
            
            
            
            
function setProgressText(txt){
  const el=document.getElementById('pdf-tool-progressText');
  if(!el)return;
  if(!document.getElementById('cfProgCSS')){
    const s=document.createElement('style');
    s.id='cfProgCSS';
    s.textContent=
      '.progress-text .prog-label{color:#64748b;font-weight:600}'+
      '.progress-text .prog-file{color:#1e40af;font-weight:800}'+
      '.progress-text .prog-sep{color:#94a3b8;font-weight:600;margin:0 4px}'+
      '.progress-text .prog-page{color:#7c3aed;font-weight:800}'+
      '.progress-text .prog-pct{color:#059669;font-weight:800;margin-left:4px}'+
      '.pdf-tool-container.dark-mode .progress-text .prog-label{color:#94a3b8}'+
      '.pdf-tool-container.dark-mode .progress-text .prog-file{color:#93c5fd}'+
      '.pdf-tool-container.dark-mode .progress-text .prog-page{color:#c4b5fd}'+
      '.pdf-tool-container.dark-mode .progress-text .prog-pct{color:#34d399}';
    document.head.appendChild(s);
  }
  if(!txt){
    el.style.display='none';
    el.textContent='';
    el.innerHTML='';
    return;
  }
  var raw=String(txt);
  try{
    var _isComp=/^Compressing\b|^Done\s+\d+/i.test(raw);
    var _tab=document.querySelector('.pdf-tool-tab.active');
    var _tid=_tab?(_tab.getAttribute('data-tab')||''):'';
    if(_isComp && _tid!=='pdf-tool-compress'){
      el.style.display='none';
      el.textContent='';
      el.innerHTML='';
      try{
        progressBar.style.display='none';
        progressFill.style.width='0%';
        spinner.style.display='none';
      }catch(e){}
      return;
    }
  }catch(e){}
  el.style.display='block';
  var m1=raw.match(/^Compressing\s+(\d+\s*\/\s*\d+)\s*\|\s*Page\s+(\d+\s*\/\s*\d+)\s*(\(\d+%\))$/i);
  if(m1){
    el.innerHTML='<span class="prog-label">Compressing</span> <span class="prog-file">'+m1[1]+'</span><span class="prog-sep">|</span><span class="prog-page">Page '+m1[2]+'</span><span class="prog-pct">'+m1[3]+'</span>';
    return;
  }
  var m2=raw.match(/^Compressing\s+(\d+\s*\/\s*\d+)\s*(\(\d+%\))$/i);
  if(m2){
    el.innerHTML='<span class="prog-label">Compressing</span> <span class="prog-file">'+m2[1]+'</span><span class="prog-pct">'+m2[2]+'</span>';
    return;
  }
  var m3=raw.match(/^Done\s+(\d+\s*\/\s*\d+)\s*(\(\d+%\))$/i);
  if(m3){
    el.innerHTML='<span class="prog-label">Done</span> <span class="prog-file">'+m3[1]+'</span><span class="prog-pct">'+m3[2]+'</span>';
    return;
  }
  el.textContent=raw;
}
function setProcessing(on){
  spinner.style.display=on?'block':'none';
  progressBar.style.display=on?'block':'none';
  if(!on){
    setProgressText('');
    try{progressFill.style.width='0%'}catch(e){}
  }
  const btns=[processRemoveBtn,processLayoutBtn,processCompressBtn,processConverterBtn,processMergeSplitBtn,document.getElementById('pdf-tool-processExtractBtn'),document.getElementById('pdf-tool-processSecurityBtn')].filter(Boolean);
  btns.forEach(b=>b.disabled=on);
}
function updateProgress(p){
  try{
    var _tab=document.querySelector('.pdf-tool-tab.active');
    var _tid=_tab?(_tab.getAttribute('data-tab')||''):'';
    if(_tid!=='pdf-tool-compress'){
      progressBar.style.display='none';
      progressFill.style.width='0%';
      return;
    }
  }catch(e){}
  progressFill.style.width=p+'%';
}
            
            
            
            
            
            
 /*           
   //use this block only for direct download without rename file         
function downloadPDF(bytes,name){const a=document.createElement('a');a.href=URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([bytes],{type:'application/pdf'}));a.download=name;document.body.appendChild(a);a.click();document.body.removeChild(a)}
function downloadBlob(blob,name){const a=document.createElement('a');a.href=URL.createObjectURL(blob);a.download=name;document.body.appendChild(a);a.click();document.body.removeChild(a)}
            
           */
            
            
   function askDownloadName(defaultName){
  const lastDot=(defaultName||'').lastIndexOf('.');
  const base=lastDot>0?defaultName.slice(0,lastDot):(defaultName||'download');
  const ext=lastDot>0?defaultName.slice(lastDot):'';
  let name=prompt('Enter file name (without extension):',base);
  if(name===null) return null;
  name=(name||'').trim()||base;
  name=name.replace(/[\\/:*?"<>|]/g,'_');
  if(ext && !name.toLowerCase().endsWith(ext.toLowerCase())) name+=ext;
  return name;
}
function downloadPDF(bytes,name){
  const finalName=askDownloadName(name||'download.pdf');
  if(!finalName) return;
  const a=document.createElement('a');
  a.href=URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([bytes],{type:'application/pdf'}));
  a.download=finalName;
  document.body.appendChild(a);a.click();document.body.removeChild(a);
}
function downloadBlob(blob,name){
  const finalName=askDownloadName(name||'download.bin');
  if(!finalName) return;
  const a=document.createElement('a');
  a.href=URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  a.download=finalName;
  document.body.appendChild(a);a.click();document.body.removeChild(a);
}
            
            
            
            
            
            
            
            
            
function updateButtonStates(){
  processRemoveBtn.disabled=!selectedFiles.remove||!pagesToRemove.value.trim()||!processedPdfBytes;
  processLayoutBtn.disabled=!selectedFiles.layout||!processedPdfBytes;
  processCompressBtn.disabled=selectedFiles.compress.length===0||!processedPdfBytes;
  processConverterBtn.disabled=conversionType.value==='pdf-to-jpg'?selectedFiles.converter.length===0:!processedImages.length||!processedPdfBytes;
  processMergeSplitBtn.disabled=selectedFiles.mergeSplit.length===0||(mergeSplitType.value==='merge'&&selectedFiles.mergeSplit.length<2)||(mergeSplitType.value==='split'&&(selectedFiles.mergeSplit.length!==1||!splitRanges.value.trim()||!parsePageNumbers(splitRanges.value.trim(),null)));
  /* Reset buttons: enabled when current tab is dirty */
  const setReset=(id,on)=>{const b=document.getElementById(id);if(b)b.disabled=!on};
  setReset('pdf-tool-resetRemoveBtn', !!dirtyFlags.remove);
  setReset('pdf-tool-resetLayoutBtn', !!dirtyFlags.layout);
  setReset('pdf-tool-resetCompressBtn', !!dirtyFlags.compress);
  setReset('pdf-tool-resetConverterBtn', !!dirtyFlags.converter);
  setReset('pdf-tool-resetMergeSplitBtn', !!dirtyFlags.mergeSplit);
  setReset('pdf-tool-resetSecurityBtn', !!dirtyFlags.security);
}
function markDirty(tab){
  if(tab&&dirtyFlags.hasOwnProperty(tab)){dirtyFlags[tab]=true;updateButtonStates()}
}
function clearDirty(tab){
  if(tab&&dirtyFlags.hasOwnProperty(tab)){dirtyFlags[tab]=false}
}

function createFileInput(accept,multiple,onChange){const input=document.createElement('input');input.type='file';input.accept=accept;input.multiple=!!multiple;input.style.display='none';document.body.appendChild(input);input.onchange=async e=>{const files=Array.from(e.target.files||[]);try{document.body.removeChild(input)}catch(_){}if(files.length)await onChange(files)};input.oncancel=()=>{try{document.body.removeChild(input)}catch(_){}};input.click()}

/* Drop area: ONLY Select button opens picker; drag-drop still works */
function setupDropArea(area,areaId,accept,multiple){
  area.addEventListener('dragover',e=>{e.preventDefault();area.classList.add('drag-over')});
  area.addEventListener('dragleave',()=>area.classList.remove('drag-over'));
  area.addEventListener('drop',async e=>{e.preventDefault();area.classList.remove('drag-over');await handleFiles(areaId,Array.from(e.dataTransfer.files),false)});
                                         
                                         
    // replace "false" by "true" in just above line for making drag and drop feature like select files button
   // replace "true" by "false" in just above line for making drag and drop feature like Add more button
                                         // use below line when drag and drop like add more button
                                        // await handleFiles(areaId,Array.from(e.dataTransfer.files),false)});
  
                                          // use below line when drag and drop like select file button
                                         //await handleFiles(areaId,Array.from(e.dataTransfer.files),true)};
  
  
  const btn=area.querySelector('.small-btn');
  if(btn){
    btn.addEventListener('click',e=>{
      e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();
      let acc=accept;
    if(areaId==='converter')acc=conversionType.value==='pdf-to-jpg'?'application/pdf':'image/*';
      /* Select File = replace mode for multi areas */
      const replace=areaId==='converter'||areaId==='mergeSplit'||areaId==='compress';
      createFileInput(acc,multiple,files=>handleFiles(areaId,files,replace));
    });
  }
}


async function detectPdfEncrypted(ab){
  /* Returns true only if PDF requires a password to open */
  let needsPassword=false;
  try{
    const task=window.pdfjsLib.getDocument({data:ab, password:''});
    await task.promise;
  }catch(err){
    const name=err&&err.name;
    const code=err&&err.code;
    const msg=(err&&(err.message||''))||'';
    if(name==='PasswordException'||code===1||code===2||/password|encrypted|NeedPassword|IncorrectPassword/i.test(msg)){
      needsPassword=true;
    }
  }
  if(needsPassword) return true;
  /* Binary /Encrypt without openable empty password still counts as locked for protect-mode */
  try{
    const u8=ab instanceof Uint8Array?ab:new Uint8Array(ab);
    const step=256*1024;
    let hasEncrypt=false;
    for(let off=0; off<u8.length; off+=step){
      const text=new TextDecoder('latin1').decode(u8.subarray(off, Math.min(off+step+32, u8.length)));
      if(/\/Encrypt[\s\/\[\d]/.test(text)){hasEncrypt=true;break}
    }
    if(hasEncrypt){
      /* try open — if opened without password, treat as unlocked for unlock-mode */
      try{
        const t2=window.pdfjsLib.getDocument({data:ab});
        await t2.promise;
        return false; /* opens freely */
      }catch(e2){
        return true;
      }
    }
  }catch(e){}
  return false;
}

let _qpdfModShared=null;
async function getQpdfModuleShared(){
  if(_qpdfModShared) return _qpdfModShared;
  setProgressText('Loading PDF unlock engine...');
  const createModule=(await import('https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/@neslinesli93/qpdf-wasm@0.3.0/+esm')).default;
  _qpdfModShared=await createModule({
    locateFile:()=>'https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/@neslinesli93/qpdf-wasm@0.3.0/dist/qpdf.wasm',
    noInitialRun:true
  });
  try{_qpdfModShared.FS.mkdir('/work')}catch(e){}
  setProgressText('');
  return _qpdfModShared;
}

function promptPdfPassword(fileName, badPass){
  return new Promise((resolve)=>{
    const modal=document.getElementById('pdfPwdModal');
    const input=document.getElementById('pdfPwdModalInput');
    const errEl=document.getElementById('pdfPwdModalErr');
    const desc=document.getElementById('pdfPwdModalDesc');
    const ok=document.getElementById('pdfPwdModalOk');
    const cancel=document.getElementById('pdfPwdModalCancel');
    if(!modal||!input){resolve(null);return}
    if(desc) desc.textContent=fileName
      ?('"'+fileName+'" is password-protected. Enter the password to continue.')
      :'This PDF is password-protected. Enter the password to continue.';
    input.value='';
    if(errEl){
      if(badPass){errEl.textContent='Incorrect password. Try again.';errEl.classList.add('show')}
      else errEl.classList.remove('show');
    }
    modal.classList.add('show');
    setTimeout(()=>{try{input.focus()}catch(_){}},40);
    function cleanup(v){
      modal.classList.remove('show');
      ok.onclick=null;cancel.onclick=null;input.onkeydown=null;
      resolve(v);
    }
    cancel.onclick=()=>cleanup(null);
    ok.onclick=()=>{
      const p=input.value;
      if(!p){if(errEl){errEl.textContent='Please enter the password.';errEl.classList.add('show')}return}
      cleanup(p);
    };
    input.onkeydown=e=>{
      if(e.key==='Enter'){e.preventDefault();ok.click()}
      if(e.key==='Escape'){e.preventDefault();cancel.click()}
    };
  });
}

async function unlockPdfArrayBuffer(ab, password){
  const uint8=ab instanceof Uint8Array?ab:new Uint8Array(ab);
  try{
    const doc=await window.pdfjsLib.getDocument({data:uint8, password:password||''}).promise;
    try{doc.destroy()}catch(_){}
  }catch(e){
    throw new Error('Incorrect password');
  }
  const qpdf=await getQpdfModuleShared();
  qpdf.FS.writeFile('/work/input.pdf', uint8);
  try{qpdf.FS.unlink('/work/output.pdf')}catch(e){}
  qpdf.callMain(['--password='+password,'--decrypt','/work/input.pdf','/work/output.pdf']);
  return qpdf.FS.readFile('/work/output.pdf');
}

/** If PDF is locked, ask password and return unlocked File; cancel → null */
async function ensureUnlockedPdfFile(file){
  if(!file) return null;
  const ab=await file.arrayBuffer();
  const locked=await detectPdfEncrypted(ab);
  if(!locked) return new File([ab], file.name,{type:'application/pdf'});
  let bad=false;
  for(;;){
    const pass=await promptPdfPassword(file.name||'PDF', bad);
    if(pass===null) return null;
    try{
      setProcessing(true);
      
      
      
      
      setProgressText('Unlocking PDF...');
      const out=await unlockPdfArrayBuffer(ab, pass);
      const name=(file.name||'document.pdf').replace(/\.pdf$/i,'')+'.pdf';
      return new File([out], name,{type:'application/pdf'});
    }catch(e){
      bad=true;
    }finally{
      setProcessing(false);
      setProgressText('');
    }
  }
}

async function handleFiles(areaId,files,replace){
  try{
    if(areaId) markDirty(areaId==='mergeSplit'?'mergeSplit':areaId);
    if(areaId==='extract'){
      const pdfs=files.filter(f=>f.type==='application/pdf');
      if(!pdfs.length){showError('Please upload a PDF file.',true);return}
      selectedFiles.extract=pdfs[0];
      const ab=await pdfs[0].arrayBuffer();
      pdfDoc=await window.pdfjsLib.getDocument({data:ab}).promise;
      await renderInputPreview(ab,'extract');
      const bar=document.getElementById('rotateBarRemove');
      if(bar) bar.classList.remove('visible');
      updateButtonStates();
      return;
    }
    if(areaId==='security'){
      const pdfs=files.filter(f=>f.type==='application/pdf'||/\.pdf$/i.test(f.name||''));
      if(!pdfs.length){showError('Please upload PDF files only.',true);return}
      const mode=document.getElementById('pdf-tool-securityType')?.value||'protect';
      const valid=[];
      let skipped=0;
      for(const file of pdfs){
        const ab=await file.arrayBuffer();
        const isEncrypted=await detectPdfEncrypted(ab);
        if(mode==='protect' && isEncrypted){ skipped++; continue; }
        if(mode==='unlock' && !isEncrypted){ skipped++; continue; }
        valid.push(file);
      }
      
      
      
          if(!valid.length){
        showError(mode==='protect'
          ? 'All selected PDFs are already locked.'
          : 'All selected PDFs are already unlocked.', true);
        var _sl=document.getElementById('securityFileList');
        if(_sl&&!_sl.querySelector('.sec-file-row')){
          var _sum=_sl.querySelector('.sec-list-summary');
          if(_sum) _sum.remove();
          _sl.classList.remove('has-files');
          _sl.dataset.cfPrevRows='0';
        }
        return;
      }
      
      
      
      if(replace){
        selectedFiles.security=valid;
      }else{
        selectedFiles.security=(selectedFiles.security||[]).concat(valid);
      }
      if(skipped>0){
        showError(skipped+' file(s) skipped (already '+(mode==='protect'?'locked':'unlocked')+').', true);
      }
      await showFileName('security', selectedFiles.security);
      if(typeof renderSecurityFileList==='function'){
        const prevCount=(replace?0:(selectedFiles.security.length-valid.length));
        const from=Math.max(0, prevCount);
        if(replace || from===0) await renderSecurityFileList();
        else await renderSecurityFileList({appendFrom:from});
      }
      markDirty('security');
      updateButtonStates();
      return;
    }
    if(areaId==='converter'){
     
      
     let filtered;
if(conversionType.value==='pdf-to-jpg'){
  filtered=files.filter(f=>f.type==='application/pdf'||/\.pdf$/i.test(f.name||''));
  if(!filtered.length){showError('Please upload PDF files only.',true);return}
}else{
  // Accept ALL image types (JPEG, PNG, WebP, GIF, BMP, HEIC, AVIF, TIFF etc.)
  filtered=files.filter(f=>{
    if(f.type && f.type.startsWith('image/')) return true;
    // type empty hone par extension se check (mobile pe common)
    return /\.(jpe?g|png|gif|webp|bmp|heic|heif|tiff?|avif|svg)$/i.test(f.name||'');
  });
  if(!filtered.length){showError('Please upload image files only.',true);return}
}
      
      
      let ready=filtered;
      if(conversionType.value==='pdf-to-jpg'){
        const unlocked=[];
        for(const f of filtered){
          const u=await ensureUnlockedPdfFile(f);
          if(!u){showError('Password required — upload cancelled.',true);return}
          unlocked.push(u);
        }
        ready=unlocked;
      }
      if(replace){
        selectedFiles.converter=ready;
        await processConverterFiles(selectedFiles.converter, false);
      }else{
        const had=selectedFiles.converter.length;
        selectedFiles.converter=selectedFiles.converter.concat(ready);
        await processConverterFiles(selectedFiles.converter, had>0);
      }
      showFileName('converter',selectedFiles.converter);
    }else if(areaId==='mergeSplit'){
      const pdfs=files.filter(f=>f.type==='application/pdf'||/\.pdf$/i.test(f.name));
      if(!pdfs.length){showError('Please upload PDF files only.',true);return}
      const unlocked=[];
      for(const f of pdfs){
        const u=await ensureUnlockedPdfFile(f);
        if(!u){showError('Password required — upload cancelled.',true);return}
        unlocked.push(u);
      }
      if(mergeSplitType.value==='split'){
        if(unlocked.length>1){showError('Please upload only one PDF for splitting.',true);return}
        selectedFiles.mergeSplit=unlocked;
      }else{
        if(replace){
          if(unlocked.length<2){showError('Please select at least 2 PDF files to merge.',true);return}
          selectedFiles.mergeSplit=unlocked;
          await processMergeSplitFiles(selectedFiles.mergeSplit, false);
        }else{
          const had=selectedFiles.mergeSplit.length;
          selectedFiles.mergeSplit=selectedFiles.mergeSplit.concat(unlocked);
          if(selectedFiles.mergeSplit.length<2){showError('Please upload at least 2 PDF files to merge.',true)}
          await processMergeSplitFiles(selectedFiles.mergeSplit, had>0);
        }
      }
      if(mergeSplitType.value==='split'){
        await processMergeSplitFiles(selectedFiles.mergeSplit, false);
      }
      showFileName('mergeSplit',selectedFiles.mergeSplit);
    }else if(areaId==='compress'){
      const pdfs=files.filter(f=>f.type==='application/pdf'||/\.pdf$/i.test(f.name));
      if(!pdfs.length){showError('Please upload PDF files only.',true);return}
      const unlocked=[];
      for(const f of pdfs){
        const u=await ensureUnlockedPdfFile(f);
        if(!u){showError('Password required — upload cancelled.',true);return}
        unlocked.push(u);
      }
      if(replace){
        selectedFiles.compress=unlocked;
        await processCompressFiles(selectedFiles.compress, false);
      }else{
        const had=selectedFiles.compress.length;
        selectedFiles.compress=selectedFiles.compress.concat(unlocked);
        await processCompressFiles(selectedFiles.compress, had>0);
      }
      showFileName('compress',selectedFiles.compress);
    }else{
      if(files.length>1){showError('Please upload only one PDF file.',true);return}
      if(!files[0]||(files[0].type!=='application/pdf'&&!/\.pdf$/i.test(files[0].name||''))){showError('Please upload a valid PDF file.',true);return}
      const unlocked=await ensureUnlockedPdfFile(files[0]);
      
      
      
      if(!unlocked){showError('Password required — upload cancelled.',true);return}
      
      
      
      
      /* Remove Pages: reset old page numbers whenever a new PDF is uploaded */
if(areaId==='remove'){
  pagesToRemove.value='';
  processedPdfBytes=null;
}
      
      
      
      
      
      
      
      
      
      
      selectedFiles[areaId]=unlocked;
      await showFileName(areaId,unlocked);
      await processSingleFile(areaId,unlocked);
    }
    updateButtonStates();
  }catch(err){showError('Error handling files: '+err.message,true)}
}

async function showFileName(areaId,files){
  const el=document.getElementById('file-name-'+areaId);if(!el)return;
  const arr=Array.isArray(files)?files:(files?[files]:[]);
  if(!arr.length){el.innerHTML='';return}
  const total=arr.reduce((s,f)=>s+(f.size||0),0);
  const isPdf=f=>f&&(f.type==='application/pdf'||/\.pdf$/i.test(f.name||''));
  if(arr.length===1){
    const f=arr[0];
    let pagesHtml='';
    if(isPdf(f)&&window.pdfjsLib){
      try{
        const ab=await f.arrayBuffer();
        const doc=await window.pdfjsLib.getDocument({data:ab}).promise;
        const n=doc.numPages;
        pagesHtml=`<span class="fb-meta">· ${n} page${n>1?'s':''}</span>`;
        try{doc.destroy()}catch(e){}
      }catch(e){}
    }
    const name=(f.name||'file').replace(/</g,'&lt;');
    el.innerHTML=`<div class="file-badge" title="${name}"><i class="fa-solid fa-file-pdf"></i><span class="fb-name">${name}</span><span class="fb-meta">· ${formatSize(f.size||0)}</span>${pagesHtml}</div>`;
  }else{
    el.innerHTML=`<div class="file-badge multi"><i class="fa-solid fa-layer-group"></i><span>${arr.length} files</span><span class="fb-meta">· Total ${formatSize(total)}</span></div>`;
  }
}

async function processSingleFile(areaId,file){
  markDirty(areaId);
  const ab=await file.arrayBuffer();
  pdfDoc=await window.pdfjsLib.getDocument({data:ab}).promise;
  processedPdfBytes=null;
  previewDiv.classList.remove('hidden-preview');
  await showFileName(areaId, file);
  if(areaId==='layout')await generateLayoutPreview(ab);
  else if(areaId==='remove'){await renderInputPreview(ab,'remove')}
  updateButtonStates();
}

            
            
            
            
            
            
            
            
            
async function processCompressFiles(files, appendOnly){
  if(!files.length){previewDiv.innerHTML='';previewDiv.classList.add('hidden-preview');processedPdfBytes=null;return}
  try{
    setProcessing(true);
    
        window._cfCompressRunId = (window._cfCompressRunId||0) + 1;
    const myCompressRun = window._cfCompressRunId;
    
    
    
      spinner.style.display='none';
    updateProgress(0);
    setProgressText('Compressing 0 / ' + files.length + ' (0%)');  
    
    
    
    
    
    previewDiv.classList.remove('hidden-preview');
    const existing=appendOnly?previewDiv.querySelectorAll('.preview-item').length:0;
    if(!appendOnly) previewDiv.innerHTML='';
    const results=[];
    /* if appending, keep previous compressed bytes if stored */
    if(appendOnly && window._compressResults) results.push(...window._compressResults);
    const start=appendOnly?existing:0;
    for(let i=start;i<files.length;i++){
      const file=files[i];
      
      
      
            if(myCompressRun !== window._cfCompressRunId) break;
      
      
      
         var overallPct = Math.round((i / files.length) * 100);
      updateProgress(overallPct);
      setProgressText('Compressing ' + (i + 1) + ' / ' + files.length + ' (' + overallPct + '%)');
      
      
      
            window._cfFileIndex = i;
      window._cfFileTotal = files.length;
      
      
      
      
      
      const ab=await file.arrayBuffer();
      const quality=getCompressQuality();
      let bytes;
      let alreadyOpt=false;
      /* Target mode: if target >= original size, skip compression */
      if(typeof quality==='number'&&quality>1){
        const targetBytes=quality*1024;
        if(targetBytes>=file.size){
          bytes=new Uint8Array(ab);
          alreadyOpt=true;
        }
      }
      if(!bytes) bytes=await compressPDF(ab,quality);
      if(bytes.length>=file.size){
        bytes=new Uint8Array(ab);
        alreadyOpt=true;
      }
      
      
      
      results.push({name:file.name,bytes,originalSize:file.size});
      
      
           var donePct = Math.round(((i + 1) / files.length) * 100);
      updateProgress(donePct);
      setProgressText('Compressing ' + (i + 1) + ' / ' + files.length + ' (' + donePct + '%)');
      
            
      
      
      const doc=await window.pdfjsLib.getDocument({data:bytes}).promise;
      const page=await doc.getPage(1);
      const vp=page.getViewport({scale:.9});
      const canvas=document.createElement('canvas');
      const ctx=canvas.getContext('2d');
      canvas.width=vp.width;canvas.height=vp.height;
      await page.render({canvasContext:ctx,viewport:vp,intent:'display'}).promise;
      const item=document.createElement('div');
      item.className='preview-item';
      item.dataset.index=i;
      item.appendChild(canvas);
      const removeBtn=document.createElement('button');
      removeBtn.className='remove-btn';
      removeBtn.innerHTML='<i class="fa-solid fa-xmark"></i>';
      removeBtn.onclick=e=>{
        e.stopPropagation();
        const idx=parseInt(item.dataset.index,10);
        selectedFiles.compress.splice(idx,1);
        if(Array.isArray(processedPdfBytes))processedPdfBytes.splice(idx,1);
        if(Array.isArray(window._compressResults))window._compressResults.splice(idx,1);
        item.remove();
        Array.from(previewDiv.querySelectorAll('.preview-item')).forEach((el,ni)=>el.dataset.index=ni);
        if(!selectedFiles.compress.length){previewDiv.classList.add('hidden-preview');processedPdfBytes=null}
        showFileName('compress',selectedFiles.compress);
        updateButtonStates();
      };
      item.appendChild(removeBtn);
      const meta=document.createElement('div');
      meta.className='file-meta';
      const newSize=bytes.length;
      const pageCount=doc.numPages;
      if(alreadyOpt){
        meta.innerHTML=`<strong>${file.name}</strong><br>${pageCount} page${pageCount>1?'s':''}<br><span style="color:#64748b">Size: ${formatSize(file.size)}</span><br><span class="comp-size" style="color:#059669">✓ Already optimized</span> <span style="color:#64748b">(no compression needed)</span>`;
      }else{
        const pct=((1-newSize/file.size)*100).toFixed(1);
        meta.innerHTML=`<strong>${file.name}</strong><br>${pageCount} page${pageCount>1?'s':''}<br><span style="color:#64748b">Before: ${formatSize(file.size)}</span> → <span class="comp-size">After: ${formatSize(newSize)}</span> <span style="color:#059669;font-weight:700">(-${pct}%)</span>`;
      }
      item.appendChild(meta);
      previewDiv.appendChild(item);
    }
    processedPdfBytes=results;
    window._compressResults=results;
    
    
    
       updateProgress(100);
    setProgressText('Done ' + files.length + ' / ' + files.length + ' (100%)');
    
    
    
  }catch(err){showError('Compress error: '+err.message,true)}
  finally{setProcessing(false);updateButtonStates()}
}

function getCompressQuality(){
  const val=qualityPreset.value;
  return val==='target'?parseFloat(targetSizeInput.value)||500:parseFloat(val);
}

            
            
            
            
            
            
            
            
function getConverterQuality(){
  const el=document.getElementById('pdfToJpgQuality');
  return el ? parseFloat(el.value) : 0.75;
}

            
            
            
            
            
            
            
/* Real-time estimated output size while sliding */
function updateEstimatedSizes(){
  /* PDF→JPG: never show est under preview */
  if(conversionType.value==='pdf-to-jpg'){
    previewDiv.querySelectorAll('.est-size').forEach(el=>el.remove());
    return;
  }
  /* JPG→PDF: use dedicated display helper */
  if(conversionType.value==='jpg-to-pdf'){
    updateJpgPdfSizeDisplay();
  }
}

function updateJpgPdfSizeDisplay(){
  if(conversionType.value!=='jpg-to-pdf')return;
  const mergeAll=document.getElementById('mergeImagesCheck')?.checked!==false;
  const q=getConverterQuality();
  /* clear previous per-card est when merge on */
  previewDiv.querySelectorAll('.preview-item .est-size').forEach(el=>{
    if(mergeAll) el.remove();
  });
  if(mergeAll&&processedPdfBytes){
    if(selectedFiles.converter.length) showFileName('converter', selectedFiles.converter);
  }else if(!mergeAll){
    selectedFiles.converter.forEach((file,i)=>{
      const item=previewDiv.querySelectorAll('.preview-item')[i];
      if(!item)return;
      const meta=item.querySelector('.file-meta');
      if(!meta)return;
      let estBytes=file.size*(0.18+q*0.82)+800;
      if(processedImages[i]){
        const b64=processedImages[i];
        estBytes=Math.round((b64.length-23)*0.75*0.85);
      }
      const estStr=estBytes>=1048576?(estBytes/1048576).toFixed(2)+' MB':(estBytes/1024).toFixed(1)+' KB';
      let sizeLine=meta.querySelector('.est-size');
      if(!sizeLine){
        sizeLine=document.createElement('div');
        sizeLine.className='est-size';
        meta.appendChild(sizeLine);
      }
      sizeLine.innerHTML='↓ Output PDF: <b>'+estStr+'</b>';
    });
    if(selectedFiles.converter.length) showFileName('converter', selectedFiles.converter);
  }
}


function rotateDataUrl(dataUrl, degrees){
  return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{
    const img=new Image();
    img.onload=()=>{
      const rad=(degrees%360)*Math.PI/180;
      const w=img.naturalWidth, h=img.naturalHeight;
      const swap=((degrees%180)+180)%180===90;
      const c=document.createElement('canvas');
      c.width=swap?h:w; c.height=swap?w:h;
      const ctx=c.getContext('2d');
      ctx.translate(c.width/2,c.height/2);
      ctx.rotate(rad);
      ctx.drawImage(img,-w/2,-h/2);
      resolve(c.toDataURL('image/jpeg',0.92));
    };
    img.onerror=reject;
    img.src=dataUrl;
  });
}

/* ===== Crop helpers (JPG → PDF) – custom crop-rect + professional Auto Crop ===== */
let cropViewZoom=1;

function lockBodyScroll(lock){
  if(lock){
    const scrollY=window.scrollY||window.pageYOffset||0;
    document.body.dataset.cropScrollY=String(scrollY);
    document.documentElement.style.overflow='hidden';
    document.body.style.overflow='hidden';
    
    
    
    
    
    
   // document.body.style.touchAction='none';
    
    
    
    
    
    document.body.style.position='fixed';
    document.body.style.top=(-scrollY)+'px';
    document.body.style.left='0';
    document.body.style.right='0';
    document.body.style.width='100%';
    document.body.classList.add('crop-modal-open');
  }else{
    const scrollY=parseInt(document.body.dataset.cropScrollY||'0',10)||0;
    document.documentElement.style.overflow='';
    document.body.style.overflow='';
    document.body.style.touchAction='';
    document.body.style.position='';
    document.body.style.top='';
    document.body.style.left='';
    document.body.style.right='';
    document.body.style.width='';
    document.body.classList.remove('crop-modal-open');
    delete document.body.dataset.cropScrollY;
    window.scrollTo(0, scrollY);
  }
}

/* ---------- Fallback content bounding-box crop ---------- */
function autoCropDataUrl(dataUrl, padding=8, threshold=245){
  return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{
    const img=new Image();
    img.onload=()=>{
      const w=img.naturalWidth, h=img.naturalHeight;
      const c=document.createElement('canvas');
      c.width=w; c.height=h;
      const ctx=c.getContext('2d');
      ctx.drawImage(img,0,0);
      const id=ctx.getImageData(0,0,w,h);
      const d=id.data;
      let minX=w, minY=h, maxX=0, maxY=0;
      let found=false;
      for(let y=0;y<h;y++){
        for(let x=0;x<w;x++){
          const i=(y*w+x)*4;
          const r=d[i], g=d[i+1], b=d[i+2], a=d[i+3];
          if(a>20 && (r<threshold || g<threshold || b<threshold)){
            if(x<minX)minX=x;
            if(x>maxX)maxX=x;
            if(y<minY)minY=y;
            if(y>maxY)maxY=y;
            found=true;
          }
        }
      }
      if(!found){ resolve(dataUrl); return; }
      minX=Math.max(0, minX-padding);
      minY=Math.max(0, minY-padding);
      maxX=Math.min(w-1, maxX+padding);
      maxY=Math.min(h-1, maxY+padding);
      const cw=maxX-minX+1, ch=maxY-minY+1;
      if(cw<10||ch<10){ resolve(dataUrl); return; }
      const out=document.createElement('canvas');
      out.width=cw; out.height=ch;
      out.getContext('2d').drawImage(c, minX, minY, cw, ch, 0, 0, cw, ch);
      resolve(out.toDataURL('image/jpeg',0.92));
      c.width=0;c.height=0;
    };
    img.onerror=reject;
    img.src=dataUrl;
  });
}

/* ---------- Professional Document Detection + Perspective Crop ---------- */
function detectAndCropDocument(dataUrl){
  return new Promise(async (resolve)=>{
    try{
      const img=await loadImagePromise(dataUrl);
      const origW=img.naturalWidth, origH=img.naturalHeight;
      /* 1. Downscale for analysis */
      const MAX_ANALYSIS=1000;
      const scale=Math.min(1, MAX_ANALYSIS/Math.max(origW,origH));
      const aW=Math.max(2, Math.round(origW*scale));
      const aH=Math.max(2, Math.round(origH*scale));
      const aCanvas=document.createElement('canvas');
      aCanvas.width=aW; aCanvas.height=aH;
      const aCtx=aCanvas.getContext('2d',{willReadFrequently:true});
      aCtx.drawImage(img,0,0,aW,aH);
      const imgData=aCtx.getImageData(0,0,aW,aH);
      const data=imgData.data;

      /* 2. Grayscale + simple box blur (noise reduction) */
      const gray=new Float32Array(aW*aH);
      for(let i=0,p=0;i<data.length;i+=4,p++){
        gray[p]=0.299*data[i]+0.587*data[i+1]+0.114*data[i+2];
      }
      const blurred=boxBlurGray(gray,aW,aH,1);

      /* 3. Sobel edge detection */
      const edges=sobelEdges(blurred,aW,aH);

      /* 4. Adaptive threshold */
      let sum=0, count=0;
      for(let i=0;i<edges.length;i++){ if(edges[i]>10){sum+=edges[i];count++;} }
      const mean=count?sum/count:30;
      const thresh=Math.max(25, Math.min(90, mean*0.75));
      const binary=new Uint8Array(aW*aH);
      for(let i=0;i<edges.length;i++) binary[i]=edges[i]>=thresh?1:0;

      /* 5. Find strong edge points (sample) */
      const pts=[];
      const step=Math.max(1, Math.floor(Math.min(aW,aH)/180));
      for(let y=2;y<aH-2;y+=step){
        for(let x=2;x<aW-2;x+=step){
          if(binary[y*aW+x]) pts.push({x,y});
        }
      }

      /* 6. Try to find best quadrilateral via convex hull + scoring */
      let bestQuad=null, bestScore=-1;
      if(pts.length>=20){
        const hull=convexHull(pts);
        if(hull.length>=4){
          /* Approximate hull to 4 corners by farthest-point sampling */
          const corners=approxQuad(hull);
          if(corners && corners.length===4){
            const ordered=orderCorners(corners);
            const score=scoreQuad(ordered,aW,aH,binary);
            if(score>bestScore){ bestScore=score; bestQuad=ordered; }
          }
        }
      }

      /* Also try axis-aligned content box as candidate */
      const contentBox=contentBoundingBox(blurred,aW,aH);
      if(contentBox){
        const q=[
          {x:contentBox.minX,y:contentBox.minY},
          {x:contentBox.maxX,y:contentBox.minY},
          {x:contentBox.maxX,y:contentBox.maxY},
          {x:contentBox.minX,y:contentBox.maxY}
        ];
        const sc=scoreQuad(q,aW,aH,binary)*0.85; /* slightly prefer true quads */
        if(sc>bestScore){ bestScore=sc; bestQuad=q; }
      }

      const confidence=Math.min(100, Math.max(0, bestScore));

      if(bestQuad && confidence>=42){
        /* Map corners back to original resolution */
        const inv=1/scale;
        const srcCorners=bestQuad.map(p=>({x:p.x*inv, y:p.y*inv}));
        /* Small safety margin (1.5%) */
        const cx=(srcCorners[0].x+srcCorners[1].x+srcCorners[2].x+srcCorners[3].x)/4;
        const cy=(srcCorners[0].y+srcCorners[1].y+srcCorners[2].y+srcCorners[3].y)/4;
        const margin=0.015;
        const expanded=srcCorners.map(p=>({
          x: cx+(p.x-cx)*(1+margin),
          y: cy+(p.y-cy)*(1+margin)
        }));
        /* Clamp */
        expanded.forEach(p=>{
          p.x=Math.max(0,Math.min(origW-1,p.x));
          p.y=Math.max(0,Math.min(origH-1,p.y));
        });
        const result=perspectiveCrop(img, expanded);
        if(result){ resolve({dataUrl:result, confidence, method:'quad'}); return; }
      }

      /* Fallback: content bounding box on original */
      const fallback=await autoCropDataUrl(dataUrl, Math.round(Math.min(origW,origH)*0.012), 240);
      resolve({dataUrl:fallback, confidence: Math.min(40,confidence), method:'fallback'});
    }catch(e){
      console.warn('Document detect failed',e);
      try{
        const fb=await autoCropDataUrl(dataUrl,12,242);
        resolve({dataUrl:fb, confidence:20, method:'fallback'});
      }catch(_){ resolve({dataUrl, confidence:0, method:'none'}); }
    }
  });
}

function loadImagePromise(src){
  return new Promise((res,rej)=>{ const im=new Image(); im.onload=()=>res(im); im.onerror=rej; im.src=src; });
}

function boxBlurGray(src,w,h,r){
  const out=new Float32Array(src.length);
  const tmp=new Float32Array(src.length);
  const diam=r*2+1;
  /* horizontal */
  for(let y=0;y<h;y++){
    let sum=0;
    for(let x=-r;x<=r;x++) sum+=src[y*w+Math.max(0,Math.min(w-1,x))];
    for(let x=0;x<w;x++){
      tmp[y*w+x]=sum/diam;
      const x1=x-r, x2=x+r+1;
      sum-=src[y*w+Math.max(0,Math.min(w-1,x1))];
      sum+=src[y*w+Math.max(0,Math.min(w-1,x2))];
    }
  }
  /* vertical */
  for(let x=0;x<w;x++){
    let sum=0;
    for(let y=-r;y<=r;y++) sum+=tmp[Math.max(0,Math.min(h-1,y))*w+x];
    for(let y=0;y<h;y++){
      out[y*w+x]=sum/diam;
      const y1=y-r, y2=y+r+1;
      sum-=tmp[Math.max(0,Math.min(h-1,y1))*w+x];
      sum+=tmp[Math.max(0,Math.min(h-1,y2))*w+x];
    }
  }
  return out;
}

function sobelEdges(gray,w,h){
  const out=new Float32Array(w*h);
  for(let y=1;y<h-1;y++){
    for(let x=1;x<w-1;x++){
      const i=y*w+x;
      const gx=
        -gray[i-w-1]-2*gray[i-1]-gray[i+w-1]+
         gray[i-w+1]+2*gray[i+1]+gray[i+w+1];
      const gy=
        -gray[i-w-1]-2*gray[i-w]-gray[i-w+1]+
         gray[i+w-1]+2*gray[i+w]+gray[i+w+1];
      out[i]=Math.sqrt(gx*gx+gy*gy);
    }
  }
  return out;
}

function contentBoundingBox(gray,w,h){
  /* Otsu-ish: find content vs near-uniform background */
  let minV=255,maxV=0;
  for(let i=0;i<gray.length;i++){ if(gray[i]<minV)minV=gray[i]; if(gray[i]>maxV)maxV=gray[i]; }
  const range=maxV-minV||1;
  /* sample border to estimate background */
  let bSum=0,bCnt=0;
  const border=Math.max(2,Math.floor(Math.min(w,h)*0.03));
  for(let y=0;y<h;y++){
    for(let x=0;x<w;x++){
      if(y<border||y>=h-border||x<border||x>=w-border){
        bSum+=gray[y*w+x]; bCnt++;
      }
    }
  }
  const bg=bCnt?bSum/bCnt:(minV+maxV)/2;
  const tol=Math.max(18, range*0.12);
  let minX=w,minY=h,maxX=0,maxY=0,found=false;
  for(let y=0;y<h;y++){
    for(let x=0;x<w;x++){
      if(Math.abs(gray[y*w+x]-bg)>tol){
        if(x<minX)minX=x; if(x>maxX)maxX=x;
        if(y<minY)minY=y; if(y>maxY)maxY=y;
        found=true;
      }
    }
  }
  if(!found) return null;
  const pad=Math.round(Math.min(w,h)*0.012);
  return {
    minX:Math.max(0,minX-pad),
    minY:Math.max(0,minY-pad),
    maxX:Math.min(w-1,maxX+pad),
    maxY:Math.min(h-1,maxY+pad)
  };
}

function convexHull(points){
  if(points.length<3) return points.slice();
  const pts=points.slice().sort((a,b)=>a.x===b.x?a.y-b.y:a.x-b.x);
  const cross=(o,a,b)=>(a.x-o.x)*(b.y-o.y)-(a.y-o.y)*(b.x-o.x);
  const lower=[];
  for(const p of pts){
    while(lower.length>=2 && cross(lower[lower.length-2],lower[lower.length-1],p)<=0) lower.pop();
    lower.push(p);
  }
  const upper=[];
  for(let i=pts.length-1;i>=0;i--){
    const p=pts[i];
    while(upper.length>=2 && cross(upper[upper.length-2],upper[upper.length-1],p)<=0) upper.pop();
    upper.push(p);
  }
  upper.pop(); lower.pop();
  return lower.concat(upper);
}

function approxQuad(hull){
  if(hull.length===4) return hull.slice();
  if(hull.length<4) return null;
  /* Find 4 points that maximize area (simple iterative farthest) */
  let best=null, bestArea=-1;
  const n=hull.length;
  /* Sample combinations of 4 hull points (limited for speed) */
  const step=Math.max(1, Math.floor(n/12));
  for(let i=0;i<n;i+=step){
    for(let j=i+1;j<n;j+=step){
      for(let k=j+1;k<n;k+=step){
        for(let l=k+1;l<n;l+=step){
          const q=[hull[i],hull[j],hull[k],hull[l]];
          const ordered=orderCorners(q);
          const area=quadArea(ordered);
          if(area>bestArea){ bestArea=area; best=ordered; }
        }
      }
    }
  }
  return best;
}

function orderCorners(pts){
  /* Order: TL, TR, BR, BL */
  const cx=pts.reduce((s,p)=>s+p.x,0)/4;
  const cy=pts.reduce((s,p)=>s+p.y,0)/4;
  const sorted=pts.slice().sort((a,b)=>{
    const aa=Math.atan2(a.y-cy,a.x-cx);
    const bb=Math.atan2(b.y-cy,b.x-cx);
    return aa-bb;
  });
  /* Find top-left (min x+y among upper half) */
  let start=0, minSum=Infinity;
  for(let i=0;i<4;i++){
    const s=sorted[i].x+sorted[i].y;
    if(s<minSum){ minSum=s; start=i; }
  }
  const out=[];
  for(let i=0;i<4;i++) out.push(sorted[(start+i)%4]);
  return out;
}

function quadArea(q){
  /* Shoelace */
  let a=0;
  for(let i=0;i<4;i++){
    const j=(i+1)%4;
    a+=q[i].x*q[j].y - q[j].x*q[i].y;
  }
  return Math.abs(a)/2;
}

function dist(a,b){ const dx=a.x-b.x, dy=a.y-b.y; return Math.sqrt(dx*dx+dy*dy); }

function scoreQuad(q,w,h,binary){
  const area=quadArea(q);
  const imgArea=w*h;
  const areaRatio=area/imgArea;
  if(areaRatio<0.08 || areaRatio>0.98) return 0;
  /* side lengths */
  const s=[dist(q[0],q[1]),dist(q[1],q[2]),dist(q[2],q[3]),dist(q[3],q[0])];
  const peri=s[0]+s[1]+s[2]+s[3];
  if(peri<40) return 0;
  /* rectangularity: opposite sides similar */
  const opp1=1-Math.abs(s[0]-s[2])/Math.max(s[0],s[2],1);
  const opp2=1-Math.abs(s[1]-s[3])/Math.max(s[1],s[3],1);
  /* aspect */
  const aspect=Math.max(s[0],s[2])/Math.max(1,Math.min(s[1],s[3]));
  const aspectScore=aspect>0.4&&aspect<3.5?1:0.4;
  /* edge strength along sides (sample) */
  let edgeHits=0, edgeSamples=0;
  for(let si=0;si<4;si++){
    const a=q[si], b=q[(si+1)%4];
    const steps=12;
    for(let t=0;t<=steps;t++){
      const x=Math.round(a.x+(b.x-a.x)*t/steps);
      const y=Math.round(a.y+(b.y-a.y)*t/steps);
      if(x>=0&&x<w&&y>=0&&y<h){
        edgeSamples++;
        if(binary[y*w+x]) edgeHits++;
      }
    }
  }
  const edgeScore=edgeSamples?edgeHits/edgeSamples:0;
  /* convex already assumed */
  let score=0;
  score+=areaRatio*35;
  score+=((opp1+opp2)/2)*20;
  score+=aspectScore*15;
  score+=edgeScore*25;
  score+=Math.min(5, peri/80);
  return score;
}

function perspectiveCrop(img, corners){
  /* corners: [tl,tr,br,bl] in original image coords */
  const tl=corners[0], tr=corners[1], br=corners[2], bl=corners[3];
  const topW=dist(tl,tr), botW=dist(bl,br);
  const leftH=dist(tl,bl), rightH=dist(tr,br);
  const dstW=Math.max(2, Math.round(Math.max(topW,botW)));
  const dstH=Math.max(2, Math.round(Math.max(leftH,rightH)));
  /* Limit output size for memory safety */
  const MAX=3500;
  let outW=dstW, outH=dstH;
  if(outW>MAX||outH>MAX){
    const s=Math.min(MAX/outW, MAX/outH);
    outW=Math.round(outW*s);
    outH=Math.round(outH*s);
  }
  const src=document.createElement('canvas');
  src.width=img.naturalWidth; src.height=img.naturalHeight;
  const sctx=src.getContext('2d');
  sctx.drawImage(img,0,0);
  const srcData=sctx.getImageData(0,0,src.width,src.height);
  const out=document.createElement('canvas');
  out.width=outW; out.height=outH;
  const octx=out.getContext('2d');
  const outData=octx.createImageData(outW,outH);
  /* Inverse bilinear mapping from dest unit square to source quad */
  for(let y=0;y<outH;y++){
    const v=y/(outH-1||1);
    for(let x=0;x<outW;x++){
      const u=x/(outW-1||1);
      /* bilinear interp of corners */
      const sx=(1-u)*(1-v)*tl.x + u*(1-v)*tr.x + u*v*br.x + (1-u)*v*bl.x;
      const sy=(1-u)*(1-v)*tl.y + u*(1-v)*tr.y + u*v*br.y + (1-u)*v*bl.y;
      const ix=Math.max(0, Math.min(src.width-1, sx));
      const iy=Math.max(0, Math.min(src.height-1, sy));
      /* bilinear sample */
      const x0=Math.floor(ix), y0=Math.floor(iy);
      const x1=Math.min(src.width-1,x0+1), y1=Math.min(src.height-1,y0+1);
      const fx=ix-x0, fy=iy-y0;
      const idx00=(y0*src.width+x0)*4;
      const idx10=(y0*src.width+x1)*4;
      const idx01=(y1*src.width+x0)*4;
      const idx11=(y1*src.width+x1)*4;
      const o=(y*outW+x)*4;
      for(let c=0;c<3;c++){
        const v00=srcData.data[idx00+c], v10=srcData.data[idx10+c];
        const v01=srcData.data[idx01+c], v11=srcData.data[idx11+c];
        outData.data[o+c]=(1-fx)*(1-fy)*v00 + fx*(1-fy)*v10 + (1-fx)*fy*v01 + fx*fy*v11;
      }
      outData.data[o+3]=255;
    }
  }
  octx.putImageData(outData,0,0);
  const q=0.92;
  return out.toDataURL('image/jpeg',q);
}

function getRotatedCanvasFromDataUrl(dataUrl, deg){
  return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{
    const img=new Image();
    img.onload=()=>{
      const canvas=document.createElement('canvas');
      const ctx=canvas.getContext('2d');
      const rad=((deg%360)+360)%360*Math.PI/180;
      const cos=Math.abs(Math.cos(rad));
      const sin=Math.abs(Math.sin(rad));
      canvas.width=Math.max(1, Math.round(img.width*cos+img.height*sin));
      canvas.height=Math.max(1, Math.round(img.width*sin+img.height*cos));
      ctx.fillStyle='#ffffff';
      ctx.fillRect(0,0,canvas.width,canvas.height);
      ctx.translate(canvas.width/2, canvas.height/2);
      ctx.rotate(rad);
      ctx.drawImage(img, -img.width/2, -img.height/2);
      resolve(canvas);
    };
    img.onerror=reject;
    img.src=dataUrl;
  });
}

function updateCropRectUI(){
  const rect=document.getElementById('cropRect');
  if(!rect)return;
  rect.style.left=cropBox.x+'px';
  rect.style.top=cropBox.y+'px';
  rect.style.width=cropBox.w+'px';
  rect.style.height=cropBox.h+'px';
  updateCropSizeBadge();
}

function updateCropSizeBadge(){
  const badge=document.getElementById('cropSizeBadge');
  if(!badge||!cropDisplayScale)return;
  const nw=Math.max(1, Math.round(cropBox.w/cropDisplayScale));
  const nh=Math.max(1, Math.round(cropBox.h/cropDisplayScale));
  badge.textContent=nw+' x '+nh;
  const ed=document.getElementById('cropSizeEditor');
  if(ed&&!ed.classList.contains('show')){
    const wi=document.getElementById('cropCustomW');
    const hi=document.getElementById('cropCustomH');
    if(wi) wi.value=String(nw);
    if(hi) hi.value=String(nh);
  }
}
function openCropSizeEditor(e){
  if(e){ e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); }
  const ed=document.getElementById('cropSizeEditor');
  const badge=document.getElementById('cropSizeBadge');
  if(!ed||!cropDisplayScale)return;
  const nw=Math.max(1, Math.round(cropBox.w/cropDisplayScale));
  const nh=Math.max(1, Math.round(cropBox.h/cropDisplayScale));
  const wi=document.getElementById('cropCustomW');
  const hi=document.getElementById('cropCustomH');
  if(wi) wi.value=String(nw);
  if(hi) hi.value=String(nh);
  ed.classList.add('show');
  if(badge) badge.style.visibility='hidden';
  if(wi) setTimeout(()=>wi.focus(),30);
}
function closeCropSizeEditor(){
  const ed=document.getElementById('cropSizeEditor');
  const badge=document.getElementById('cropSizeBadge');
  if(ed) ed.classList.remove('show');
  if(badge) badge.style.visibility='';
}
function applyCustomCropSize(){
  const wi=document.getElementById('cropCustomW');
  const hi=document.getElementById('cropCustomH');
  if(!wi||!hi||!cropDisplayScale)return;
  let nw=Math.max(1, parseInt(wi.value,10)||1);
  let nh=Math.max(1, parseInt(hi.value,10)||1);
  const ratio=getActiveCropRatio();
  if(ratio){
    /* Keep width, adjust height to locked ratio */
    nh=Math.max(1, Math.round(nw/ratio));
    hi.value=String(nh);
  }
  const area=document.getElementById('cropArea');
  const areaW=area?area.clientWidth:480;
  const areaH=area?area.clientHeight:320;
  let w=nw*cropDisplayScale;
  let h=nh*cropDisplayScale;
  w=Math.min(Math.max(30,w), areaW);
  h=Math.min(Math.max(30,h), areaH);
  const cx=cropBox.x+cropBox.w/2;
  const cy=cropBox.y+cropBox.h/2;
  cropBox.w=w;
  cropBox.h=h;
  cropBox.x=Math.max(0, Math.min(cx-w/2, areaW-w));
  cropBox.y=Math.max(0, Math.min(cy-h/2, areaH-h));
  updateCropRectUI();
  closeCropSizeEditor();
}

function getActiveCropRatio(){
  if(cropPresetRatio==='free'){
 
    const lock=document.getElementById('lockRatio');
    if(lock&&lock.checked&&cropImgNatural.w&&cropImgNatural.h){
      return cropImgNatural.w/cropImgNatural.h;
    }
    return null;
  }
  if(cropPresetRatio==='original'){
    if(cropImgNatural.w&&cropImgNatural.h) return cropImgNatural.w/cropImgNatural.h;
    return null;
  }
  const n=parseFloat(cropPresetRatio);
  return isFinite(n)&&n>0?n:null;
}

function applyCropPreset(ratioKey, forceRelayout){
  cropPresetRatio=ratioKey||'free';
  const sel=document.getElementById('cropPresetSelect');
  if(sel&&sel.value!==cropPresetRatio) sel.value=cropPresetRatio;
 
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
 /*const lock=document.getElementById('lockRatio');
 // this code block for preset time disable checkbox with on
if(lock){
  if(cropPresetRatio==='free'){
    lock.checked=false;
    lock.disabled=false;
  }else{
    lock.checked=true;
    lock.disabled=true;
  }
}*/
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  const lock=document.getElementById('lockRatio');
if(lock){
  lock.disabled=false;

  if(cropPresetRatio==='free'){
    lock.checked=false;
  }else{
    lock.checked=true;
  }
}
  
  
  
  
  
  
  const ratio=getActiveCropRatio();
  if(ratio&&cropBox.w>0){
    const area=document.getElementById('cropArea');
    const areaW=area?area.clientWidth:480;
    const areaH=area?area.clientHeight:320;
    const cx=cropBox.x+cropBox.w/2;
    const cy=cropBox.y+cropBox.h/2;
    let nw=cropBox.w, nh=nw/ratio;
    if(nh>areaH*0.95){ nh=areaH*0.95; nw=nh*ratio; }
    if(nw>areaW*0.95){ nw=areaW*0.95; nh=nw/ratio; }
    nw=Math.max(40,nw); nh=Math.max(40,nh);
    cropBox.w=nw; cropBox.h=nh;
    cropBox.x=Math.max(0, Math.min(areaW-nw, cx-nw/2));
    cropBox.y=Math.max(0, Math.min(areaH-nh, cy-nh/2));
    updateCropRectUI();
  }else if(forceRelayout){
    layoutCropImage(false);
  }
}

function nudgeCropBox(dx,dy){
  const area=document.getElementById('cropArea');
  if(!area)return;
  const areaW=area.clientWidth, areaH=area.clientHeight;
  cropBox.x=Math.max(0, Math.min(areaW-cropBox.w, cropBox.x+dx));
  cropBox.y=Math.max(0, Math.min(areaH-cropBox.h, cropBox.y+dy));
  updateCropRectUI();
}

function layoutCropImage(keepBox){
  const area=document.getElementById('cropArea');
  const cropImg=document.getElementById('cropImage');
  if(!area||!cropImg)return;
  const areaW=area.offsetWidth||area.clientWidth||480;
  const areaH=area.offsetHeight||area.clientHeight||320;
  const baseScale=Math.min(areaW/cropImgNatural.w, areaH/cropImgNatural.h, 1);
  cropDisplayScale=baseScale*cropViewZoom;
  const dispW=cropImgNatural.w*cropDisplayScale;
  const dispH=cropImgNatural.h*cropDisplayScale;
  cropImg.style.width=dispW+'px';
  cropImg.style.height=dispH+'px';
  cropImg.style.left=((areaW-dispW)/2)+'px';
  cropImg.style.top=((areaH-dispH)/2)+'px';
  if(!keepBox){
    const ratio=getActiveCropRatio()||(cropImgNatural.w/cropImgNatural.h);
    let boxW, boxH;
    if(dispW/dispH > ratio){
      boxH=dispH*0.92;
      boxW=boxH*ratio;
    }else{
      boxW=dispW*0.92;
      boxH=boxW/ratio;
    }
    cropBox={x:(areaW-boxW)/2, y:(areaH-boxH)/2, w:boxW, h:boxH};
  }
  updateCropRectUI();
}

async function openCropModal(idx){
  if(idx<0||!processedImages[idx])return;
  cropTargetIndex=idx;
  cropSessionOriginal=processedImages[idx];
  cropRotation=0;
  cropViewZoom=1;
  cropPresetRatio='free';
  const slider=document.getElementById('cropRotateSlider');
  const valEl=document.getElementById('cropRotateValue');
  if(slider){slider.value=0}
  if(valEl)valEl.textContent='0 deg';
  const lock=document.getElementById('lockRatio');
  
  
 // if(lock) lock.checked=true;
  
  if(lock) lock.checked=false;
  
  const presetSel=document.getElementById('cropPresetSelect');
  if(presetSel) presetSel.value='free';
  const modal=document.getElementById('cropModal');
  if(!modal)return;
  /* Ensure modal is above all website chrome (Blogger/theme stacking) */
  if(modal.parentElement!==document.body){
    document.body.appendChild(modal);
  }
  modal.classList.add('show');
  lockBodyScroll(true);
  const rotated=await getRotatedCanvasFromDataUrl(cropSessionOriginal, 0);
  cropImgNatural={w:rotated.width, h:rotated.height};
  const cropImg=document.getElementById('cropImage');
  if(cropImg){
    cropImg.style.transform='none';
    cropImg.style.transformOrigin='center center';
    cropImg.src=rotated.toDataURL('image/jpeg',0.92);
  }
  requestAnimationFrame(()=>{
    requestAnimationFrame(()=>layoutCropImage(false));
  });
}

function closeCropModal(){
  const modal=document.getElementById('cropModal');
  if(modal) modal.classList.remove('show');
  lockBodyScroll(false);
  cropTargetIndex=-1;
  cropSessionOriginal=null;
  cropRotation=0;
  cropViewZoom=1;
  if(typeof closeCropSizeEditor==='function') closeCropSizeEditor();
  const imgEl=document.getElementById('cropImage');
  if(imgEl){ imgEl.style.transform='none'; imgEl.src=''; }
}

            
            
            
            
            
            
            
            
            
            
            
            
            
async function applyCropFromModal(){
  if(cropTargetIndex<0||!cropSessionOriginal)return;

  try{
    const rotated=await getRotatedCanvasFromDataUrl(
      cropSessionOriginal,
      cropRotation
    );

    const cropImg=document.getElementById('cropImage');
    const cropArea=document.getElementById('cropArea');

    if(!cropImg||!cropArea)return;

    /*
      IMPORTANT:
      Crop box coordinates are relative to #cropArea.
      The image can be zoomed, moved and CSS-rotated.
      Therefore we calculate the crop using the actual
      displayed image position and the four crop-box corners.
    */

    const imgLeft=parseFloat(cropImg.style.left)||0;
    const imgTop=parseFloat(cropImg.style.top)||0;

    const dispW=parseFloat(cropImg.style.width)||
      ((cropImgNatural.w||0)*cropDisplayScale);

    const dispH=parseFloat(cropImg.style.height)||
      ((cropImgNatural.h||0)*cropDisplayScale);

    const srcW=cropImgNatural.w||0;
    const srcH=cropImgNatural.h||0;

    if(!srcW||!srcH||!dispW||!dispH)return;

    const angle=(cropRotation||0)*Math.PI/180;

    const cos=Math.cos(angle);
    const sin=Math.sin(angle);

    /*
      CSS transform rotates the displayed image around its center.
      Convert crop-box corners back to the original image coordinate
      system and then into the rotated canvas coordinate system.
    */
    const imgCenterX=imgLeft+(dispW/2);
    const imgCenterY=imgTop+(dispH/2);

    const corners=[
      {x:cropBox.x,              y:cropBox.y},
      {x:cropBox.x+cropBox.w,     y:cropBox.y},
      {x:cropBox.x+cropBox.w,     y:cropBox.y+cropBox.h},
      {x:cropBox.x,              y:cropBox.y+cropBox.h}
    ];

    const rotatedPoints=[];

    corners.forEach(function(point){

      /*
        Step 1:
        Convert crop-area coordinates to coordinates relative
        to the center of the displayed image.
      */
      const dx=point.x-imgCenterX;
      const dy=point.y-imgCenterY;

      /*
        Step 2:
        Undo the CSS rotation.
      */
      const unrotX=(dx*cos)+(dy*sin);
      const unrotY=(-dx*sin)+(dy*cos);

      /*
        Step 3:
        Convert displayed-image coordinates to natural-image
        coordinates.
      */
      const sourceX=(unrotX+(dispW/2))*(srcW/dispW);
      const sourceY=(unrotY+(dispH/2))*(srcH/dispH);

      /*
        Step 4:
        Convert the natural-image point into the same rotated
        coordinate system used by getRotatedCanvasFromDataUrl().
      */
      const sourceCenterX=srcW/2;
      const sourceCenterY=srcH/2;

      const sdx=sourceX-sourceCenterX;
      const sdy=sourceY-sourceCenterY;

      const rotatedX=
        (sdx*cos)-(sdy*sin)+(rotated.width/2);

      const rotatedY=
        (sdx*sin)+(sdy*cos)+(rotated.height/2);

      rotatedPoints.push({
        x:rotatedX,
        y:rotatedY
      });
    });

    /*
      Find the exact axis-aligned rectangle containing
      the selected crop area.
    */
    const xs=rotatedPoints.map(function(p){return p.x;});
    const ys=rotatedPoints.map(function(p){return p.y;});

    const natX=Math.min.apply(null,xs);
    const natY=Math.min.apply(null,ys);
    const natRight=Math.max.apply(null,xs);
    const natBottom=Math.max.apply(null,ys);

    const natW=natRight-natX;
    const natH=natBottom-natY;

    const canvas=document.createElement('canvas');

    canvas.width=Math.max(1,Math.round(natW));
    canvas.height=Math.max(1,Math.round(natH));

    const ctx=canvas.getContext('2d');

    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled=true;
    ctx.imageSmoothingQuality='high';

    /*
      Keep the same white background behaviour.
      This also correctly handles crop areas extending
      outside the visible image after zoom/pan/rotation.
    */
    ctx.fillStyle='#fff';
    ctx.fillRect(0,0,canvas.width,canvas.height);

    /*
      Draw the exact selected area from the rotated canvas.
    */
    ctx.drawImage(
      rotated,
      natX,
      natY,
      natW,
      natH,
      0,
      0,
      canvas.width,
      canvas.height
    );

    const q=parseFloat(
      document.getElementById('jpgOutputQuality')?.value||'0.92'
    );

    const dataUrl=canvas.toDataURL(
      'image/jpeg',
      Math.max(0.7,q)
    );

 
    
    
    processedImages[cropTargetIndex]=dataUrl;
    
    
    
   const item=previewDiv.querySelectorAll('.preview-item')[cropTargetIndex];

if(item){
  const img=item.querySelector('img');
  if(img) img.src=dataUrl;
}

if(document.getElementById('mergeImagesCheck')?.checked!==false){
  processedPdfBytes=await convertJPGToPDF(processedImages);
}

    
    
    
    
    
if(typeof updateJpgPdfSizeDisplay==='function'){
  updateJpgPdfSizeDisplay();
}

    
   if(typeof window.cfRefreshJpgPdfSize==='function'){
  window.cfRefreshJpgPdfSize();
} 
    
    
updateButtonStates();
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
markDirty('converter');

showSuccessToast('Image cropped successfully!'); 
    
    

    const idx=cropTargetIndex;

    cropTargetIndex=-1;
    cropSessionOriginal=null;

    closeCropModal();

  

    if(typeof updatePreview==='function') updatePreview();
    if(typeof updateSummary==='function') updateSummary();

  }catch(err){
    showError('Crop failed: '+err.message,true);
  }
}
            
            
            
            
            
async function doAutoCropInModal(){
  if(cropTargetIndex<0||!cropSessionOriginal)return;
  try{
    setProgressText('Detecting document...');
    const autoBtn=document.getElementById('autoCropBtn');
    if(autoBtn){ autoBtn.disabled=true; autoBtn.textContent='Detecting...'; }
    /* Work on currently rotated view */
    const rotated=await getRotatedCanvasFromDataUrl(cropSessionOriginal, cropRotation);
    const tempUrl=rotated.toDataURL('image/jpeg',0.92);
    const result=await detectAndCropDocument(tempUrl);
    const cropped=result.dataUrl;
    cropSessionOriginal=cropped;
    cropRotation=0;
    cropViewZoom=1;
    const slider=document.getElementById('cropRotateSlider');
    const valEl=document.getElementById('cropRotateValue');
    if(slider)slider.value=0;
    if(valEl)valEl.textContent='0 deg';
    const img=new Image();
    img.onload=()=>{
      cropImgNatural={w:img.naturalWidth, h:img.naturalHeight};
      const cropImg=document.getElementById('cropImage');
      cropImg.src=cropped;
      requestAnimationFrame(()=>{
        requestAnimationFrame(()=>{
          layoutCropImage(false);
          setProgressText('');
          if(autoBtn){
            autoBtn.disabled=false;
            autoBtn.innerHTML='<i class="fa-solid fa-wand-magic-sparkles"></i> Auto Crop';
          }
          const conf=Math.round(result.confidence||0);
          if(result.method==='quad' && conf>=55){
            showSuccessToast('Document detected ('+conf+'% confidence)');
          }else if(result.method==='fallback'){
            showSuccessToast('Auto crop applied (content bounds)');
          }
        });
      });
    };
    img.src=cropped;
  }catch(err){
    setProgressText('');
    const autoBtn=document.getElementById('autoCropBtn');
    if(autoBtn){ autoBtn.disabled=false; autoBtn.innerHTML='<i class="fa-solid fa-wand-magic-sparkles"></i> Auto Crop'; }
    showError('Auto crop failed: '+err.message, true);
  }
}

function onCropStart(e){
  const point=e.touches?e.touches[0]:e;
  const target=e.target;
  if(target&&(target.id==='cropSizeBadge'||target.closest('#cropSizeBadge')||
     target.id==='cropSizeEditor'||target.closest('#cropSizeEditor'))){
    return;
  }
  cropDragging=true;
  cropStartMouse={x:point.clientX, y:point.clientY};
  cropStartBox={...cropBox};
  if(target.classList.contains('crop-handle')){
    cropDragMode=target.dataset.handle;
  }else if(target.id==='cropRect'||target.closest('.crop-rect')){
    cropDragMode='move';
  }else{
    cropDragMode=null;
    cropDragging=false;
  }
  e.preventDefault();
}
function onCropMove(e){
  if(!cropDragging||!cropDragMode)return;
  const point=e.touches?e.touches[0]:e;
  const dx=point.clientX-cropStartMouse.x;
  const dy=point.clientY-cropStartMouse.y;
  const ratio=getActiveCropRatio();
  const lock=!!ratio;
  const area=document.getElementById('cropArea');
  const areaW=area?area.clientWidth:480;
  const areaH=area?area.clientHeight:320;
  let nx=cropStartBox.x, ny=cropStartBox.y, nw=cropStartBox.w, nh=cropStartBox.h;
  if(cropDragMode==='move'){
    nx=cropStartBox.x+dx;
    ny=cropStartBox.y+dy;
  }else{
    if(cropDragMode.includes('r')) nw=cropStartBox.w+dx;
    if(cropDragMode.includes('l')){ nw=cropStartBox.w-dx; nx=cropStartBox.x+dx; }
    if(cropDragMode.includes('b')) nh=cropStartBox.h+dy;
    if(cropDragMode.includes('t')){ nh=cropStartBox.h-dy; ny=cropStartBox.y+dy; }
    if(lock){
      if(cropDragMode==='tm'||cropDragMode==='bm'){
        nw=nh*ratio;
        nx=cropStartBox.x+(cropStartBox.w-nw)/2;
      }else if(cropDragMode==='ml'||cropDragMode==='mr'){
        nh=nw/ratio;
        ny=cropStartBox.y+(cropStartBox.h-nh)/2;
      }else{
        if(Math.abs(dx)>Math.abs(dy)){
          nh=nw/ratio;
          if(cropDragMode.includes('t')) ny=cropStartBox.y+(cropStartBox.h-nh);
        }else{
          nw=nh*ratio;
          if(cropDragMode.includes('l')) nx=cropStartBox.x+(cropStartBox.w-nw);
        }
      }
    }
  }
  nw=Math.max(30,nw);
  nh=Math.max(30,nh);
  if(lock){
    if(cropDragMode.includes('r')||cropDragMode.includes('l')||cropDragMode==='mr'||cropDragMode==='ml'){
      nh=nw/ratio;
    }else if(cropDragMode!=='move'){
      nw=nh*ratio;
    }
  }
  nx=Math.max(0, Math.min(nx, areaW-nw));
  ny=Math.max(0, Math.min(ny, areaH-nh));
  if(nx+nw>areaW){ nw=areaW-nx; if(lock) nh=nw/ratio; }
  if(ny+nh>areaH){ nh=areaH-ny; if(lock) nw=nh*ratio; }
  cropBox={x:nx,y:ny,w:nw,h:nh};
  updateCropRectUI();
  e.preventDefault();
}
function onCropEnd(){ cropDragging=false; cropDragMode=null; }

async function processConverterFiles(files, appendOnly){
  try{
    if(!appendOnly && converterSortable){converterSortable.destroy();converterSortable=null}
    previewDiv.classList.remove('hidden-preview');
    if(!appendOnly){
      previewDiv.innerHTML='';
      processedImages=[];
      processedPdfBytes=null;
    }
    if(!files||!files.length){previewDiv.classList.add('hidden-preview');updateButtonStates();return}
    const q=getConverterQuality();
    const startIdx=appendOnly?previewDiv.querySelectorAll('.preview-item').length:0;

    if(conversionType.value==='pdf-to-jpg'){
      for(let i=startIdx;i<files.length;i++){
        const file=files[i];
        const ab=await file.arrayBuffer();
        const doc=await window.pdfjsLib.getDocument({data:ab}).promise;
        const page=await doc.getPage(1);
        const vp=page.getViewport({scale:1.2});
        const canvas=document.createElement('canvas');
        const ctx=canvas.getContext('2d');
        canvas.width=vp.width;canvas.height=vp.height;
        await page.render({canvasContext:ctx,viewport:vp}).promise;
        const item=document.createElement('div');
        item.className='preview-item';
        item.dataset.index=i;
        const dataUrl=canvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg',q);
        const img=new Image();
        img.src=dataUrl;
        item.appendChild(img);
        /* base64 length ≈ actual jpeg bytes * 1.37; use for exact-ish per-page size */
        const perPageBytes=Math.round((dataUrl.length-23)*0.75);
        item.dataset.perPage=perPageBytes;item.dataset.atQ=q;
        const removeBtn=document.createElement('button');
        removeBtn.className='remove-btn';
        removeBtn.innerHTML='<i class="fa-solid fa-xmark"></i>';
        removeBtn.onclick=e=>{
          e.stopPropagation();
          const idx=parseInt(item.dataset.index,10);
          selectedFiles.converter.splice(idx,1);
          item.remove();
          Array.from(previewDiv.querySelectorAll('.preview-item')).forEach((el,ni)=>el.dataset.index=ni);
          if(!selectedFiles.converter.length){previewDiv.classList.add('hidden-preview');processedPdfBytes=null;processedImages=[]}
          showFileName('converter',selectedFiles.converter);
          updateEstimatedSizes();
          updateButtonStates();
        };
        item.appendChild(removeBtn);
        item.dataset.pages=doc.numPages;
        const meta=document.createElement('div');
        meta.className='file-meta';
        const totalOut=perPageBytes*doc.numPages;
        const outStr=totalOut>=1048576?(totalOut/1048576).toFixed(2)+' MB':(totalOut/1024).toFixed(1)+' KB';
        meta.innerHTML=`<strong>${file.name}</strong><br>${doc.numPages} page${doc.numPages>1?'s':''} · ${formatSize(file.size)}`;
        item.appendChild(meta);
        previewDiv.appendChild(item);
      }
    }else{
      /* JPG → PDF */
    const images=appendOnly?processedImages.slice():[];
const originals=appendOnly?(processedImagesOriginal||[]).slice():[];
      for(let i=startIdx;i<files.length;i++){
        const file=files[i];
        const dataUrl=await new Promise(resolve=>{
          const reader=new FileReader();
          reader.onload=ev=>{
            const img=new Image();
            img.onload=()=>{
              const canvas=document.createElement('canvas');
              const ctx=canvas.getContext('2d');
              canvas.width=img.width;canvas.height=img.height;
              ctx.fillStyle='#fff';ctx.fillRect(0,0,canvas.width,canvas.height);
              ctx.drawImage(img,0,0);
            
              
              
              
              
              
              
              
              
              
              
              
              
              
              
              
              
                           /* Store full quality; Normal / High / Maximum apply in convertJPGToPDF */
              canvas.width=img.width;
              canvas.height=img.height;
              ctx.fillStyle='#fff';ctx.fillRect(0,0,canvas.width,canvas.height);
              ctx.drawImage(img,0,0,canvas.width,canvas.height);
              resolve(canvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg', 1));
              
              
              
              
              
              
            };
            img.src=ev.target.result;
          };
          reader.readAsDataURL(file);
        });
      images.push(dataUrl);
originals.push(dataUrl);
        const item=document.createElement('div');
        item.className='preview-item';
        item.dataset.index=i;
        item.dataset.rotation='0';
        item.style.cursor='grab';
        const imgEl=new Image();
        imgEl.src=dataUrl;
        item.appendChild(imgEl);
        const dragHandle=document.createElement('button');
        dragHandle.type='button';
        dragHandle.className='drag-handle';
        dragHandle.title='Drag to reorder';
        dragHandle.innerHTML='<i class="fa-solid fa-grip-lines"></i>';
        dragHandle.addEventListener('click',e=>e.stopPropagation());
        item.appendChild(dragHandle);
        const rotateBtn=document.createElement('button');
        rotateBtn.className='rotate-btn';
        rotateBtn.title='Rotate 90 degrees';
        rotateBtn.innerHTML='<i class="fa-solid fa-rotate-right"></i>';
        rotateBtn.onclick=async e=>{
          e.stopPropagation();
          const idx=parseInt(item.dataset.index,10);
        
          
          
         const orig=(processedImagesOriginal&&processedImagesOriginal[idx])||processedImages[idx];
if(!orig)return;
const nextAngle=(parseInt(item.dataset.rotation||'0',10)+90)%360;
const rotated=nextAngle===0?orig:await rotateDataUrl(orig,nextAngle);
processedImages[idx]=rotated;
imgEl.src=rotated;
item.dataset.rotation=String(nextAngle);
          
          
          
          if(document.getElementById('mergeImagesCheck')?.checked!==false){
            processedPdfBytes=await convertJPGToPDF(processedImages);
          }
          
          
          updateJpgPdfSizeDisplay&&updateJpgPdfSizeDisplay();
          
          if(typeof window.cfRefreshJpgPdfSize==='function') window.cfRefreshJpgPdfSize();
          
          updateButtonStates();
        };
        
        
        item.appendChild(rotateBtn);
        const cropBtn=document.createElement('button');
        cropBtn.className='crop-btn';
        cropBtn.title='Crop image';
        cropBtn.innerHTML='<i class="fa-solid fa-crop-simple"></i>';
        cropBtn.onclick=e=>{
          e.stopPropagation();
          const idx=parseInt(item.dataset.index,10);
          openCropModal(idx);
        };
        item.appendChild(cropBtn);
        const removeBtn=document.createElement('button');
        removeBtn.className='remove-btn';
        removeBtn.innerHTML='<i class="fa-solid fa-xmark"></i>';
        removeBtn.onclick=e=>{
          e.stopPropagation();
          const idx=parseInt(item.dataset.index,10);
          selectedFiles.converter.splice(idx,1);
          processedImages.splice(idx,1);
          item.remove();
          Array.from(previewDiv.querySelectorAll('.preview-item')).forEach((el,ni)=>el.dataset.index=ni);
          if(!selectedFiles.converter.length){
            previewDiv.classList.add('hidden-preview');
            processedPdfBytes=null;processedImages=[];
            if(converterSortable){converterSortable.destroy();converterSortable=null}
          }else{
            convertJPGToPDF(processedImages).then(b=>{processedPdfBytes=b;updateButtonStates()});
          }
          showFileName('converter',selectedFiles.converter);
          updateEstimatedSizes();
          updateButtonStates();
        };
        item.appendChild(removeBtn);
        const meta=document.createElement('div');
        meta.className='file-meta';
        meta.innerHTML=`<strong>${file.name}</strong><br>${formatSize(file.size)}`;
        item.appendChild(meta);
        previewDiv.appendChild(item);
      }
    processedImages=images;
processedImagesOriginal=originals;
      processedPdfBytes=await convertJPGToPDF(images);
      updateJpgPdfSizeDisplay();

      /* Enable drag-reorder for JPG→PDF */
      if(typeof Sortable!=='undefined'&&files.length>1){
        const isTouchUI=window.matchMedia('(hover: none) and (pointer: coarse)').matches||(('ontouchstart' in window||navigator.maxTouchPoints>0)&&window.innerWidth<=900);
        converterSortable=Sortable.create(previewDiv,{
          animation:180,
          delay:isTouchUI?150:0,
          delayOnTouchOnly:true,
          touchStartThreshold:isTouchUI?5:3,
          handle:isTouchUI?'.drag-handle':undefined,
          draggable:'.preview-item',
          filter:'.remove-btn,.rotate-btn,.crop-btn',
          preventOnFilter:true,
          forceFallback:isTouchUI,
          fallbackOnBody:isTouchUI,
          fallbackTolerance:isTouchUI?3:0,
          ghostClass:'sortable-ghost',
          chosenClass:'sortable-chosen',
          dragClass:'sortable-drag',
          onEnd:async()=>{
            const order=Array.from(previewDiv.querySelectorAll('.preview-item')).map(el=>parseInt(el.dataset.index,10));
            const newFiles=order.map(i=>selectedFiles.converter[i]);
            const newImages=order.map(i=>processedImages[i]);
            selectedFiles.converter=newFiles;
            processedImages=newImages;
            /* re-index */
            Array.from(previewDiv.querySelectorAll('.preview-item')).forEach((el,idx)=>el.dataset.index=idx);
            processedPdfBytes=await convertJPGToPDF(processedImages);
            updateButtonStates();
          }
        });
      }
    }
    updateEstimatedSizes();
    updateButtonStates();
  }catch(err){showError('Converter error: '+err.message,true)}
}

            
            
            
            
            
            
            
            
            
 async function processMergeSplitFiles(files, appendOnly){
  try{
    previewDiv.classList.remove('hidden-preview');
    if(mergeSplitType.value==='merge'){
      const existingCount=appendOnly?previewDiv.querySelectorAll('.preview-item').length:0;
      if(!appendOnly){
        previewDiv.innerHTML='';
        if(typeof mergeSortable!=='undefined'&&mergeSortable){try{mergeSortable.destroy()}catch(e){}mergeSortable=null}
      }
      const startIdx=appendOnly?existingCount:0;
      const filesToRender=appendOnly?files.slice(startIdx):files;
      for(let j=0;j<filesToRender.length;j++){
        const file=filesToRender[j];
        const i=startIdx+j;
        const ab=await file.arrayBuffer();
        const doc=await window.pdfjsLib.getDocument({data:ab}).promise;
        const page=await doc.getPage(1);
        const vp=page.getViewport({scale:.7});
        const canvas=document.createElement('canvas');
        const ctx=canvas.getContext('2d');
        canvas.width=vp.width;canvas.height=vp.height;
        await page.render({canvasContext:ctx,viewport:vp,intent:'display'}).promise;
        const item=document.createElement('div');
        item.className='preview-item';
        item.dataset.index=i;
        item.style.cursor='grab';
        item.appendChild(canvas);
        const dragHandle=document.createElement('button');
        dragHandle.type='button';
        dragHandle.className='drag-handle';
        dragHandle.title='Drag to reorder';
        dragHandle.innerHTML='<i class="fa-solid fa-grip-lines"></i>';
        dragHandle.addEventListener('click',e=>e.stopPropagation());
        item.appendChild(dragHandle);
        const removeBtn=document.createElement('button');
        removeBtn.className='remove-btn';
        removeBtn.innerHTML='<i class="fa-solid fa-xmark"></i>';
        removeBtn.onclick=e=>{
          e.stopPropagation();
          const idx=parseInt(item.dataset.index,10);
          selectedFiles.mergeSplit.splice(idx,1);
          item.remove();
          Array.from(previewDiv.querySelectorAll('.preview-item')).forEach((el,ni)=>el.dataset.index=ni);
          if(!selectedFiles.mergeSplit.length){previewDiv.classList.add('hidden-preview');processedPdfBytes=null}
          else if(mergeSplitType.value==='merge'&&selectedFiles.mergeSplit.length>=2){
            Promise.all(selectedFiles.mergeSplit.map(f=>f.arrayBuffer())).then(bufs=>mergePDFs(bufs)).then(b=>{processedPdfBytes=b;updateButtonStates()});
          }else{processedPdfBytes=null}
          showFileName('mergeSplit',selectedFiles.mergeSplit);
          updateButtonStates();
        };
        item.appendChild(removeBtn);
        const meta=document.createElement('div');
        meta.className='file-meta';
        meta.innerHTML=`<strong>${file.name}</strong><br>${formatSize(file.size)} · ${doc.numPages} page${doc.numPages>1?'s':''}`;
        item.appendChild(meta);
        previewDiv.appendChild(item);
      }
      if(selectedFiles.mergeSplit.length>=2){
        const buffers=await Promise.all(selectedFiles.mergeSplit.map(f=>f.arrayBuffer()));
        processedPdfBytes=await mergePDFs(buffers);
      }else processedPdfBytes=null;
      if(typeof mergeSortable!=='undefined'&&mergeSortable){try{mergeSortable.destroy()}catch(e){}mergeSortable=null}
      if(typeof Sortable!=='undefined'&&selectedFiles.mergeSplit.length>1){
        const isTouchUI=window.matchMedia('(hover: none) and (pointer: coarse)').matches||(('ontouchstart' in window||navigator.maxTouchPoints>0)&&window.innerWidth<=900);
        mergeSortable=Sortable.create(previewDiv,{
          animation:180,
          delay:isTouchUI?150:0,
          delayOnTouchOnly:true,
          touchStartThreshold:isTouchUI?5:3,
