          handle:isTouchUI?'.drag-handle':undefined,
          draggable:'.preview-item',
          filter:'.remove-btn',
          preventOnFilter:true,
          forceFallback:isTouchUI,
          fallbackOnBody:isTouchUI,
          fallbackTolerance:isTouchUI?3:0,
          ghostClass:'sortable-ghost',
          chosenClass:'sortable-chosen',
          dragClass:'sortable-drag',
          onEnd:async()=>{
            if(mergeSplitType.value!=='merge')return;
            const order=Array.from(previewDiv.querySelectorAll('.preview-item')).map(el=>parseInt(el.dataset.index,10));
            selectedFiles.mergeSplit=order.map(i=>selectedFiles.mergeSplit[i]);
            Array.from(previewDiv.querySelectorAll('.preview-item')).forEach((el,idx)=>el.dataset.index=idx);
            if(selectedFiles.mergeSplit.length>=2){
              const buffers=await Promise.all(selectedFiles.mergeSplit.map(f=>f.arrayBuffer()));
              processedPdfBytes=await mergePDFs(buffers);
            }
            updateButtonStates();
          }
        });
      }
    }else{
      if(files.length!==1)return;
      const ab=await files[0].arrayBuffer();
      pdfDoc=await window.pdfjsLib.getDocument({data:ab}).promise;
      /* select ALL pages by default */
      const allPages=Array.from({length:pdfDoc.numPages},(_,i)=>i+1);
      splitRanges.value=formatPageRanges(allPages);
      const smg=document.getElementById('splitMergeGroup');
      if(smg) smg.style.display='block';
      await renderInputPreview(ab,'split');
      if(files[0]) await showFileName('mergeSplit', files[0]);
    }
    updateButtonStates();
  }catch(err){showError('Merge/Split error: '+err.message,true)}
}         
            
            
            
            
            
            
            
            

function parsePageNumbers(input,totalPages){
  if(!input&&totalPages)return null;
  const pages=[];
  const ranges=input.split(',').map(s=>s.trim());
  for(const r of ranges){
    if(/^\d+$/.test(r)){const p=parseInt(r,10);if(totalPages&&(p<1||p>totalPages)){showError(`Page ${p} out of range (1-${totalPages}).`,true);return null}pages.push(p)}
    else if(/^\d+-\d+$/.test(r)){const[a,b]=r.split('-').map(Number);if(totalPages&&(a<1||b>totalPages||a>b)){showError(`Invalid range ${r}.`,true);return null}for(let j=a;j<=b;j++)pages.push(j)}
    else{showError(`Invalid format "${r}". Use e.g. 2,4-6.`,true);return null}
  }
  const unique=[...new Set(pages)].sort((a,b)=>a-b);
  return unique;
}
/* Smart page-range formatter: [1,2,3,4,6] → "1-4,6" */
function formatPageRanges(pages){
  if(!pages||!pages.length)return '';
  const sorted=[...new Set(pages)].map(Number).sort((a,b)=>a-b);
  const parts=[];
  let start=sorted[0],prev=sorted[0];
  for(let i=1;i<=sorted.length;i++){
    if(i<sorted.length&&sorted[i]===prev+1){prev=sorted[i];continue}
    parts.push(start===prev?String(start):start+'-'+prev);
    if(i<sorted.length){start=sorted[i];prev=sorted[i]}
  }
  return parts.join(',');
}

function parseSelectedFromInput(input,totalPages){
  const v=input.trim();
  if(!v)return [];
  return parsePageNumbers(v,totalPages)||[];
}


async function removePages(pagesToDelete,ab){
  const doc=await PDFLib.PDFDocument.load(ab);
  pagesToDelete.map(p=>p-1).reverse().forEach(i=>doc.removePage(i));
  return await doc.save();
}

            
            
            
            
            
            
            
            
            
 // async function applyLayout(ab){ generated by grok           
            
                     
 async function applyLayout(ab){
  const src=await PDFLib.PDFDocument.load(ab);
  const dest=await PDFLib.PDFDocument.create();
  const pagesPerSheet=parseInt(document.getElementById('pdf-tool-pagesPerSheet').value,10);
  const layoutBorder=document.getElementById('pdf-tool-layoutBorder').checked;
  const readingDirection=document.getElementById('pdf-tool-readingDirection').value;
  const pageSize=document.getElementById('pdf-tool-pageSize').value;
  const orientation=document.getElementById('pdf-tool-orientation').value;
  const innerMargin=parseFloat(document.getElementById('pdf-tool-innerMargin').value)*2.83465;
  const outerMargin=parseFloat(document.getElementById('pdf-tool-outerMargin').value)*2.83465;
  const pageSizes={'A0':[2383.94,3370.39],'A1':[1683.78,2383.94],'A2':[1190.55,1683.78],'A3':[841.89,1190.55],'A4':[595.28,841.89],'A5':[419.53,595.28],'A6':[297.64,419.53],'Letter':[612,792],'Legal':[612,1008],'B4':[708.66,1000.63],'B5':[498.90,708.66]};

  /* Grid first — needed for smart automatic orientation */
  const grid={2:[2,1],3:[3,1],4:[2,2],6:[3,2],8:[4,2],9:[3,3],10:[5,2],12:[4,3],15:[5,3]};
  let cols,rows;
  if(readingDirection==='ttb' && (pagesPerSheet===2 || pagesPerSheet===3)){
    cols=1;
    rows=pagesPerSheet;
  }else{
    [cols,rows]=grid[pagesPerSheet]||[2,1];
  }

  let dims;
  if(pageSize==='Original'){
    const p=src.getPage(0);
    dims=[p.getWidth(),p.getHeight()];
  }else if(pageSize==='Custom'){
    const w=parseFloat(document.getElementById('pdf-tool-customWidth').value);
    const h=parseFloat(document.getElementById('pdf-tool-customHeight').value);
    const unit=document.getElementById('pdf-tool-sharedUnit').value;
    const factor={mm:2.83465,cm:28.3465,inch:72}[unit];
    dims=[w*factor,h*factor];
  }else{
    dims=pageSizes[pageSize]||[595.28,841.89];
  }

  if(orientation==='landscape'){
    if(dims[0]<dims[1]) dims=[dims[1],dims[0]];
  }else if(orientation==='portrait'){
    if(dims[0]>dims[1]) dims=[dims[1],dims[0]];
  }else{
    /* Automatic: sheet matches grid shape (wide grid → landscape, tall → portrait) */
    var wantLandscape=cols>rows;
    var wantPortrait=rows>cols;
    if(wantLandscape && dims[0]<dims[1]) dims=[dims[1],dims[0]];
    else if(wantPortrait && dims[0]>dims[1]) dims=[dims[1],dims[0]];
    /* square grid (e.g. 2×2, 3×3): keep paper as chosen (A4 stays portrait) */
  }

  const cellW=(dims[0]-2*outerMargin-(cols-1)*innerMargin)/cols;
  const cellH=(dims[1]-2*outerMargin-(rows-1)*innerMargin)/rows;
  const total=src.getPageCount();
  const sheetCount=Math.ceil(total/pagesPerSheet);
  let i=0;
  let sheetIdx=0;

  while(i<total){
    sheetIdx++;
    if(typeof setProgressText==='function'){
      setProgressText('Layout sheet '+sheetIdx+' / '+sheetCount);
    }
    if(typeof updateProgress==='function'){
      updateProgress(Math.round((sheetIdx/sheetCount)*100));
    }

    const newPage=dest.addPage([dims[0],dims[1]]);
    const slice=src.getPages().slice(i,i+pagesPerSheet);
    const embedded=await dest.embedPages(slice);

    /* Uniform scale for this sheet — all pages same size, no uneven look */
    let sheetScale=Infinity;
    for(let j=0;j<embedded.length;j++){
      const natW=embedded[j].width||1;
      const natH=embedded[j].height||1;
      const s=Math.min(cellW/natW, cellH/natH);
      if(s<sheetScale) sheetScale=s;
    }
    if(!isFinite(sheetScale)||sheetScale<=0) sheetScale=1;

    for(let j=0;j<embedded.length;j++){
      let row,col;
      if(readingDirection==='ttb'){
        col=Math.floor(j/rows);
        row=j%rows;
      }else{
        row=Math.floor(j/cols);
        col=j%cols;
        if(readingDirection==='rtl') col=cols-1-col;
      }

      const cellX=outerMargin+col*(cellW+innerMargin);
      const cellY=dims[1]-outerMargin-(row+1)*cellH-row*innerMargin;

      const emb=embedded[j];
      const natW=emb.width||1;
      const natH=emb.height||1;
      const drawW=natW*sheetScale;
      const drawH=natH*sheetScale;
      const x=cellX+(cellW-drawW)/2;
      const y=cellY+(cellH-drawH)/2;

      newPage.drawPage(emb,{x,y,width:drawW,height:drawH});
      if(layoutBorder){
        newPage.drawRectangle({
          x:x,y:y,width:drawW,height:drawH,
          borderWidth:1,
          borderColor:PDFLib.rgb(0,0,0)
        });
      }
    }
    i+=pagesPerSheet;
  }

  if(typeof setProgressText==='function') setProgressText('');
  if(typeof updateProgress==='function') updateProgress(0);
  return await dest.save();
}            
            
   
            
     
   
            
            
            
            
            
            
            
            
  /*
========================================
BACKUP: Improved applyLayout()
========================================

async function applyLayout(ab){
  const src=await PDFLib.PDFDocument.load(ab);
  const dest=await PDFLib.PDFDocument.create();

  const getEl=id=>document.getElementById(id);

  const pagesPerSheetRaw=parseInt(getEl('pdf-tool-pagesPerSheet')?.value,10);
  const pagesPerSheet=
    Number.isFinite(pagesPerSheetRaw)&&pagesPerSheetRaw>0
      ? pagesPerSheetRaw
      : 2;

  const layoutBorder=!!getEl('pdf-tool-layoutBorder')?.checked;
  const readingDirection=getEl('pdf-tool-readingDirection')?.value||'ltr';
  const orientation=getEl('pdf-tool-orientation')?.value||'automatic';
  const pageSize=getEl('pdf-tool-pageSize')?.value||'Original';

  const mmToPt=2.83465;

  let outerMargin=parseFloat(getEl('pdf-tool-outerMargin')?.value);
  let innerMargin=parseFloat(getEl('pdf-tool-innerMargin')?.value);

  if(!Number.isFinite(outerMargin)||outerMargin<0)outerMargin=0;
  if(!Number.isFinite(innerMargin)||innerMargin<0)innerMargin=0;

  outerMargin*=mmToPt;
  innerMargin*=mmToPt;

  const pageSizes={
    A0:[2383.94,3370.39],
    A1:[1683.78,2383.94],
    A2:[1190.55,1683.78],
    A3:[841.89,1190.55],
    A4:[595.28,841.89],
    A5:[419.53,595.28],
    A6:[297.64,419.53],
    Letter:[612,792],
    Legal:[612,1008],
    B4:[708.66,1000.63],
    B5:[498.90,708.66]
  };

  let baseDims;

  if(pageSize==='Original'){
    const firstPage=src.getPage(0);
    baseDims=[
      firstPage.getWidth(),
      firstPage.getHeight()
    ];
  }
  else if(pageSize==='Custom'){
    const w=parseFloat(getEl('pdf-tool-customWidth')?.value);
    const h=parseFloat(getEl('pdf-tool-customHeight')?.value);
    const unit=getEl('pdf-tool-sharedUnit')?.value||'mm';

    const factor={
      mm:2.83465,
      cm:28.3465,
      inch:72
    }[unit]||2.83465;

    const customW=
      Number.isFinite(w)&&w>0
        ? w*factor
        : 595.28;

    const customH=
      Number.isFinite(h)&&h>0
        ? h*factor
        : 841.89;

    baseDims=[customW,customH];
  }
  else{
    baseDims=pageSizes[pageSize]||[595.28,841.89];
  }

  const grid={
    2:[2,1],
    3:[3,1],
    4:[2,2],
    6:[3,2],
    8:[4,2],
    9:[3,3],
    10:[5,2],
    12:[4,3],
    15:[5,3]
  };

  const baseGrid=grid[pagesPerSheet]||[2,1];

  let cols=baseGrid[0];
  let rows=baseGrid[1];

  
  if(readingDirection==='ttb'){
    cols=baseGrid[1];
    rows=baseGrid[0];
  }

  const sourcePages=src.getPages();

  const sourceSizes=sourcePages.map(p=>{
    let w=p.getWidth();
    let h=p.getHeight();

    if(!Number.isFinite(w)||w<=0)w=595.28;
    if(!Number.isFinite(h)||h<=0)h=841.89;

    return {w,h};
  });

  function orientationScore(sheetDims){

    const sheetW=sheetDims[0];
    const sheetH=sheetDims[1];

    const usableW=
      sheetW-
      2*outerMargin-
      (cols-1)*innerMargin;

    const usableH=
      sheetH-
      2*outerMargin-
      (rows-1)*innerMargin;

    if(usableW<=0||usableH<=0)return -Infinity;

    const cellW=usableW/cols;
    const cellH=usableH/rows;

    if(cellW<=0||cellH<=0)return -Infinity;

    const sampleCount=Math.min(sourceSizes.length,30);

    if(sampleCount===0)return 0;

    let score=0;

    for(let i=0;i<sampleCount;i++){

      const pw=sourceSizes[i].w;
      const ph=sourceSizes[i].h;

      const scale=Math.min(
        cellW/pw,
        cellH/ph
      );

      if(scale<=0)continue;

      score+=(pw*ph)*(scale*scale);
    }

    return score/sampleCount;
  }

  let dims=[baseDims[0],baseDims[1]];

  if(orientation==='portrait'){

    if(dims[0]>dims[1]){
      dims=[dims[1],dims[0]];
    }

  }
  else if(orientation==='landscape'){

    if(dims[0]<dims[1]){
      dims=[dims[1],dims[0]];
    }

  }
  else{

    const portraitDims=
      baseDims[0]<=baseDims[1]
        ? [baseDims[0],baseDims[1]]
        : [baseDims[1],baseDims[0]];

    const landscapeDims=
      baseDims[0]>=baseDims[1]
        ? [baseDims[0],baseDims[1]]
        : [baseDims[1],baseDims[0]];

    const portraitScore=
      orientationScore(portraitDims);

    const landscapeScore=
      orientationScore(landscapeDims);

    if(landscapeScore>portraitScore){
      dims=landscapeDims;
    }
    else{
      dims=portraitDims;
    }
  }

  const minCellSize=1;

  const maxOuterX=Math.max(
    0,
    (
      dims[0]-
      (cols-1)*innerMargin-
      minCellSize*cols
    )/2
  );

  const maxOuterY=Math.max(
    0,
    (
      dims[1]-
      (rows-1)*innerMargin-
      minCellSize*rows
    )/2
  );

  outerMargin=Math.min(
    outerMargin,
    maxOuterX,
    maxOuterY
  );

  const maxInnerX=
    cols>1
      ? Math.max(
          0,
          (
            dims[0]-
            2*outerMargin-
            minCellSize*cols
          )/(cols-1)
        )
      : innerMargin;

  const maxInnerY=
    rows>1
      ? Math.max(
          0,
          (
            dims[1]-
            2*outerMargin-
            minCellSize*rows
          )/(rows-1)
        )
      : innerMargin;

  if(cols>1){
    innerMargin=Math.min(innerMargin,maxInnerX);
  }

  if(rows>1){
    innerMargin=Math.min(innerMargin,maxInnerY);
  }

  const pageW=
    (
      dims[0]-
      2*outerMargin-
      (cols-1)*innerMargin
    )/cols;

  const pageH=
    (
      dims[1]-
      2*outerMargin-
      (rows-1)*innerMargin
    )/rows;

  if(pageW<=0||pageH<=0){
    throw new Error('Invalid layout margins or page size.');
  }

  const total=sourcePages.length;

  for(let start=0;start<total;start+=pagesPerSheet){

    const newPage=dest.addPage(dims);

    const slice=sourcePages.slice(
      start,
      start+pagesPerSheet
    );

    const embedded=await dest.embedPages(slice);

    for(let j=0;j<embedded.length;j++){

      let row;
      let col;

      if(readingDirection==='ttb'){

        
        row=j%rows;
        col=Math.floor(j/rows);

      }
      else{

        row=Math.floor(j/cols);
        col=j%cols;

        if(readingDirection==='rtl'){
          col=cols-1-col;
        }
      }

      const cellX=
        outerMargin+
        col*(pageW+innerMargin);

      const cellY=
        dims[1]-
        outerMargin-
        (row+1)*pageH-
        row*innerMargin;

      const sourceW=
        sourceSizes[start+j]?.w||595.28;

      const sourceH=
        sourceSizes[start+j]?.h||841.89;

      const scale=Math.min(
        pageW/sourceW,
        pageH/sourceH
      );

      const drawW=sourceW*scale;
      const drawH=sourceH*scale;

      const drawX=
        cellX+
        (pageW-drawW)/2;

      const drawY=
        cellY+
        (pageH-drawH)/2;

      newPage.drawPage(
        embedded[j],
        {
          x:drawX,
          y:drawY,
          width:drawW,
          height:drawH
        }
      );

      if(layoutBorder){

        newPage.drawRectangle({
          x:drawX,
          y:drawY,
          width:drawW,
          height:drawH,
          borderWidth:1,
          borderColor:PDFLib.rgb(0,0,0)
        });
      }
    }
  }

  return await dest.save();
}

========================================
END BACKUP: Improved applyLayout()
========================================
*/          
            
            
            
            
            
            
            
            
            
            
            
            
            
            
            
            
            
            

async function compressPDF(ab,qualityOrTarget){
  const isTarget=qualityPreset.value==='target';
  const originalSize=ab.byteLength||ab.length;
  const doc=await window.pdfjsLib.getDocument({data:ab}).promise;
  const numPages=doc.numPages;
  
  
    window._cfPagePass = 0;
  async function renderAtQuality(q, scaleMul){
        window._cfPagePass = (window._cfPagePass || 0) + 1;
    var passNo = window._cfPagePass;
    
    
    const images=[];
    const baseScale=scaleMul!=null?scaleMul:(q>=.75?1.2:q>=.45?0.95:0.7);
    for(let p=1;p<=numPages;p++){
      
    
      
      
      
          if(passNo === 1){
        var fi = (typeof window._cfFileIndex === 'number') ? window._cfFileIndex : 0;
        var ft = window._cfFileTotal || 1;
        var filePct = Math.round((fi / ft) * 100);
        setProgressText('Compressing ' + (fi + 1) + ' / ' + ft + ' | Page ' + p + ' / ' + numPages + ' (' + filePct + '%)');
      }
      
      
      
      
      
      
      
      
      
      
      const page=await doc.getPage(p);
      const vp=page.getViewport({scale:baseScale});
      const canvas=document.createElement('canvas');
      const ctx=canvas.getContext('2d');
      canvas.width=vp.width;canvas.height=vp.height;
      await page.render({canvasContext:ctx,viewport:vp}).promise;
      images.push(canvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg', Math.max(0.15, Math.min(0.92, q))));
      canvas.width=0;canvas.height=0;
    }
    const newDoc=await PDFLib.PDFDocument.create();
    let refW=0;
    for(let idx=0;idx<images.length;idx++){
      const jpgBytes=Uint8Array.from(atob(images[idx].split(',')[1]),c=>c.charCodeAt(0));
      const jpg=await newDoc.embedJpg(jpgBytes);
      const{width,height}=jpg;
      if(idx===0)refW=width;
      const s=refW/width;
      const w=refW;
      const h=Math.round(height*s);
      const page=newDoc.addPage([w,h]);
      page.drawImage(jpg,{x:0,y:0,width:w,height:h});
    }
    return await newDoc.save();
  }
  /* Always pick result that is STRICTLY smaller than original when possible */
  async function pickBest(candidates){
    let best=null;
    for(const b of candidates){
      if(!b)continue;
      if(b.length>=originalSize)continue;
      if(!best||b.length<best.length) best=b;
    }
    return best;
  }

  if(!isTarget){
    /* Preset: try primary quality, then stronger compression if size grew */
    const q=typeof qualityOrTarget==='number'?qualityOrTarget:0.6;
    let result=await renderAtQuality(q);
    if(result.length>=originalSize){
      result=await renderAtQuality(Math.min(q,0.35), 0.65);
    }
    if(result.length>=originalSize){
      result=await renderAtQuality(0.2, 0.5);
    }
    if(result.length>=originalSize){
      /* cannot shrink further without extreme loss — return original */
      return new Uint8Array(ab);
    }
    return result;
  }

  /* Target mode: binary search for size <= targetKB AND < original */
  const targetBytes=Math.min(qualityOrTarget*1024, originalSize-1);
  if(targetBytes<=0||originalSize<=targetBytes){
    return new Uint8Array(ab); /* already under target */
  }
  /* Prefer result as close as possible to target, still <= target and < original */
  let lo=0.12, hi=0.92, bestUnder=null;
  for(let iter=0;iter<11;iter++){
    const mid=(lo+hi)/2;
    const scaleMul=mid>=0.7?1.15:mid>=0.5?1.0:mid>=0.35?0.85:0.65;
    const bytes=await renderAtQuality(mid, scaleMul);
    if(bytes.length<=targetBytes && bytes.length<originalSize){
      if(!bestUnder || bytes.length>bestUnder.length) bestUnder=bytes;
      lo=mid; /* try higher quality to get closer to target */
    }else{
      hi=mid;
    }
  }
  if(bestUnder) return bestUnder;
  let forced=await renderAtQuality(0.18, 0.5);
  if(forced.length<originalSize && forced.length<=targetBytes) return forced;
  if(forced.length<originalSize) return forced;
  return new Uint8Array(ab);
}


async function renderJpgPdfOutputPreview(pdfBytes){
  if(!pdfBytes||!previewDiv)return;
  try{
    const doc=await window.pdfjsLib.getDocument({data:pdfBytes}).promise;
    const n=doc.numPages;
    const items=previewDiv.querySelectorAll('.preview-item');
    /* Replace each card's image with rendered PDF page (download-accurate) */
    for(let i=1;i<=n;i++){
      const page=await doc.getPage(i);
      const vp=page.getViewport({scale:0.6});
      const canvas=document.createElement('canvas');
      const ctx=canvas.getContext('2d');
      canvas.width=vp.width;canvas.height=vp.height;
      await page.render({canvasContext:ctx,viewport:vp,intent:'display'}).promise;
      const item=items[i-1];
      if(item){
        const oldImg=item.querySelector('img,canvas');
        if(oldImg) oldImg.replaceWith(canvas);
        else item.insertBefore(canvas, item.firstChild);
      }
    }
  }catch(e){console.warn('preview render',e)}
}

async function convertJPGToPDF(images){
  const pdfDoc=await PDFLib.PDFDocument.create();
  const q=parseFloat(document.getElementById('jpgOutputQuality')?.value||'0.72');
  const pageSizeMode=document.getElementById('jpgPageSize')?.value||'auto';
  const orientMode=document.getElementById('jpgOrientation')?.value||'auto';
  const marginSel=document.getElementById('jpgMargin')?.value||'0';
  let margin=marginSel==='custom'?parseFloat(document.getElementById('jpgCustomMargin')?.value||'24'):parseFloat(marginSel)||0;

  const SIZES={
    a4:[595.28,841.89], letter:[612,792], legal:[612,1008], a3:[841.89,1190.55], a5:[419.53,595.28]
  };

  /* equal-width / equal-width-ratio: common page width, keep aspect ratio */
  let equalW=0;
  if(pageSizeMode==='equal-width'||pageSizeMode==='equal-width-ratio'){
    for(const dataUrl of images){
      const img=await new Promise((res,rej)=>{const im=new Image();im.onload=()=>res(im);im.onerror=rej;im.src=dataUrl});
      if(img.naturalWidth>equalW) equalW=img.naturalWidth;
    }
    equalW=Math.max(equalW, 100);
    
    
    // Equal Page Width (Keep Ratio) option will be made mobile friendly (not scrolled horizontally) by add only 2 line, if don't want then comment these 2 below lines
        const EQUAL_MAX_W=595.28;
    if(equalW>EQUAL_MAX_W) equalW=EQUAL_MAX_W;
    
    
    
  }

  for(const dataUrl of images){
   
    
    
    
    
    
    
       let embedBytes;
    if(q<0.95){
      /* Normal (0.72) / High (0.85): compress; Normal pe max 1800px */
      const img=await new Promise((res,rej)=>{const im=new Image();im.onload=()=>res(im);im.onerror=rej;im.src=dataUrl});
      const maxDim=(q<=0.75)?1800:99999;
      const sc=Math.min(1, maxDim/Math.max(img.naturalWidth, img.naturalHeight));
      const c=document.createElement('canvas');
      c.width=Math.round(img.naturalWidth*sc);
      c.height=Math.round(img.naturalHeight*sc);
      c.getContext('2d').drawImage(img,0,0,c.width,c.height);
      const qUrl=c.toDataURL('image/jpeg',q);
      embedBytes=Uint8Array.from(atob(qUrl.split(',')[1]),ch=>ch.charCodeAt(0));
      c.width=0;c.height=0;
    }else{
      /* Maximum (1): full quality, no re-compress */
      embedBytes=await fetch(dataUrl).then(r=>r.arrayBuffer());
    }
    
    
    
    
    let jpg;
    try{jpg=await pdfDoc.embedJpg(embedBytes)}
    catch(e){jpg=await pdfDoc.embedPng(embedBytes)}
    const iw=jpg.width, ih=jpg.height;

    let pageW, pageH, fitMode='fit';

    if(pageSizeMode==='a4-fixed'){
      /* Classic: always A4 portrait 595.28×841.89, fit, center, no crop/stretch */
      pageW=595.28; pageH=841.89; fitMode='fit';
    }else if(pageSizeMode==='letter-fixed'){
      /* Always Letter portrait 612×792, fit, center, no crop/stretch */
      pageW=612; pageH=792; fitMode='fit';
    }else if(pageSizeMode==='auto'||pageSizeMode==='original'){
      pageW=iw; pageH=ih; fitMode='original';
    }else if(pageSizeMode==='equal-width'||pageSizeMode==='equal-width-ratio'){
      pageW=equalW+2*margin;
      const scale=equalW/iw;
      pageH=ih*scale+2*margin;
      fitMode='fit';
    }else if(pageSizeMode==='custom'){
          const MM_TO_PT=72/25.4;
      pageW=(parseFloat(document.getElementById('jpgCustomW')?.value)||210)*MM_TO_PT;
      pageH=(parseFloat(document.getElementById('jpgCustomH')?.value)||297)*MM_TO_PT;
      fitMode='fit';
    }else if(pageSizeMode.startsWith('fit-')){
      const key=pageSizeMode.slice(4);
      [pageW,pageH]=SIZES[key]||SIZES.a4;
      fitMode='fit';
    }else if(pageSizeMode.startsWith('fill-')){
      const key=pageSizeMode.slice(5);
      [pageW,pageH]=SIZES[key]||SIZES.a4;
      fitMode='fill';
    }else{
      [pageW,pageH]=SIZES.a4; fitMode='fit';
    }

    if(pageSizeMode!=='auto'&&pageSizeMode!=='original'&&pageSizeMode!=='equal-width'&&pageSizeMode!=='equal-width-ratio'&&pageSizeMode!=='a4-fixed'&&pageSizeMode!=='letter-fixed'){
      if(orientMode==='portrait'&&pageW>pageH){[pageW,pageH]=[pageH,pageW]}
      else if(orientMode==='landscape'&&pageH>pageW){[pageW,pageH]=[pageH,pageW]}
      else if(orientMode==='auto'){
        if(iw>ih && pageW<pageH){[pageW,pageH]=[pageH,pageW]}
        if(ih>iw && pageH<pageW){[pageW,pageH]=[pageH,pageW]}
      }
    }

    const maxW=Math.max(10, pageW-2*margin);
    const maxH=Math.max(10, pageH-2*margin);
    let drawW, drawH, x, y;

    if(fitMode==='original'||pageSizeMode==='auto'){
      drawW=iw; drawH=ih;
      pageW=iw+2*margin; pageH=ih+2*margin;
      x=margin; y=margin;
    }else if(pageSizeMode==='equal-width'||pageSizeMode==='equal-width-ratio'){
      /* keep aspect ratio — scale to common width, height follows */
      drawW=equalW; drawH=ih*(equalW/iw);
      x=margin; y=margin;
      pageW=equalW+2*margin; pageH=drawH+2*margin;
    }else if(fitMode==='fill'){
      const s=Math.max(maxW/iw, maxH/ih);
      drawW=iw*s; drawH=ih*s;
      x=margin+(maxW-drawW)/2; y=margin+(maxH-drawH)/2;
    }else{
      /* fit — preserve aspect ratio, no crop/distort */
      const s=Math.min(maxW/iw, maxH/ih);
      drawW=iw*s; drawH=ih*s;
      x=margin+(maxW-drawW)/2; y=margin+(maxH-drawH)/2;
    }

    const page=pdfDoc.addPage([pageW, pageH]);
    page.drawImage(jpg,{x,y,width:drawW,height:drawH});
  }
  return await pdfDoc.save();
}


async function convertPDFToJPG(ab,maxPages=999){
  const doc=await window.pdfjsLib.getDocument({data:ab}).promise;
  const num=Math.min(doc.numPages,maxPages);
 
  
   
  
  
  
   const q=getConverterQuality();
  
  
  /* ============================================================
   PDF → JPG Quality Control (only 2 modes)
   ------------------------------------------------------------
   const scale = q >= 0.90 ? 7 : 4.5, jpegQ = q >= 0.90 ? 0.95 : q;

   Full meaning of this line:
   • q = select se aane wali value (Normal = 0.75, High = 0.92)
   • scale  = PDF page kitna zoom karke render hogi (clearness)
   • jpegQ  = final JPG ki compression quality (0 se 1)

   Condition breakdown:
   • agar q >= 0.90 (High)  → scale = 7   , jpegQ = 0.95
   • agar q < 0.90  (Normal) → scale = 4.5 , jpegQ = q (yani 0.75)

   Result:
   • Normal (Default) → scale 4.5 + jpegQ 0.75  (balanced + clear)
   • High Quality     → scale 7   + jpegQ 0.95  (bahut sharp + badi file)

   Future edit:
   - Normal scale badhana:  4.5 ko 5 / 5.5 kar do
   - High scale badhana:    7 ko 8 kar do (zyada heavy + slow hoga)
   
   
   min scale 3 max scale 6
   const scale = q >= 0.90 ? 6 : 3, jpegQ = q >= 0.90 ? 0.95 : q;
   ============================================================ */
  
  
  
  
  
  
 /* arun PDF→JPG: Normal=scale 4.5, High=scale 7 */
    // const scale = q >= 0.90 ? 7 : 4.5, jpegQ = q >= 0.90 ? 0.95 : q;
  
  
  
  
  
 /* PDF→JPG scale — FUTURE ME SIRF YAHAN CHANGE KARO */
window.cfGetPdfToJpgScale = function (q) {
  var isMobile = /Android|iPhone|iPad|iPod|Mobile/i.test(navigator.userAgent)
    || (window.matchMedia && matchMedia('(hover:none) and (pointer:coarse)').matches);
  var scale = isMobile
    ? (q >= 0.90 ? 3.5 : 2.5)   // mobile: High=3.5, Normal=2.5
    : (q >= 0.90 ? 6 : 4);      // desktop: High=6, Normal=4
  var jpegQ = q >= 0.90 ? 0.93 : q;
  return { scale: scale, jpegQ: jpegQ };
};

var sq = window.cfGetPdfToJpgScale(q);
var scale = sq.scale;
var jpegQ = sq.jpegQ;
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  const images=[];
  for(let i=1;i<=num;i++){
    const page=await doc.getPage(i);
    const vp=page.getViewport({scale:scale});
    const canvas=document.createElement('canvas');
    const ctx=canvas.getContext('2d');
    canvas.width=vp.width;canvas.height=vp.height;
    await page.render({canvasContext:ctx,viewport:vp}).promise;
    images.push(canvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg',jpegQ));
    
    
    
    
    canvas.remove();
  }
  return images;
}

async function mergePDFs(buffers){
  const merged=await PDFLib.PDFDocument.create();
  for(const buf of buffers){
    const doc=await PDFLib.PDFDocument.load(buf);
    const pages=await merged.copyPages(doc,doc.getPageIndices());
    pages.forEach(p=>merged.addPage(p));
  }
  return await merged.save();
}

async function splitPDF(ab,pagesToSplit){
  const src=await PDFLib.PDFDocument.load(ab);
  const total=src.getPageCount();
  const pageNums=pagesToSplit||Array.from({length:total},(_,i)=>i+1);
  const docs=[];
  for(const num of pageNums){
    const newDoc=await PDFLib.PDFDocument.create();
    const[copied]=await newDoc.copyPages(src,[num-1]);
    newDoc.addPage(copied);
    docs.push(await newDoc.save());
  }
  return docs;
}

async function renderInputPreview(ab, mode){
  if(!pdfDoc)return;
  previewDiv.innerHTML='';
  const doc=await window.pdfjsLib.getDocument({data:ab}).promise;
  const totalPages=doc.numPages;
  const num=totalPages; /* show all pages for remove & split */
  /* which pages already selected? */
  let selectedSet=new Set();
  if(mode==='remove'){
    const parsed=parseSelectedFromInput(pagesToRemove.value,totalPages);
    parsed.forEach(p=>selectedSet.add(p));
  }else if(mode==='split'){
    const parsed=parseSelectedFromInput(splitRanges.value,totalPages);
    if(parsed.length){
      parsed.forEach(p=>selectedSet.add(p));
    }else{
      /* default: all pages selected */
      for(let p=1;p<=totalPages;p++) selectedSet.add(p);
      if(typeof splitRanges!=='undefined'&&splitRanges){
        splitRanges.value=formatPageRanges(Array.from(selectedSet));
      }
    }
  }
  for(let i=1;i<=num;i++){
    const page=await doc.getPage(i);
    const vp=page.getViewport({scale: mode==='split'||mode==='remove' ? 0.55 : Math.min(currentScale||1, 0.9)});
    const canvas=document.createElement('canvas');
    const ctx=canvas.getContext('2d');
    canvas.width=vp.width;canvas.height=vp.height;
    await page.render({canvasContext:ctx,viewport:vp}).promise;
    const item=document.createElement('div');
    item.className='preview-item';
    item.dataset.page=i;
    item.appendChild(canvas);
    if(mode==='remove'){
      item.classList.add('page-selectable');
      const dragHandle=document.createElement('button');
      dragHandle.type='button';
      dragHandle.className='drag-handle';
      dragHandle.title='Drag to reorder';
      dragHandle.innerHTML='<i class="fa-solid fa-grip-lines"></i>';
      dragHandle.addEventListener('click',e=>e.stopPropagation());
      item.appendChild(dragHandle);
      if(selectedSet.has(i)) item.classList.add('marked-remove');
      item.addEventListener('click',e=>{
        if(e.target.closest('.remove-btn,.drag-handle'))return;
        e.preventDefault();
        toggleRemovePage(i, totalPages, item);
      });
    }else if(mode==='split'){
      item.classList.add('page-selectable');
      const tick=document.createElement('div');
      tick.className='page-tick';
      tick.innerHTML='<i class="fa-solid fa-check"></i>';
      item.appendChild(tick);
      const dragHandle=document.createElement('button');
      dragHandle.type='button';
      dragHandle.className='drag-handle';
      dragHandle.title='Drag to reorder';
      dragHandle.innerHTML='<i class="fa-solid fa-grip-lines"></i>';
      dragHandle.addEventListener('click',e=>e.stopPropagation());
      item.appendChild(dragHandle);
      if(selectedSet.has(i)) item.classList.add('marked-keep');
      item.addEventListener('click',e=>{
        if(e.target.closest('.drag-handle'))return;
        e.preventDefault();
        toggleSplitPage(i, totalPages, item);
      });
    }
    const meta=document.createElement('div');
    meta.className='file-meta';
    meta.innerHTML=`<strong>Page ${i}</strong>`;
    item.appendChild(meta);
    previewDiv.appendChild(item);
  }
  /* Split: drag-reorder pages (touch-friendly) */
  if(mode==='split'&&typeof Sortable!=='undefined'){
    if(previewDiv._splitSortable){try{previewDiv._splitSortable.destroy()}catch(e){}}
    const isTouchUI=window.matchMedia('(hover: none) and (pointer: coarse)').matches||(('ontouchstart' in window||navigator.maxTouchPoints>0)&&window.innerWidth<=900);
    previewDiv._splitSortable=Sortable.create(previewDiv,{
      animation:120,
      draggable:'.preview-item',
      delay:isTouchUI?150:0,
      delayOnTouchOnly:true,
      touchStartThreshold:isTouchUI?5:3,
      handle:isTouchUI?'.drag-handle':undefined,
      filter:'.remove-btn,.page-tick',
      preventOnFilter:true,
      forceFallback:isTouchUI,
      fallbackOnBody:isTouchUI,
      fallbackTolerance:isTouchUI?3:0,
      ghostClass:'sortable-ghost',
      chosenClass:'sortable-chosen',
      onEnd:function(){
        const order=Array.from(previewDiv.querySelectorAll('.preview-item')).map(el=>parseInt(el.dataset.page,10));
        previewDiv._splitOrder=order;
        const sel=new Set(parseSelectedFromInput(splitRanges.value, order.length||999));
        const orderedSelected=order.filter(p=>sel.has(p));
        if(orderedSelected.length) splitRanges.value=formatPageRanges(orderedSelected);
        updateButtonStates();
      }
    });
  }
  /* Remove tab: drag-reorder pages (updates PDF order) */
  if(mode==='remove'&&typeof Sortable!=='undefined'){
    if(previewDiv._removeSortable){try{previewDiv._removeSortable.destroy()}catch(e){}}
    const isTouchUI=window.matchMedia('(hover: none) and (pointer: coarse)').matches||(('ontouchstart' in window||navigator.maxTouchPoints>0)&&window.innerWidth<=900);
    previewDiv._removeSortable=Sortable.create(previewDiv,{
      animation:150,
      draggable:'.preview-item',
      delay:isTouchUI?150:0,
      delayOnTouchOnly:true,
      touchStartThreshold:isTouchUI?5:3,
      handle:isTouchUI?'.drag-handle':undefined,
      filter:'.remove-btn',
      preventOnFilter:true,
      forceFallback:isTouchUI,
      fallbackOnBody:isTouchUI,
      fallbackTolerance:isTouchUI?3:0,
      ghostClass:'sortable-ghost',
      chosenClass:'sortable-chosen',
      onEnd:async function(){
        if(!selectedFiles.remove)return;
        const order=Array.from(previewDiv.querySelectorAll('.preview-item')).map(el=>parseInt(el.dataset.page,10));
        try{
          setProcessing(true);
          setProgressText('Reordering pages...');
          const ab=await selectedFiles.remove.arrayBuffer();
          const src=await PDFLib.PDFDocument.load(ab);
          const dest=await PDFLib.PDFDocument.create();
          const indices=order.map(p=>p-1);
          const copied=await dest.copyPages(src, indices);
          copied.forEach(p=>dest.addPage(p));
          const bytes=await dest.save();
          selectedFiles.remove=new File([bytes],selectedFiles.remove.name,{type:'application/pdf'});
          pdfDoc=await window.pdfjsLib.getDocument({data:bytes}).promise;
          /* remap selected remove marks to new positions */
          const oldSel=new Set(parseSelectedFromInput(pagesToRemove.value, order.length||999));
          const newSel=[];
          order.forEach((oldPage,newIdx)=>{if(oldSel.has(oldPage)) newSel.push(newIdx+1)});
          pagesToRemove.value=formatPageRanges(newSel);
          await renderInputPreview(bytes,'remove');
          if(pagesToRemove.value.trim()) await generateRemovePagesPreview(bytes);
          else processedPdfBytes=null;
          showFileName('remove', selectedFiles.remove);
          updateButtonStates();
        }catch(err){showError('Reorder error: '+err.message,true)}
        finally{setProcessing(false)}
      }
    });
  }
}

function toggleRemovePage(pageNum, totalPages, item){
  let selected=parseSelectedFromInput(pagesToRemove.value, totalPages);
  const idx=selected.indexOf(pageNum);
  if(idx>=0){
    selected.splice(idx,1);
    item.classList.remove('marked-remove');
  }else{
    selected.push(pageNum);
    item.classList.add('marked-remove');
  }
  pagesToRemove.value=formatPageRanges(selected);
  /* trigger preview of result */
  pagesToRemove.dispatchEvent(new Event('input',{bubbles:true}));
}

function toggleSplitPage(pageNum, totalPages, item){
  let selected=parseSelectedFromInput(splitRanges.value, totalPages);
  const idx=selected.indexOf(pageNum);
  if(idx>=0){
    selected.splice(idx,1);
    item.classList.remove('marked-keep');
  }else{
    selected.push(pageNum);
    item.classList.add('marked-keep');
  }
  splitRanges.value=formatPageRanges(selected);
  updateButtonStates();
}

async function previewSelectedPages(ab,pages){
  previewDiv.innerHTML='';
  const doc=await window.pdfjsLib.getDocument({data:ab}).promise;
  for(const p of pages){
    const page=await doc.getPage(p);
    const vp=page.getViewport({scale:currentScale});
    const canvas=document.createElement('canvas');
    const ctx=canvas.getContext('2d');
    canvas.width=vp.width;canvas.height=vp.height;
    await page.render({canvasContext:ctx,viewport:vp}).promise;
    const item=document.createElement('div');
    item.className='preview-item';
    item.appendChild(canvas);
    const meta=document.createElement('div');
    meta.className='file-meta';
    meta.innerHTML=`<strong>Page ${p}</strong>`;
    item.appendChild(meta);
    previewDiv.appendChild(item);
  }
}

            
            
            
            
            
            
            
         
            
            
            
            
async function generateLayoutPreview(ab){
  window._cfLayoutRunId=(window._cfLayoutRunId||0)+1;
  const myRun=window._cfLayoutRunId;
  try{
    setProcessing(true);
    try{spinner.style.display='none';progressBar.style.display='none'}catch(e){}
    setProgressText('Building layout preview...');
    previewDiv.innerHTML='';
    previewDiv.classList.remove('hidden-preview');
    const bytes=await applyLayout(ab);
    if(myRun!==window._cfLayoutRunId) return;
    processedPdfBytes=bytes;
    previewDiv.innerHTML='';
    const doc=await window.pdfjsLib.getDocument({data:bytes}).promise;
    const num=doc.numPages;
    for(let i=1;i<=num;i++){
      if(myRun!==window._cfLayoutRunId) return;
      setProgressText('Rendering layout page '+i+' of '+num+'...');
      const page=await doc.getPage(i);
      const vp=page.getViewport({scale:.7});
      const canvas=document.createElement('canvas');
      const ctx=canvas.getContext('2d');
      canvas.width=vp.width;canvas.height=vp.height;
      await page.render({canvasContext:ctx,viewport:vp}).promise;
      if(myRun!==window._cfLayoutRunId) return;
      const item=document.createElement('div');
      item.className='preview-item';
      item.appendChild(canvas);
      const meta=document.createElement('div');
      meta.className='file-meta';
      meta.innerHTML=`<strong>Output page ${i}</strong>`;
      item.appendChild(meta);
      previewDiv.appendChild(item);
    }
  }catch(err){
    if(myRun===window._cfLayoutRunId) showError('Layout error: '+err.message,true);
  }
  finally{
    if(myRun===window._cfLayoutRunId){
      setProcessing(false);
      try{spinner.style.display='none'}catch(e){}
      updateButtonStates();
    }
  }
}
            
            
            
           
            
            
            
            
            
            
            
            

async function generateRemovePagesPreview(ab){
  try{
    const pages=parsePageNumbers(pagesToRemove.value.trim(),pdfDoc.numPages);
    if(!pages||!pages.length){processedPdfBytes=null;updateButtonStates();return}
    if(pdfDoc&&pages.length>=pdfDoc.numPages){showError('Cannot remove all pages.',true);processedPdfBytes=null;updateButtonStates();return}
    const bytes=await removePages(pages,ab);
    processedPdfBytes=bytes;
    updateButtonStates();
  }catch(err){showError('Remove error: '+err.message,true);processedPdfBytes=null;updateButtonStates()}
}

function isMostlyGrayscale(imageData){
  const px=imageData.data;let gray=0;const total=px.length/4;
  for(let i=0;i<px.length;i+=4){const r=px[i],g=px[i+1],b=px[i+2];if(Math.abs(r-g)<12&&Math.abs(g-b)<12&&Math.abs(r-b)<12)gray++}
  return gray/total>.8;
}

    showError('Download complete!',false);
  };
  processConverterBtn.onclick=async()=>{
    try{
      processConverterBtn.disabled=true;processConverterBtn.textContent='Processing...';
      if(conversionType.value==='pdf-to-jpg'){
        const zip=new JSZip();
        for(let i=0;i<selectedFiles.converter.length;i++){
          const file=selectedFiles.converter[i];
          const folder=zip.folder(file.name.replace(/\.pdf$/i,'')||`file_${i+1}`);
          const ab=await file.arrayBuffer();
          const imgs=await convertPDFToJPG(ab);
          imgs.forEach((img,idx)=>folder.file(`page_${idx+1}.jpg`,img.split(',')[1],{base64:true}));
        }
      
        
        
        
        
        
              const blob=await zip.generateAsync({type:'blob',compression:'DEFLATE'});
        if(selectedFiles.converter.length===1){
          const names=Object.keys(zip.files).filter(n=>!zip.files[n].dir);
          if(names.length===1){
            
            
                      downloadBlob(new Blob([await zip.file(names[0]).async('uint8array')],{type:'image/jpeg'}),
              (selectedFiles.converter[0].name||'page').replace(/\.pdf$/i,'')+'.jpg');
            
            
            
          }else downloadBlob(blob,(selectedFiles.converter[0].name||'PDF').replace(/\.pdf$/i,'')+'_JPG.zip');
        }else downloadBlob(blob,'ComputerFy_PDFs_to_JPG.zip');
        
        
        
        
        
        
        
        
        showError('All PDFs converted & zipped!',false);
      }else if(processedImages.length){
        const mergeAll=document.getElementById('mergeImagesCheck')?.checked!==false;
        if(mergeAll){
          const bytes=await convertJPGToPDF(processedImages);
          downloadPDF(bytes,'ComputerFy_JPG_to_PDF.pdf');
          showError('PDF downloaded successfully!',false);
        }else{
          /* each image → separate PDF in ZIP */
          const zip=new JSZip();
          for(let i=0;i<processedImages.length;i++){
            const single=await convertJPGToPDF([processedImages[i]]);
            const name=(selectedFiles.converter[i]?.name||`image_${i+1}`).replace(/\.[^.]+$/,'')+'.pdf';
            zip.file(name, single);
          }
          const blob=await zip.generateAsync({type:'blob',compression:'DEFLATE'});
          downloadBlob(blob,'ComputerFy_Images_to_PDFs.zip');
          showError('Separate PDFs zipped successfully!',false);
        }
      }
    }catch(err){showError('Conversion error: '+err.message,true)}
    
    
     finally{processConverterBtn.disabled=false;processConverterBtn.innerHTML='<i class="fa-solid fa-download"></i> Download'}
    
   // finally{processConverterBtn.disabled=false;processConverterBtn.innerHTML='<i class="fa-solid fa-download"></i> Download Result'}
  };
  
  processMergeSplitBtn.onclick=async()=>{
    try{
      //this belew line responsible for progressbar and spinner
      //setProcessing(true);
      
      
      if(mergeSplitType.value==='merge'&&selectedFiles.mergeSplit.length>1){
        const buffers=await Promise.all(selectedFiles.mergeSplit.map(f=>f.arrayBuffer()));
        const bytes=await mergePDFs(buffers);
        downloadPDF(bytes,'ComputerFy_merged_pdf.pdf');
        showError('Merged PDF downloaded!',false);
      }else if(mergeSplitType.value==='split'&&selectedFiles.mergeSplit.length===1){
        const ab=await selectedFiles.mergeSplit[0].arrayBuffer();
        let pages=null;
        if(previewDiv._splitOrder&&previewDiv.querySelectorAll('.preview-item.marked-keep').length){
          pages=Array.from(previewDiv.querySelectorAll('.preview-item.marked-keep')).map(el=>parseInt(el.dataset.page,10));
        }else{
          const ranges=splitRanges.value.trim();
          pages=ranges?parsePageNumbers(ranges,null):null;
        }
        if(!pages||!pages.length){showError('Select at least one page.',true);setProcessing(false);return}
        const mergeOne=document.getElementById('splitMergeCheck')?.checked===true;
        if(mergeOne){
          /* single PDF with selected pages only */
          const src=await PDFLib.PDFDocument.load(ab);
          const dest=await PDFLib.PDFDocument.create();
          const copied=await dest.copyPages(src, pages.map(p=>p-1));
          copied.forEach(p=>dest.addPage(p));
          const bytes=await dest.save();
          downloadPDF(bytes,'ComputerFy_extracted_pages.pdf');
          showError('Merged PDF downloaded!',false);
        }else{
          const parts=await splitPDF(ab,pages);
          if(parts.length===1){
            downloadPDF(parts[0],'ComputerFy_page_'+pages[0]+'.pdf');
          }else{
            const zip=new JSZip();
            parts.forEach((b,i)=>zip.file('page_'+pages[i]+'.pdf',b));
            const blob=await zip.generateAsync({type:'blob'});
            downloadBlob(blob,'ComputerFy_split_pages.zip');
          }
          showError('Pages downloaded!',false);
        }
      }
    }catch(err){showError('Merge/Split error: '+err.message,true)}
    finally{setProcessing(false)}
  };


  /* ===== EXTRACT ===== */
  const dropAreaExtract=document.getElementById('pdf-tool-dropAreaExtract');
  const processExtractBtn=document.getElementById('pdf-tool-processExtractBtn');
  const clearExtractBtn=document.getElementById('pdf-tool-resetExtractBtn');
  const extractRanges=document.getElementById('pdf-tool-extractRanges');
  if(dropAreaExtract){
    setupDropArea(dropAreaExtract,'extract','application/pdf',false);
  }
  // handle extract in handleFiles - patch via extending
  const _origHandle=handleFiles;
  window.handleFiles=handleFiles=async function(areaId,files,replace){
    if(areaId==='extract'){
      const pdfs=files.filter(f=>f.type==='application/pdf');
      if(!pdfs.length){showError('Please upload a PDF file.',true);return}
      selectedFiles.extract=pdfs[0];
      showFileName('extract',pdfs[0]);
      const ab=await pdfs[0].arrayBuffer();
      pdfDoc=await window.pdfjsLib.getDocument({data:ab}).promise;
      await renderInputPreview(ab,'extract');
      updateButtonStates();
      return;
    }
    /* security multi-file handled in main handleFiles */
    return _origHandle(areaId,files,replace);
  };
  // re-bind extract drop after override - setupDropArea already bound to old handleFiles
  // So re-setup extract and security
  if(dropAreaExtract){
    const btn=dropAreaExtract.querySelector('.small-btn');
    if(btn){
      const nb=btn.cloneNode(true);
      btn.parentNode.replaceChild(nb,btn);
      nb.addEventListener('click',e=>{
        e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();
        createFileInput('application/pdf',false,files=>handleFiles('extract',files,true));
      });
    }
    dropAreaExtract.addEventListener('drop',async e=>{
      e.preventDefault();dropAreaExtract.classList.remove('drag-over');
      await handleFiles('extract',Array.from(e.dataTransfer.files),true);
    });
  }
  const dropAreaSecurity=document.getElementById('pdf-tool-dropAreaSecurity');
  if(dropAreaSecurity){
    setupDropArea(dropAreaSecurity,'security','application/pdf',true);
    const btn=dropAreaSecurity.querySelector('.small-btn');
    if(btn){
      const nb=btn.cloneNode(true);
      btn.parentNode.replaceChild(nb,btn);
      nb.addEventListener('click',e=>{
        e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();
        createFileInput('application/pdf',true,files=>handleFiles('security',files,true));
      });
    }
    dropAreaSecurity.addEventListener('dragover',e=>{e.preventDefault();dropAreaSecurity.classList.add('drag-over')});
    dropAreaSecurity.addEventListener('dragleave',()=>dropAreaSecurity.classList.remove('drag-over'));
    dropAreaSecurity.addEventListener('drop',async e=>{
      e.preventDefault();dropAreaSecurity.classList.remove('drag-over');
      await handleFiles('security',Array.from(e.dataTransfer.files),true);
    });
  }
  const addMoreSec=document.getElementById('addMoreSecurityBtn');
  if(addMoreSec){
    addMoreSec.addEventListener('click',e=>{
      e.stopPropagation();
      createFileInput('application/pdf',true,files=>handleFiles('security',files,false));
    });
  }

  /* render extract mode like split */
  const _origRender=renderInputPreview;
  renderInputPreview=async function(ab, mode){
    if(mode==='extract'){
      if(!pdfDoc)return;
      previewDiv.innerHTML='';
      previewDiv.classList.remove('hidden-preview');
      const doc=await window.pdfjsLib.getDocument({data:ab}).promise;
      const totalPages=doc.numPages;
      let selectedSet=new Set(parseSelectedFromInput(extractRanges?.value||'', totalPages));
      for(let i=1;i<=totalPages;i++){
        setProgressText('Loading page '+i+'/'+totalPages+'...');
        const page=await doc.getPage(i);
        const vp=page.getViewport({scale:0.7});
        const canvas=document.createElement('canvas');
        const ctx=canvas.getContext('2d');
        canvas.width=vp.width;canvas.height=vp.height;
        await page.render({canvasContext:ctx,viewport:vp}).promise;
        const item=document.createElement('div');
        item.className='preview-item page-selectable';
        item.dataset.page=i;
        item.appendChild(canvas);
        const tick=document.createElement('div');
        tick.className='page-tick';
        tick.innerHTML='<i class="fa-solid fa-check"></i>';
        item.appendChild(tick);
        if(selectedSet.has(i)) item.classList.add('marked-keep');
        item.addEventListener('click',e=>{
          e.preventDefault();
          let selected=parseSelectedFromInput(extractRanges.value, totalPages);
          const idx=selected.indexOf(i);
          if(idx>=0){selected.splice(idx,1);item.classList.remove('marked-keep')}
          else{selected.push(i);item.classList.add('marked-keep')}
          extractRanges.value=formatPageRanges(selected);
          updateButtonStates();
        });
        const meta=document.createElement('div');
        meta.className='file-meta';
        meta.innerHTML=`<strong>Page ${i}</strong>`;
        item.appendChild(meta);
        previewDiv.appendChild(item);
      }
      setProgressText('');
      updateButtonStates();
      return;
    }
    return _origRender(ab, mode);
  };

  if(extractRanges){
    extractRanges.addEventListener('input',debounce(()=>{
      updateButtonStates();
      if(!pdfDoc||!selectedFiles.extract)return;
      const selected=new Set(extractRanges.value.trim()?parseSelectedFromInput(extractRanges.value,pdfDoc.numPages):[]);
      previewDiv.querySelectorAll('.preview-item.page-selectable').forEach(item=>{
        item.classList.toggle('marked-keep', selected.has(parseInt(item.dataset.page,10)));
      });
    },250));
  }

  if(processExtractBtn){
    processExtractBtn.onclick=async()=>{
      if(!selectedFiles.extract||!extractRanges.value.trim())return;
      try{
        setProcessing(true);
        setProgressText('Extracting pages...');
        const ab=await selectedFiles.extract.arrayBuffer();
        const pages=parsePageNumbers(extractRanges.value.trim(),null);
        if(!pages||!pages.length){showError('Select at least one page.',true);setProcessing(false);return}
        const parts=await splitPDF(ab, pages);
        if(parts.length===1){
          downloadPDF(parts[0],'ComputerFy_extracted_page.pdf');
        }else{
          const zip=new JSZip();
          parts.forEach((b,i)=>zip.file(`extracted_page_${pages[i]}.pdf`,b));
          const blob=await zip.generateAsync({type:'blob'});
          downloadBlob(blob,'ComputerFy_extracted_pages.zip');
        }
        showError('Pages extracted successfully!',false);
      }catch(err){showError('Extract error: '+err.message,true)}
      finally{setProcessing(false)}
    };
  }
  if(clearExtractBtn){
    clearExtractBtn.onclick=()=>{
      selectedFiles.extract=null;pdfDoc=null;
      if(extractRanges)extractRanges.value='';
      document.getElementById('file-name-extract').innerHTML='';
      previewDiv.innerHTML='';previewDiv.classList.add('hidden-preview');
      updateButtonStates();
    };
  }

  /* ===== SECURITY / PASSWORD ===== */
  const securityType=document.getElementById('pdf-tool-securityType');
  const passwordInput=document.getElementById('pdf-tool-passwordInput');
  const passwordConfirm=document.getElementById('pdf-tool-passwordConfirm');
  const ownerPasswordInput=document.getElementById('pdf-tool-ownerPassword');
  const processSecurityBtn=document.getElementById('pdf-tool-processSecurityBtn');
  const clearSecurityBtn=document.getElementById('pdf-tool-resetSecurityBtn');
  const confirmPassGroup=document.getElementById('confirmPassGroup');
  const ownerPassGroup=document.getElementById('ownerPassGroup');
  const permissionsGroup=document.getElementById('permissionsGroup');
  const samePassCheck=document.getElementById('samePasswordAllCheck');
  const samePassWrap=document.getElementById('samePassToggleWrap');
  const securityGlobalPassSection=document.getElementById('securityGlobalPassSection');
  const securityFileListEl=document.getElementById('securityFileList');

  function updateSecListSummary(){
    if(!securityFileListEl) return;
    const files=Array.isArray(selectedFiles.security)?selectedFiles.security:[];
    let sum=securityFileListEl.querySelector('.sec-list-summary');
    if(!files.length){
      if(sum) sum.remove();
      return;
    }
    let total=0;
    files.forEach(f=>{ total+=(f.size||0); });
    const sizeStr=typeof formatSize==='function'?formatSize(total):'';
   
    
   
    
      const txt=(files.length>1?files.length+'/'+files.length:files.length)+' file'+(files.length>1?'s':'')+' | Total '+sizeStr;
    if(!sum){
      sum=document.createElement('div');
      sum.className='sec-list-summary';
      securityFileListEl.insertBefore(sum, securityFileListEl.firstChild);
    }
    sum.innerHTML='<i class="fa-solid fa-layer-group"></i> '+txt;
    sum.dataset.cfDone=String(files.length);
    sum.dataset.cfTotal=String(files.length);
  }
  
  
  
  

  window.renderSecurityFileList=async function renderSecurityFileList(opts){
    if(!securityFileListEl) return;
    opts=opts||{};
    const files=Array.isArray(selectedFiles.security)?selectedFiles.security:[];
    if(!files.length){
      securityFileListEl.innerHTML='';
      securityFileListEl.classList.remove('has-files');
      syncSecurityUI();
      return;
    }
    securityFileListEl.classList.add('has-files');
    updateSecListSummary();
    const isUnlock=securityType&&securityType.value==='unlock';
    const sameAll=samePassCheck&&samePassCheck.checked;
    const showPerFilePass=isUnlock && !sameAll && files.length>1;

    async function ensurePages(file){
      if(typeof file._secPages==='number') return file._secPages;
      try{
        const ab=await file.arrayBuffer();
        const doc=await window.pdfjsLib.getDocument({data:ab}).promise;
        file._secPages=doc.numPages||0;
        try{doc.destroy()}catch(_){}
      }catch(_){ file._secPages=0; }
      return file._secPages;
    }

    function bindRowControls(row){
      const btn=row.querySelector('.sec-file-pass .pwd-eye');
      if(btn){
        btn.onclick=e=>{
          e.preventDefault();
          const idx=btn.getAttribute('data-idx');
          const input=row.querySelector('.sec-per-file-pass');
          if(!input) return;
          const show=input.type==='password';
          input.type=show?'text':'password';
          const ic=btn.querySelector('i');
          if(ic){ ic.classList.toggle('fa-eye',!show); ic.classList.toggle('fa-eye-slash',show); }
        };
      }
      const inp=row.querySelector('.sec-per-file-pass');
      if(inp) inp.addEventListener('input',updateSecurityBtn);
    }

    function makeRow(file, i){
      const row=document.createElement('div');
      row.className='sec-file-row';
      row.dataset.index=String(i);

      const icon=document.createElement('div');
      icon.className='sec-file-icon';
      icon.innerHTML='<i class="fa-solid fa-file-pdf"></i>';

      const info=document.createElement('div');
      info.className='sec-file-info';
      const nameEl=document.createElement('div');
      nameEl.className='sec-file-name';
      nameEl.textContent=file.name||('PDF '+(i+1));
      const meta=document.createElement('div');
      meta.className='sec-file-meta';
      const sizeStr=typeof formatSize==='function'?formatSize(file.size||0):'';
      const p=file._secPages;
      /* locked PDFs often report 0 pages — hide page count when unknown/0 */
      if(typeof p==='number' && p>0){
        meta.textContent=sizeStr+' | '+p+' page'+(p>1?'s':'');
      }else{
        meta.textContent=sizeStr;
      }
      info.appendChild(nameEl);
      info.appendChild(meta);

      const passWrap=document.createElement('div');
      passWrap.className='sec-file-pass'+(showPerFilePass?' show':'');
      passWrap.innerHTML='<div class="pwd-wrap"><input type="password" class="sec-per-file-pass" data-idx="'+i+'" placeholder="Password for this file" autocomplete="off"><button type="button" class="pwd-eye" data-idx="'+i+'" title="Show/Hide password" aria-label="Show password"><i class="fa-solid fa-eye"></i></button></div>';
      info.appendChild(passWrap);

      const rm=document.createElement('button');
      rm.type='button';
      rm.className='sec-file-remove';
      rm.title='Remove';
      rm.innerHTML='<i class="fa-solid fa-xmark"></i>';
      rm.onclick=()=>{
        const idx=parseInt(row.dataset.index,10);
        if(isNaN(idx)) return;
        selectedFiles.security.splice(idx,1);
        row.remove();
        /* reindex remaining rows without rebuild */
        const rows=securityFileListEl.querySelectorAll('.sec-file-row');
        rows.forEach((r,j)=>{
          r.dataset.index=String(j);
          const pi=r.querySelector('.sec-per-file-pass');
          if(pi) pi.setAttribute('data-idx',String(j));
          const eye=r.querySelector('.pwd-eye');
          if(eye) eye.setAttribute('data-idx',String(j));
        });
        if(!selectedFiles.security.length){
          securityFileListEl.innerHTML='';
          securityFileListEl.classList.remove('has-files');
        }else{
          updateSecListSummary();
        }
               syncSecurityUI();
        updateButtonStates();
        if(typeof updateSecurityBtn==='function') updateSecurityBtn();
      };

      row.appendChild(icon);
      row.appendChild(info);
      row.appendChild(rm);
      bindRowControls(row);
      return {row, meta, file};
    }

    /* Incremental append only */
    if(opts.appendFrom!=null){
      const from=opts.appendFrom|0;
      for(let i=from;i<files.length;i++){
        const file=files[i];
        await ensurePages(file);
        const {row}=makeRow(file, i);
        securityFileListEl.appendChild(row);
      }
      /* update show/hide per-file pass on all rows */
      securityFileListEl.querySelectorAll('.sec-file-pass').forEach(el=>{
        el.classList.toggle('show', !!showPerFilePass);
      });
      updateSecListSummary();
      syncSecurityUI();
      return;
    }

    /* Full rebuild only when needed (mode switch / first load) */
    securityFileListEl.innerHTML='';
    for(let i=0;i<files.length;i++){
      const file=files[i];
      await ensurePages(file);
      const {row}=makeRow(file, i);
      securityFileListEl.appendChild(row);
    }
    syncSecurityUI();
  };

  function syncSecurityUI(){
    const isProtect=securityType && securityType.value==='protect';
    const isUnlock=!isProtect;
    const files=Array.isArray(selectedFiles.security)?selectedFiles.security:[];
    const multi=files.length>1;

    if(confirmPassGroup) confirmPassGroup.style.display=isProtect?'block':'none';
    const advWrap=document.getElementById('advancedSecurityToggleWrap');
    const advPanel=document.getElementById('advancedSecurityPanel');
    const chevron=document.getElementById('advancedSecurityChevron');
    if(advWrap) advWrap.style.display=isProtect?'block':'none';
    if(!isProtect){
      if(advPanel){
        advPanel.style.display='none';
        advPanel.dataset.open='0';
      }
      if(chevron) chevron.style.transform='';
    }

    if(samePassWrap){
      samePassWrap.classList.toggle('visible', isUnlock && multi);
    }
    const sameAll=isUnlock && multi && samePassCheck && samePassCheck.checked;
    const perFile=isUnlock && multi && samePassCheck && !samePassCheck.checked;

    if(securityGlobalPassSection){
      securityGlobalPassSection.style.display=perFile?'none':'block';
    }
    const passLabel=document.getElementById('securityPassLabel');
    if(passLabel){
      passLabel.innerHTML=isProtect
        ? 'User Password <span style="font-weight:500;color:#64748b;font-size:11px">(required to open)</span>'
        : 'Password <span style="font-weight:500;color:#64748b;font-size:11px">(to unlock)</span>';
    }

    if(securityFileListEl){
      securityFileListEl.querySelectorAll('.sec-file-pass').forEach(el=>{
        el.classList.toggle('show', !!perFile);
      });
    }

    /* Button label: Lock / Unlock */
    if(processSecurityBtn){
      const icon=isProtect?'fa-lock':'fa-lock-open';
      const label=isProtect?'Lock':'Unlock';
      processSecurityBtn.innerHTML='<i class="fa-solid '+icon+'"></i> '+label;
    }
    updateSecListSummary();
  }

  if(securityType){
    securityType.addEventListener('change',()=>{
      selectedFiles.security=[];
      if(passwordInput) passwordInput.value='';
      if(passwordConfirm) passwordConfirm.value='';
      if(ownerPasswordInput) ownerPasswordInput.value='';
      if(samePassCheck) samePassCheck.checked=false;
      const fn=document.getElementById('file-name-security');
      if(fn) fn.innerHTML='';
      if(securityFileListEl){ securityFileListEl.innerHTML=''; securityFileListEl.classList.remove('has-files'); }
      syncSecurityUI();
      if(errorDiv) errorDiv.style.display='none';
      updateButtonStates();
    });
    syncSecurityUI();
  }

  if(samePassCheck){
    samePassCheck.addEventListener('change',()=>{
      syncSecurityUI();
      updateSecurityBtn();
    });
  }

  function updateSecurityBtn(){
    if(!processSecurityBtn)return;
    const hasFile=Array.isArray(selectedFiles.security) && selectedFiles.security.length>0;
    const isUnlock=securityType&&securityType.value==='unlock';
    const multi=hasFile && selectedFiles.security.length>1;
    const sameAll=samePassCheck&&samePassCheck.checked;
    let hasPass=false;
    if(isUnlock && multi && !sameAll){
      const inputs=securityFileListEl?securityFileListEl.querySelectorAll('.sec-per-file-pass'):[];
      hasPass=inputs.length>0 && Array.from(inputs).every(inp=>inp.value&&inp.value.length>0);
    }else{
      hasPass=passwordInput&&passwordInput.value.length>0;
    }
    processSecurityBtn.disabled=!hasFile||!hasPass;
  }
  if(passwordInput) passwordInput.addEventListener('input',updateSecurityBtn);
  if(passwordConfirm) passwordConfirm.addEventListener('input',updateSecurityBtn);
  if(ownerPasswordInput) ownerPasswordInput.addEventListener('input',updateSecurityBtn);

  /* Advanced security collapse/expand */
  const advToggleBtn=document.getElementById('advancedSecurityToggle');
  if(advToggleBtn){
    advToggleBtn.addEventListener('click',()=>{
      const panel=document.getElementById('advancedSecurityPanel');
      const chevron=document.getElementById('advancedSecurityChevron');
      if(!panel) return;
      const open=panel.dataset.open==='1';
      panel.style.display=open?'none':'block';
      panel.dataset.open=open?'0':'1';
      if(chevron) chevron.style.transform=open?'':'rotate(90deg)';
    });
  }

  /* qpdf WebAssembly – AES-256 lock/unlock */
  let _qpdfMod=null;
  async function getQpdf(){
    if(_qpdfMod) return _qpdfMod;
    setProgressText('Loading qpdf engine...');
    const createModule=(await import('https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/@neslinesli93/qpdf-wasm@0.3.0/+esm')).default;
    _qpdfMod=await createModule({
      locateFile:()=>'https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/@neslinesli93/qpdf-wasm@0.3.0/dist/qpdf.wasm',
      noInitialRun:true
    });
    try{_qpdfMod.FS.mkdir('/work')}catch(e){}
    return _qpdfMod;
  }
  async function qpdfEncrypt(uint8, userPass, ownerPass, printPerm, modifyPerm, extractPerm){
    const qpdf=await getQpdf();
    qpdf.FS.writeFile('/work/input.pdf', uint8);
    try{qpdf.FS.unlink('/work/output.pdf')}catch(e){}
    const owner=ownerPass||userPass;
    const args=[
      '--encrypt', userPass, owner, '256',
      '--print='+printPerm,
      '--modify='+modifyPerm,
      '--extract='+extractPerm,
      '--','/work/input.pdf','/work/output.pdf'
    ];
    qpdf.callMain(args);
    return qpdf.FS.readFile('/work/output.pdf');
  }
  async function qpdfDecrypt(uint8, password){
    const qpdf=await getQpdf();
    qpdf.FS.writeFile('/work/input.pdf', uint8);
    try{qpdf.FS.unlink('/work/output.pdf')}catch(e){}
    qpdf.callMain([
      '--password='+password,
      '--decrypt',
      '/work/input.pdf','/work/output.pdf'
    ]);
    return qpdf.FS.readFile('/work/output.pdf');
  }

  if(processSecurityBtn){
    processSecurityBtn.onclick=async()=>{
      if(!Array.isArray(selectedFiles.security)||!selectedFiles.security.length)return;
      try{
        setProcessing(true);
        
        
        // below code block responsible for removing progress bar
           try{
          if(typeof progressBar!=='undefined'&&progressBar) progressBar.style.display='none';
          if(typeof spinner!=='undefined'&&spinner) spinner.style.display='none';
        }catch(e){}     
        
        
        
        
        const mode=securityType.value;
        const isUnlock=mode==='unlock';
        const multi=selectedFiles.security.length>1;
        const sameAll=!isUnlock || !multi || (samePassCheck&&samePassCheck.checked);

        if(mode==='protect'){
          const pass=passwordInput.value;
          if(!pass){showError('Enter user password.',true);setProcessing(false);return;}
          if(passwordConfirm&&pass!==passwordConfirm.value){
            showError('User passwords do not match.',true);setProcessing(false);return;
          }
        }

        const ownerPass=(ownerPasswordInput&&ownerPasswordInput.value)?ownerPasswordInput.value:(passwordInput.value||'');
        const printPerm=document.getElementById('pdf-perm-print')?.value||'full';
        const modifyPerm=document.getElementById('pdf-perm-modify')?.value||'none';
        const extractPerm=document.getElementById('pdf-perm-extract')?.value||'n';
        const results=[];

        for(let i=0;i<selectedFiles.security.length;i++){
          const file=selectedFiles.security[i];
          setProgressText((mode==='protect'?'Encrypting':'Unlocking')+' '+(i+1)+' of '+selectedFiles.security.length+'...');
          const ab=await file.arrayBuffer();
          const uint8=new Uint8Array(ab);
          let out;
          if(mode==='protect'){
            const pass=passwordInput.value;
            out=await qpdfEncrypt(uint8, pass, ownerPass, printPerm, modifyPerm, extractPerm);
            results.push({name:'protected_'+file.name, bytes:out});
          }else{
            let pass='';
            if(sameAll){
              pass=passwordInput.value;
            }else{
              const inp=securityFileListEl&&securityFileListEl.querySelector('.sec-per-file-pass[data-idx="'+i+'"]');
              pass=inp?inp.value:'';
            }
            if(!pass){
              showError('Password missing for "'+file.name+'".',true);
              continue;
            }
            const locked=await detectPdfEncrypted(ab);
            if(!locked){
              showError('"'+file.name+'" is already unlocked — skipped.',true);
              continue;
            }
            try{
              out=await qpdfDecrypt(uint8, pass);
              results.push({name:'unlocked_'+file.name, bytes:out});
            }catch(uerr){
              showError('Could not unlock "'+file.name+'". Wrong password?',true);
            }
          }
        }
        if(!results.length){
          showError('No files processed.',true);
          return;
        }
        if(results.length===1){
          downloadPDF(results[0].bytes, results[0].name);
        }else{
          const zip=new JSZip();
          results.forEach(r=>zip.file(r.name, r.bytes));
          const blob=await zip.generateAsync({type:'blob'});
          downloadBlob(blob, mode==='protect'?'ComputerFy_protected_pdfs.zip':'ComputerFy_unlocked_pdfs.zip');
        }
        showError(results.length+' file(s) processed successfully!',false);
      }catch(err){showError('Security error: '+err.message,true)}
      finally{setProcessing(false);setProgressText('')}
    };
  }
  if(clearSecurityBtn){
    clearSecurityBtn.onclick=()=>{
      if(!confirm('Reset this tab?\n\nUploaded files and password fields will be cleared.')) return;
      selectedFiles.security=[];
      if(passwordInput)passwordInput.value='';
      if(passwordConfirm)passwordConfirm.value='';
      if(ownerPasswordInput)ownerPasswordInput.value='';
      if(samePassCheck) samePassCheck.checked=false;
      const fn=document.getElementById('file-name-security');
      if(fn) fn.innerHTML='';
      if(securityFileListEl){ securityFileListEl.innerHTML=''; securityFileListEl.classList.remove('has-files'); }
      clearDirty('security');
      syncSecurityUI();
      try{saveSettings()}catch(e){}
      updateButtonStates();
    };
  }

  /* ===== ROTATE / REORDER (Remove tab) ===== */
  const rotateLeftBtn=document.getElementById('rotateLeftBtn');
  const rotateRightBtn=document.getElementById('rotateRightBtn');
  const reorderPagesBtn=document.getElementById('reorderPagesBtn');
  async function applyRotationAndDownload(degrees){
    if(!selectedFiles.remove||!pdfDoc){showError('Upload a PDF first.',true);return}
    try{
      setProcessing(true);
      setProgressText('Rotating pages...');
      const ab=await selectedFiles.remove.arrayBuffer();
      const src=await PDFLib.PDFDocument.load(ab);
      const marked=parseSelectedFromInput(pagesToRemove.value, pdfDoc.numPages);
      const pagesToRotate=marked.length?marked:null; /* if none marked, rotate all */
      const total=src.getPageCount();
      for(let i=0;i<total;i++){
        const pageNum=i+1;
        if(pagesToRotate&&!pagesToRotate.includes(pageNum)) continue;
        const page=src.getPage(i);
        const cur=page.getRotation().angle||0;
        page.setRotation(PDFLib.degrees((cur+degrees+360)%360));
      }
      const bytes=await src.save();
      processedPdfBytes=bytes;
      downloadPDF(bytes,'ComputerFy_rotated_pdf.pdf');
      showError('Pages rotated & downloaded!',false);
      /* refresh preview */
      pdfDoc=await window.pdfjsLib.getDocument({data:bytes}).promise;
      selectedFiles.remove=new File([bytes],selectedFiles.remove.name,{type:'application/pdf'});
      await renderInputPreview(bytes,'remove');
    }catch(err){showError('Rotate error: '+err.message,true)}
    finally{setProcessing(false)}
  }
  if(rotateLeftBtn) rotateLeftBtn.onclick=()=>applyRotationAndDownload(-90);
  if(rotateRightBtn) rotateRightBtn.onclick=()=>applyRotationAndDownload(90);
  if(reorderPagesBtn){
    reorderPagesBtn.onclick=()=>{
      if(!selectedFiles.remove){showError('Upload a PDF first.',true);return}
      reorderMode=!reorderMode;
      reorderPagesBtn.style.background=reorderMode?'#dbeafe':'';
      if(reorderMode&&typeof Sortable!=='undefined'){
        if(previewDiv._pageSortable) previewDiv._pageSortable.destroy();
        previewDiv._pageSortable=Sortable.create(previewDiv,{
          animation:150,draggable:'.preview-item',
          onEnd:async()=>{
            const order=Array.from(previewDiv.querySelectorAll('.preview-item')).map(el=>parseInt(el.dataset.page,10));
            try{
              setProcessing(true);
              setProgressText('Reordering pages...');
              const ab=await selectedFiles.remove.arrayBuffer();
              const src=await PDFLib.PDFDocument.load(ab);
              const dest=await PDFLib.PDFDocument.create();
              const indices=order.map(p=>p-1);
              const copied=await dest.copyPages(src, indices);
              copied.forEach(p=>dest.addPage(p));
              const bytes=await dest.save();
              processedPdfBytes=bytes;
              selectedFiles.remove=new File([bytes],selectedFiles.remove.name,{type:'application/pdf'});
              pdfDoc=await window.pdfjsLib.getDocument({data:bytes}).promise;
              pagesToRemove.value='';
              await renderInputPreview(bytes,'remove');
              showError('Pages reordered! Download when ready.',false);
            }catch(err){showError('Reorder error: '+err.message,true)}
            finally{setProcessing(false)}
          }
        });
        showError('Drag pages to reorder, then they will be applied.',false);
      }else if(previewDiv._pageSortable){
        previewDiv._pageSortable.destroy();
        previewDiv._pageSortable=null;
      }
    };
  }

  };

    }
    updateSecurityBtn();
  };

  const SETTINGS_KEY='pdfToolSettings_v1';
  function collectSettings(){
    return {
