      readingDirection: document.getElementById('pdf-tool-readingDirection')?.value,
      orientation: document.getElementById('pdf-tool-orientation')?.value,
      pageSize: document.getElementById('pdf-tool-pageSize')?.value,
      outerMargin: document.getElementById('pdf-tool-outerMargin')?.value,
      innerMargin: document.getElementById('pdf-tool-innerMargin')?.value,
      layoutBorder: document.getElementById('pdf-tool-layoutBorder')?.checked,
      customWidth: document.getElementById('pdf-tool-customWidth')?.value,
      customHeight: document.getElementById('pdf-tool-customHeight')?.value,
      sharedUnit: document.getElementById('pdf-tool-sharedUnit')?.value,
      qualityPreset: document.getElementById('qualityPreset')?.value,
      targetSize: document.getElementById('targetSizeInput')?.value,
      conversionType: document.getElementById('pdf-tool-conversionType')?.value,
      
      
      converterQuality: document.getElementById('converterQuality')?.value,
      
      pdfToJpgQuality: document.getElementById('pdfToJpgQuality')?.value,
      
      
      mergeImages: document.getElementById('mergeImagesCheck')?.checked,
      mergeSplitType: document.getElementById('pdf-tool-mergeSplitType')?.value,
      splitMerge: document.getElementById('splitMergeCheck')?.checked,
      securityType: document.getElementById('pdf-tool-securityType')?.value,
      jpgOutputQuality: document.getElementById('jpgOutputQuality')?.value,
      jpgPageSize: document.getElementById('jpgPageSize')?.value,
      jpgOrientation: document.getElementById('jpgOrientation')?.value,
      jpgMargin: document.getElementById('jpgMargin')?.value,
      jpgCustomMargin: document.getElementById('jpgCustomMargin')?.value,
      jpgCustomW: document.getElementById('jpgCustomW')?.value,
      jpgCustomH: document.getElementById('jpgCustomH')?.value,
      theme: document.querySelector('.pdf-tool-container')?.classList.contains('dark-mode')?'dark':'light'
    };
  }
  function saveSettings(){
    try{localStorage.setItem(SETTINGS_KEY, JSON.stringify(collectSettings()))}catch(e){}
  }
  function loadSettings(){
    try{
      const raw=localStorage.getItem(SETTINGS_KEY);
      if(!raw)return;
      const s=JSON.parse(raw);
      const set=(id,val,isCheck)=>{
        const el=document.getElementById(id);
        if(!el||val===undefined||val===null)return;
        if(isCheck) el.checked=!!val;
        else el.value=val;
      };
      set('pdf-tool-pagesPerSheet',s.pagesPerSheet);
      set('pdf-tool-readingDirection',s.readingDirection);
      set('pdf-tool-orientation',s.orientation);
      set('pdf-tool-pageSize',s.pageSize);
      set('pdf-tool-outerMargin',s.outerMargin);
      set('pdf-tool-innerMargin',s.innerMargin);
      set('pdf-tool-layoutBorder',s.layoutBorder,true);
      set('pdf-tool-customWidth',s.customWidth);
      set('pdf-tool-customHeight',s.customHeight);
      set('pdf-tool-sharedUnit',s.sharedUnit);
      set('qualityPreset',s.qualityPreset);
      set('targetSizeInput',s.targetSize);
      set('pdf-tool-conversionType',s.conversionType);
      
      
      set('converterQuality',s.converterQuality);
      set('pdfToJpgQuality', s.pdfToJpgQuality);
      
      if(s.converterQuality!=null){
        const cv=document.getElementById('converterQualityVal');
        if(cv) cv.textContent=s.converterQuality+'%';
        document.querySelectorAll('.slider-marks span').forEach(sp=>{
          sp.classList.toggle('active', String(sp.dataset.q)===String(s.converterQuality));
        });
      }
      set('mergeImagesCheck',s.mergeImages,true);
      set('pdf-tool-mergeSplitType',s.mergeSplitType);
      set('splitMergeCheck',s.splitMerge,true);
      set('pdf-tool-securityType',s.securityType);
      set('jpgOutputQuality',s.jpgOutputQuality);
      set('jpgPageSize',s.jpgPageSize);
      set('jpgOrientation',s.jpgOrientation);
      set('jpgMargin',s.jpgMargin);
      set('jpgCustomMargin',s.jpgCustomMargin);
      set('jpgCustomW',s.jpgCustomW);
      set('jpgCustomH',s.jpgCustomH);
      /* show custom rows if needed */
      const crow=document.getElementById('jpgCustomSizeRow');
      if(crow) crow.style.display=(s.jpgPageSize==='custom')?'block':'none';
      const mrow=document.getElementById('jpgCustomMarginRow');
      if(mrow) mrow.style.display=(s.jpgMargin==='custom')?'block':'none';
      const jpgSet=document.getElementById('jpgToPdfSettings');
      if(jpgSet) jpgSet.style.display=(s.conversionType==='jpg-to-pdf'||!s.conversionType)?'block':'none';
      if(s.conversionType==='pdf-to-jpg'){ if(jpgSet) jpgSet.style.display='none'; }
      /* apply dependent UI */
      const csg=document.getElementById('pdf-tool-custom-size-group')||document.querySelector('.pdf-tool-custom-size-group');
      if(csg&&s.pageSize==='Custom') csg.classList.add('active');
      const tsg=document.getElementById('targetSizeGroup')||document.querySelector('.target-size-group');
      if(tsg) tsg.classList.toggle('active', s.qualityPreset==='target');
      if(s.mergeSplitType==='split'){
        const sr=document.getElementById('pdf-tool-splitRanges');
        if(sr) sr.classList.add('active');
        const tip=document.getElementById('tip-split');
        if(tip) tip.style.display='block';
      }
      const mergeGrp=document.getElementById('mergeImagesGroup');
      if(mergeGrp) mergeGrp.style.display=s.conversionType==='jpg-to-pdf'?'block':'none';
      const confirmPass=document.getElementById('confirmPassGroup');
      if(confirmPass) confirmPass.style.display=s.securityType==='protect'?'block':'none';
      const advW=document.getElementById('advancedSecurityToggleWrap');
      if(advW) advW.style.display=s.securityType==='protect'?'block':'none';
      const advP=document.getElementById('advancedSecurityPanel');
      if(advP && s.securityType!=='protect'){ advP.style.display='none'; advP.dataset.open='0'; }
      const advCh=document.getElementById('advancedSecurityChevron');
      if(advCh && s.securityType!=='protect') advCh.style.transform='';
      const apBtn=document.getElementById('pdf-tool-processSecurityBtn');
      if(apBtn){
        const ic=s.securityType==='unlock'?'fa-lock-open':'fa-lock';
        const lb=s.securityType==='unlock'?'Unlock':'Lock';
        apBtn.innerHTML='<i class="fa-solid '+ic+'"></i> '+lb;
      }
      if(s.conversionType==='pdf-to-jpg'){
        const btn=document.querySelector('#pdf-tool-dropAreaConverter .small-btn');
        if(btn) btn.innerHTML='<i class="fa-solid fa-folder-open"></i> Select PDF(s)';
      }
    }catch(e){console.warn('Settings load failed',e)}
  }

  /* Mark dirty when any setting/input changes on a tab */
  function bindDirty(ids, tab){
    ids.forEach(id=>{
      const el=document.getElementById(id);
      if(!el)return;
      el.addEventListener('change',()=>markDirty(tab));
      el.addEventListener('input',()=>markDirty(tab));
    });
  }
  bindDirty(['pdf-tool-pagesPerSheet','pdf-tool-readingDirection','pdf-tool-orientation','pdf-tool-pageSize','pdf-tool-outerMargin','pdf-tool-innerMargin','pdf-tool-layoutBorder','pdf-tool-customWidth','pdf-tool-customHeight','pdf-tool-sharedUnit'],'layout');
  bindDirty(['pdf-tool-pagesToRemove'],'remove');
  bindDirty(['qualityPreset','targetSizeInput'],'compress');

  
  
 bindDirty(['pdf-tool-conversionType','converterQuality','pdfToJpgQuality','mergeImagesCheck','jpgOutputQuality','jpgPageSize','jpgOrientation','jpgMargin','jpgCustomMargin','jpgCustomW','jpgCustomH'],'converter');

  /* Crop modal bindings (premium) */
  const cropModal=document.getElementById('cropModal');
  const cropModalClose=document.getElementById('cropModalClose');

  function applyCropZoom(prevZoom){
    const area=cropAreaEl;
    if(!area||!cropImgNatural.w)return;
    const areaW=area.clientWidth||480;
    const areaH=area.clientHeight||320;
    const baseScale=Math.min(areaW/cropImgNatural.w, areaH/cropImgNatural.h, 1);
    cropDisplayScale=baseScale*cropViewZoom;
    const dispW=cropImgNatural.w*cropDisplayScale;
    const dispH=cropImgNatural.h*cropDisplayScale;
    const cropImg=document.getElementById('cropImage');
    if(cropImg){
      const boxCx=cropBox.x+cropBox.w/2;
      const boxCy=cropBox.y+cropBox.h/2;
      const imgLeft=parseFloat(cropImg.style.left)||0;
      const imgTop=parseFloat(cropImg.style.top)||0;
      const prevScale=baseScale*prevZoom;
      const relX=(boxCx-imgLeft)/Math.max(1e-6,prevScale);
      const relY=(boxCy-imgTop)/Math.max(1e-6,prevScale);
      cropImg.style.width=dispW+'px';
      cropImg.style.height=dispH+'px';
      cropImg.style.left=(boxCx-relX*cropDisplayScale)+'px';
      cropImg.style.top=(boxCy-relY*cropDisplayScale)+'px';
    }
    updateCropRectUI();
  }

  function formatCropDegLabel(deg){
    let d=((deg%360)+360)%360;
    if(d>180) d-=360;
    if(d===0) return '0 deg';
    return (d>0?'+'+d:String(d))+' deg';
  }
  function toSliderDeg(deg){
    let d=((deg%360)+360)%360;
    if(d>180) d-=360;
    return d;
  }
  /* Professional live rotate: CSS only — crop box size/position stays fixed */
  function setCropRotationLive(signedDeg){
    let v=parseInt(signedDeg,10);
    if(isNaN(v)) v=0;
    v=Math.max(-180, Math.min(180, v));
    cropRotation=((v%360)+360)%360;
    if(cropRotateSlider) cropRotateSlider.value=v;
    const valEl=document.getElementById('cropRotateValue');
    if(valEl) valEl.textContent=formatCropDegLabel(v);
    const cropImg=document.getElementById('cropImage');
    if(cropImg){
      cropImg.style.transformOrigin='center center';
      cropImg.style.transform=(v===0)?'none':('rotate('+v+'deg)');
    }
    /* cropBox intentionally unchanged */
  }
  async function setCropRotation(deg){
    /* ±90 buttons / keyboard: still live CSS, no canvas reload, box stable */
    let signed=toSliderDeg(deg);
    setCropRotationLive(signed);
  }

  if(cropModalClose) cropModalClose.onclick=closeCropModal;
  if(applyCropBtn) applyCropBtn.onclick=()=>applyCropFromModal();
  if(resetCropBtn) resetCropBtn.onclick=async()=>{
    if(cropTargetIndex<0||!processedImages[cropTargetIndex])return;
    cropSessionOriginal=processedImages[cropTargetIndex];
    cropRotation=0;
    cropViewZoom=1;
    cropPresetRatio='free';
    if(cropRotateSlider){cropRotateSlider.value=0}
    const valEl=document.getElementById('cropRotateValue');
    if(valEl)valEl.textContent='0 deg';
    const lock=document.getElementById('lockRatio');
    
    
    
   // if(lock)lock.checked=true;
    
    if(lock)lock.checked=false;
    
    
    const presetSel=document.getElementById('cropPresetSelect');
    if(presetSel) presetSel.value='free';
    const rotated=await getRotatedCanvasFromDataUrl(cropSessionOriginal,0);
    cropImgNatural={w:rotated.width,h:rotated.height};
    const cropImg=document.getElementById('cropImage');
    if(cropImg){
      cropImg.style.transform='none';
      cropImg.src=rotated.toDataURL('image/jpeg',0.92);
      requestAnimationFrame(()=>requestAnimationFrame(()=>layoutCropImage(false)));
    }
  };
  if(cropRotateLeftBtn) cropRotateLeftBtn.onclick=()=>setCropRotationLive(toSliderDeg(cropRotation)-90);
  if(cropRotateRightBtn) cropRotateRightBtn.onclick=()=>setCropRotationLive(toSliderDeg(cropRotation)+90);
  if(cropRotateSlider){
    cropRotateSlider.setAttribute('title','Rotate image (Ctrl + Left / Right arrow)');
    cropRotateSlider.addEventListener('input',function(){
      setCropRotationLive(parseInt(this.value,10)||0);
    });
  }
  /* Custom size editor on resolution badge */
  const cropSizeBadge=document.getElementById('cropSizeBadge');
  const cropCustomSizeApply=document.getElementById('cropCustomSizeApply');
  const cropSizeEditor=document.getElementById('cropSizeEditor');
  if(cropSizeBadge){
    cropSizeBadge.addEventListener('click',openCropSizeEditor);
    cropSizeBadge.addEventListener('mousedown',e=>e.stopPropagation());
    cropSizeBadge.addEventListener('touchstart',e=>e.stopPropagation(),{passive:true});
  }
  if(cropCustomSizeApply){
    cropCustomSizeApply.addEventListener('click',function(e){
      e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation();
      applyCustomCropSize();
    });
  }
  if(cropSizeEditor){
    cropSizeEditor.addEventListener('mousedown',e=>e.stopPropagation());
    cropSizeEditor.addEventListener('touchstart',e=>e.stopPropagation(),{passive:true});
    cropSizeEditor.addEventListener('keydown',function(e){
      if(e.key==='Enter'){ e.preventDefault(); applyCustomCropSize(); }
      if(e.key==='Escape'){ e.preventDefault(); closeCropSizeEditor(); }
    });
  }
  document.addEventListener('mousedown',function(e){
    const ed=document.getElementById('cropSizeEditor');
    if(!ed||!ed.classList.contains('show')) return;
    if(ed.contains(e.target)||(e.target&&e.target.id==='cropSizeBadge')) return;
    closeCropSizeEditor();
  });
  const cropPresetSelect=document.getElementById('cropPresetSelect');
  if(cropPresetSelect){
    cropPresetSelect.addEventListener('change',function(){
      applyCropPreset(this.value||'free', false);
    });
  }
  const lockRatioEl=document.getElementById('lockRatio');
  if(lockRatioEl){
    lockRatioEl.addEventListener('change',()=>{
      if(!lockRatioEl.checked){
        cropPresetRatio='free';
        const sel=document.getElementById('cropPresetSelect');
        if(sel) sel.value='free';
      }
    });
  }
  function isPointInsideCropBox(clientX, clientY, pad){
    if(!cropAreaEl) return false;
    const p=(typeof pad==='number')?pad:0;
    const rect=cropAreaEl.getBoundingClientRect();
    const mx=clientX-rect.left;
    const my=clientY-rect.top;
    return mx>=cropBox.x-p && mx<=cropBox.x+cropBox.w+p &&
           my>=cropBox.y-p && my<=cropBox.y+cropBox.h+p;
  }
  function isCropInteractiveTarget(target){
    if(!target||!target.closest) return false;
    return !!(target.classList&&target.classList.contains('crop-handle'))||
           !!target.closest('.crop-handle')||
           target.id==='cropRect'||
           !!target.closest('.crop-rect');
  }
  function isMobileTouchUI(){
    return window.matchMedia('(hover: none) and (pointer: coarse)').matches||
      (('ontouchstart' in window||navigator.maxTouchPoints>0)&&window.innerWidth<=900);
  }
  /* Mobile only: briefly allow native page zoom outside purple box */
  function softUnlockForNativeZoom(){
    if(!isMobileTouchUI()) return;
    document.body.style.position='';
    document.body.style.top='';
    document.body.style.left='';
    document.body.style.right='';
    document.body.style.width='';
    document.body.style.overflow='';
    document.documentElement.style.overflow='';
    document.body.style.touchAction='auto';
  }
  function restoreCropBodyLock(){
    if(!isMobileTouchUI()) return;
    const modal=document.getElementById('cropModal');
    if(!modal||!modal.classList.contains('show')) return;
    const scrollY=parseInt(document.body.dataset.cropScrollY||'0',10)||0;
    document.documentElement.style.overflow='hidden';
    document.body.style.overflow='hidden';
    document.body.style.position='fixed';
    document.body.style.top=(-scrollY)+'px';
    document.body.style.left='0';
    document.body.style.right='0';
    document.body.style.width='100%';
  }
  if(cropAreaEl){
    cropAreaEl.addEventListener('mousedown',onCropStart);
    cropAreaEl.addEventListener('touchstart',function(e){
      if(e.touches&&e.touches.length===2){
        const t0=e.touches[0], t1=e.touches[1];
        /* pad so handles near edges still count as inside for pinch */
        const bothInside=isPointInsideCropBox(t0.clientX,t0.clientY,24)&&isPointInsideCropBox(t1.clientX,t1.clientY,24);
        /* Outside purple box (mobile): do not capture — browser page zoom */
        if(!bothInside){
          softUnlockForNativeZoom();
          return;
        }
        e.preventDefault();
        const dx=t0.clientX-t1.clientX;
        const dy=t0.clientY-t1.clientY;
        cropPinchStartDist=Math.sqrt(dx*dx+dy*dy)||1;
        cropPinchStartZoom=cropViewZoom;
        cropDragging=false;
        return;
      }
      /* 1-finger: allow handles/rect always; block only true outside dark area */
      if(e.touches&&e.touches.length===1){
        const t=e.touches[0];
        if(isCropInteractiveTarget(e.target)){
          /* handle or purple box — always start resize/move */
        }else if(!isPointInsideCropBox(t.clientX,t.clientY,20)){
          return;
        }
      }
      onCropStart(e);
    },{passive:false});
    cropAreaEl.addEventListener('touchmove',function(e){
      if(e.touches&&e.touches.length===2){
        const t0=e.touches[0], t1=e.touches[1];
        const bothInside=isPointInsideCropBox(t0.clientX,t0.clientY,24)&&isPointInsideCropBox(t1.clientX,t1.clientY,24);
        if(!bothInside){
          softUnlockForNativeZoom();
          return; /* native page zoom — no preventDefault */
        }
        e.preventDefault();
        const dx=t0.clientX-t1.clientX;
        const dy=t0.clientY-t1.clientY;
        const dist=Math.sqrt(dx*dx+dy*dy)||1;
        const prev=cropViewZoom;
        const next=Math.max(0.5, Math.min(4, cropPinchStartZoom*(dist/cropPinchStartDist)));
        if(Math.abs(next-prev)<0.01) return;
        cropViewZoom=next;
        if(!cropAreaEl._zoomRaf){
          cropAreaEl._zoomRaf=requestAnimationFrame(()=>{
            cropAreaEl._zoomRaf=0;
            applyCropZoom(prev);
          });
        }
        return;
      }
      onCropMove(e);
    },{passive:false});
    document.addEventListener('touchend',function(){
      if(isMobileTouchUI()) restoreCropBodyLock();
    },{passive:true});
    document.addEventListener('touchcancel',function(){
      if(isMobileTouchUI()) restoreCropBodyLock();
    },{passive:true});
    /* Wheel zoom only inside crop rectangle */
    cropAreaEl.addEventListener('wheel',function(e){
      if(!document.getElementById('cropModal')?.classList.contains('show')) return;
      if(!isPointInsideCropBox(e.clientX,e.clientY)) return;
      e.preventDefault();
      e.stopPropagation();
      const delta=e.deltaY>0?-0.12:0.12;
      const prev=cropViewZoom;
      cropViewZoom=Math.max(0.5, Math.min(4, cropViewZoom+delta));
      if(cropViewZoom===prev) return;
      applyCropZoom(prev);
    },{passive:false});
  }
  document.addEventListener('mousemove',onCropMove);
  document.addEventListener('mouseup',onCropEnd);
  document.addEventListener('touchmove',function(e){
    if(!cropDragging||!cropDragMode) return;
    onCropMove(e);
  },{passive:false});
  document.addEventListener('touchend',onCropEnd);
  /* Exit only via header close (X) — no backdrop click close */
  if(cropModal){
    cropModal.addEventListener('click',e=>{ e.stopPropagation(); });
  }
  document.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{
    const open=cropModal&&cropModal.classList.contains('show');
    if(!open) return;
    if(e.key==='Escape'){ closeCropModal(); return; }
    if(e.key==='Enter'&&!e.shiftKey){ e.preventDefault(); applyCropFromModal(); return; }
    if(e.key==='+'||e.key==='='){ e.preventDefault(); const p=cropViewZoom; cropViewZoom=Math.min(4,cropViewZoom+0.15); applyCropZoom(p); return; }
    if(e.key==='-'||e.key==='_'){ e.preventDefault(); const p=cropViewZoom; cropViewZoom=Math.max(0.5,cropViewZoom-0.15); applyCropZoom(p); return; }
    if((e.ctrlKey||e.metaKey)&&e.key==='ArrowLeft'){
      e.preventDefault();
      setCropRotationLive(toSliderDeg(cropRotation)-1);
      return;
    }
    if((e.ctrlKey||e.metaKey)&&e.key==='ArrowRight'){
      e.preventDefault();
      setCropRotationLive(toSliderDeg(cropRotation)+1);
      return;
    }
    if(e.key==='ArrowLeft'){ e.preventDefault(); nudgeCropBox(e.shiftKey?-10:-3,0); return; }
    if(e.key==='ArrowRight'){ e.preventDefault(); nudgeCropBox(e.shiftKey?10:3,0); return; }
    if(e.key==='ArrowUp'){ e.preventDefault(); nudgeCropBox(0,e.shiftKey?-10:-3); return; }
    if(e.key==='ArrowDown'){ e.preventDefault(); nudgeCropBox(0,e.shiftKey?10:3); return; }
    if(e.key==='[' ){ e.preventDefault(); setCropRotationLive(toSliderDeg(cropRotation)-90); return; }
    if(e.key===']' ){ e.preventDefault(); setCropRotationLive(toSliderDeg(cropRotation)+90); return; }
  });

  updateButtonStates();
});
})();
</script>

           '&pn=' + encodeURIComponent(UPI_NAME || 'PDF Tools') +

           encodeURIComponent(getUpiPayLink());
  }

  var AREA_MAP = {
    'pdf-tool-dropAreaCompress':   'compress',
    'pdf-tool-dropAreaConverter':  'converter',
    'pdf-tool-dropAreaMergeSplit': 'mergeSplit',
    'pdf-tool-dropAreaSecurity':   'security'

  var tracked  = { compress:0, converter:0, mergeSplit:0, security:0 };
  var lastAddAt= { compress:0, converter:0, mergeSplit:0, security:0 };

  function isPremium(){
    try{
      if(localStorage.getItem('pdfToolPremium')==='1') return true;
      var k=localStorage.getItem('pdfToolPremiumKey')||'';
      if(k && PREMIUM_KEYS.indexOf(k)>=0) return true;
    }catch(e){}
    return false;
  }
  function setPremium(on, key){
    try{
      if(on){
        localStorage.setItem('pdfToolPremium','1');
        if(key) localStorage.setItem('pdfToolPremiumKey', key);
      }else{
        localStorage.removeItem('pdfToolPremium');
        localStorage.removeItem('pdfToolPremiumKey');
      }
    }catch(e){}
    updatePremiumBadge();
  }
  function isMobile(){
    try{
      return /Android|webOS|iPhone|iPad|iPod|BlackBerry|IEMobile|Opera Mini/i.test(navigator.userAgent||'')
        || (window.matchMedia && matchMedia('(hover:none) and (pointer:coarse)').matches && window.innerWidth<=900);
    }catch(e){ return window.innerWidth<=700; }
  }
  function limitKey(areaId){
    if(areaId==='compress') return 'compress';
    if(areaId==='converter'){
      var ct=document.getElementById('pdf-tool-conversionType');
      return (ct && ct.value==='pdf-to-jpg') ? 'pdf-to-jpg' : 'jpg-to-pdf';
    }
    if(areaId==='mergeSplit'){
      var t=document.getElementById('pdf-tool-mergeSplitType');
      if(t && t.value==='merge') return 'merge';
      return null;
    }
    if(areaId==='security'){
      var st=document.getElementById('pdf-tool-securityType');
      return (st && st.value==='unlock') ? 'unlock' : 'lock';
    }
    return null;
  }
  function maxFor(areaId){
    var key=limitKey(areaId);
    if(!key) return 0;
    var table=isPremium() ? PREMIUM_LIMITS : FREE_LIMITS;
    if(!table[key]) return 0;
    var n=parseInt(isMobile() ? table[key].mobile : table[key].desktop, 10);
    return (isNaN(n)||n<0) ? 0 : n;
  }
  function countFromDom(areaId){
    try{
      if(areaId==='security') return document.querySelectorAll('#securityFileList .sec-file-row').length;
      var idMap={ compress:'file-name-compress', converter:'file-name-converter', mergeSplit:'file-name-mergeSplit' };
      var el=document.getElementById(idMap[areaId]||'');
      if(el){
        var badges=el.querySelectorAll('.file-badge');
        if(badges.length>1) return badges.length;
        if(badges.length===1){
          var tx=badges[0].textContent||'';
          var m=tx.match(/(\d+)\s*files?/i);
          if(m) return parseInt(m[1],10);
          if(tx.trim()) return 1;
        }
        if(!el.textContent.trim()) return 0;
      }
      if(areaId==='converter'||areaId==='compress'||areaId==='mergeSplit'){
        var preview=document.getElementById('pdf-tool-preview');
        if(preview && !preview.classList.contains('hidden-preview')){
          var items=preview.querySelectorAll('.preview-item');
          if(items.length) return items.length;
        }
      }
    }catch(e){}
    return 0;
  }
  function currentCount(areaId){
    var d=countFromDom(areaId), now=Date.now();
    if(d>0){ tracked[areaId]=d; return d; }
    if(lastAddAt[areaId] && (now-lastAddAt[areaId])<1500) return tracked[areaId]||0;
    tracked[areaId]=0; return 0;
  }
  function remember(areaId, files, replace){
    var n=(files&&files.length)||0;
    if(replace) tracked[areaId]=n; else tracked[areaId]=(tracked[areaId]||0)+n;
    lastAddAt[areaId]=Date.now();
    setTimeout(function(){ tracked[areaId]=countFromDom(areaId); }, 500);
    setTimeout(function(){ tracked[areaId]=countFromDom(areaId); }, 1500);
  }
  function clearAllTracked(){
    tracked={ compress:0, converter:0, mergeSplit:0, security:0 };
    lastAddAt={ compress:0, converter:0, mergeSplit:0, security:0 };
  }

     '@media (min-width:700px){'+
  '#cfProModal .cf-card{width:min(780px,94vw);max-height:90vh;overflow:auto}'+

        '#cfProModal{padding:8px}'+
