        '#cfProModal .cf-contact-row{flex-wrap:wrap}'+
        '#cfProModal .cf-contact-btn{flex:1 1 30%;font-size:11.5px;padding:7px 4px}'+
        '#cfProModal .cf-foot{padding:6px 12px 12px;flex-direction:column;align-items:stretch}'+
        '#cfProModal .cf-btn{width:100%}'+
        '#cfProModal .cf-key-row{flex-direction:column}'+
      '}';
    document.head.appendChild(s);
  }

  function waLink(){
    var text=encodeURIComponent('Hi, I paid '+PREMIUM_PRICE+' for PDF Tool Premium. Please send my license key.\nUPI: '+UPI_ID+'\nUTR: ');
    return 'https://wa.me/'+String(WHATSAPP_NUMBER).replace(/\D/g,'')+'?text='+text;
  }
  function tgLink(){
    var text=encodeURIComponent('Hi, I paid '+PREMIUM_PRICE+' for PDF Tool Premium. Please send my license key.\nUPI: '+UPI_ID+'\nUTR: ');
    return 'https://t.me/'+String(TELEGRAM_USERNAME).replace(/^@/,'')+'?text='+text;
  }
  function gmLink(){
    var subject=encodeURIComponent('Premium License Key Request - PDF Tool');
    var body=encodeURIComponent('Hi,\n\nI paid '+PREMIUM_PRICE+' for PDF Tool Premium.\nUPI: '+UPI_ID+'\nUTR / Transaction ID: \n\nPlease send my license key.\n\nThanks');
    return 'mailto:'+GMAIL_ADDRESS+'?subject='+subject+'&body='+body;
  }

  function ensureModal(){
    ensureCSS();
    if(document.getElementById('cfProModal')) return;
    var m=document.createElement('div');
    m.id='cfProModal';

    var qrHtml = '<img class="cf-qr" id="cfAutoQr" src="'+getAutoQrUrl()+'" alt="UPI QR" loading="lazy">';

    m.innerHTML=
      '<div class="cf-card" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="cfProTitle">'+
        '<div class="cf-hero">'+
          '<div class="cf-badge">★ PREMIUM</div>'+
          '<h3 id="cfProTitle">Unlock full power</h3>'+
          '<p id="cfProReason">Free plan limit reached. Upgrade once and batch up to 50 files.</p>'+
          '<div class="cf-price-row"><div class="cf-price">'+PREMIUM_PRICE+'</div><div class="cf-price-sub">'+PREMIUM_LABEL+'</div></div>'+
        '</div>'+
        '<div class="cf-body">'+
          '<ul class="cf-features">'+
            '<li><i class="fa-solid fa-check"></i><span>Up to <strong>50 files</strong> per batch</span></li>'+
            '<li><i class="fa-solid fa-check"></i><span>One-time payment — no subscription</span></li>'+
            '<li><i class="fa-solid fa-check"></i><span>Instant unlock with license key</span></li>'+
            '<li><i class="fa-solid fa-check"></i><span>Files stay private (browser only)</span></li>'+
      '<li><i class="fa-solid fa-check"></i><span>Free life-time customer support (Whatsapp Text Chat)</span></li>'+
          '</ul>'+
          '<div class="cf-pay">'+
            '<div class="cf-pay-title"><i class="fa-solid fa-qrcode"></i> Pay with UPI</div>'+
            '<div class="cf-pay-options">'+
              '<div class="cf-qr-col">'+qrHtml+'</div>'+
              '<div class="cf-upi-col">'+
                '<div class="cf-upi-box"><span><small style="font-weight:600;color:#64748b;display:block;margin-bottom:1px;font-size:10.5px">'+UPI_NAME+'</small><code id="cfUpiId">'+UPI_ID+'</code></span>'+
                '<button type="button" class="cf-copy" id="cfCopyUpi">Copy</button></div>'+
                '<a class="cf-upi-btn" id="cfUpiAppBtn" href="'+getUpiPayLink()+'"><i class="fa-solid fa-mobile-screen-button"></i> Pay with UPI App</a>'+
              '</div>'+
            '</div>'+
            '<p class="cf-note">'+SUPPORT_NOTE+'</p>'+
            '<div class="cf-contact-row">'+
              '<a class="cf-contact-btn cf-wa" id="cfWaBtn" href="'+waLink()+'" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><i class="fa-brands fa-whatsapp"></i> WhatsApp</a>'+
              '<a class="cf-contact-btn cf-tg" id="cfTgBtn" href="'+tgLink()+'" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><i class="fa-brands fa-telegram"></i> Telegram</a>'+
              '<a class="cf-contact-btn cf-gm" id="cfGmBtn" href="'+gmLink()+'"><i class="fa-solid fa-envelope"></i> Gmail</a>'+
            '</div>'+
          '</div>'+
          '<div class="cf-divider">Already paid?</div>'+
          '<div class="cf-key-row">'+
            '<input id="cfKeyInput" type="text" placeholder="Enter license key" autocomplete="off" spellcheck="false">'+
            '<button type="button" class="cf-btn cf-btn-primary" id="cfKeyBtn">Unlock</button>'+
          '</div>'+
          '<div class="cf-success" id="cfKeySuccess">Premium unlocked. You can now upload up to 50 files.</div>'+
        '</div>'+
        '<div class="cf-foot">'+
          '<div class="cf-trust"><i class="fa-solid fa-shield-halved"></i> Secure UPI · No card needed</div>'+
          '<button type="button" class="cf-btn cf-btn-ghost" id="cfProClose">Maybe later</button>'+
        '</div>'+
      '</div>';
    document.body.appendChild(m);
    m.addEventListener('click', function(e){ if(e.target===m) hideModal(); });
    document.getElementById('cfProClose').onclick=hideModal;
    document.getElementById('cfCopyUpi').onclick=function(){
      var id=UPI_ID;
      if(navigator.clipboard&&navigator.clipboard.writeText){
        navigator.clipboard.writeText(id).then(function(){ flashCopy(); });
      }else{
        var t=document.createElement('textarea'); t.value=id; document.body.appendChild(t); t.select();
        try{ document.execCommand('copy'); }catch(e){}
        document.body.removeChild(t); flashCopy();
      }
    };
    document.getElementById('cfKeyBtn').onclick=function(){
      var v=(document.getElementById('cfKeyInput').value||'').trim();
      if(tryUnlock(v)){
        var ok=document.getElementById('cfKeySuccess');
        if(ok) ok.style.display='block';
        setTimeout(hideModal, 900);
      }else{
        alert('Invalid license key. Please check and try again, or contact support after payment.');
      }
    };
    document.getElementById('cfKeyInput').addEventListener('keydown', function(e){
      if(e.key==='Enter') document.getElementById('cfKeyBtn').click();
    });
  }
  function flashCopy(){
    var b=document.getElementById('cfCopyUpi');
    if(!b) return;
    var old=b.textContent; b.textContent='Copied';
    setTimeout(function(){ b.textContent=old; }, 1200);
  }
  function showModal(reason){
    ensureModal();
    // refresh dynamic parts
    var qrImg = document.getElementById('cfAutoQr');
    if(qrImg) qrImg.src = getAutoQrUrl();
    var upiCode = document.getElementById('cfUpiId');
    if(upiCode) upiCode.textContent = UPI_ID;
    var upiBtn = document.getElementById('cfUpiAppBtn');
    if(upiBtn) upiBtn.href = getUpiPayLink();
    var wa = document.getElementById('cfWaBtn');
    if(wa) wa.href = waLink();
    var tg = document.getElementById('cfTgBtn');
    if(tg) tg.href = tgLink();
    var gm = document.getElementById('cfGmBtn');
    if(gm) gm.href = gmLink();

    var r=document.getElementById('cfProReason');
    if(r && reason) r.textContent=reason;
    var ok=document.getElementById('cfKeySuccess');
    if(ok) ok.style.display='none';
    document.getElementById('cfProModal').classList.add('show');
  }
  function hideModal(){
    var m=document.getElementById('cfProModal');
    if(m) m.classList.remove('show');
  }
  function tryUnlock(key){
    if(!key) return false;
    if(PREMIUM_KEYS.indexOf(key)>=0){
      setPremium(true, key);
      if(typeof showError==='function') showError('Premium unlocked — up to 50 files enabled.', false);
      return true;
    }
    return false;
  }
  function updatePremiumBadge(){
    ensureCSS();
    var box=document.querySelector('.pdf-tool-container');
    if(!box) return;
    var b=document.getElementById('cfProBadge');
    if(!b){ b=document.createElement('div'); b.id='cfProBadge'; b.innerHTML='★ Premium'; box.appendChild(b); }
    b.classList.toggle('on', isPremium());
  }
  function notifyLimit(max){
    if(isPremium()){
      if(typeof showError==='function') showError('Maximum '+max+' files allowed.', true);
      else alert('Maximum '+max+' files allowed.');
      return;
    }
    // Proper characters — no HTML entities
  showModal('Free plan allows up to ' + max + ' files at once. Upgrade for up to 50 files \u2014 one-time ' + PREMIUM_PRICE + '.');
  
  }

  function filterFiles(areaId, files, replace){
    var max=maxFor(areaId);
    if(!max) return Array.prototype.slice.call(files||[]);
    var list=Array.prototype.slice.call(files||[]);
    var existing=replace?0:currentCount(areaId);
    var room=Math.max(0, max-existing);
    if(list.length>room){ notifyLimit(max); list=list.slice(0,room); }
    return list;
  }
  function callHandle(areaId, files, replace){
    var fn=window.handleFiles;
    if(typeof fn!=='function') return;
    remember(areaId, files, replace);
    return fn(areaId, files, replace);
  }
  function acceptFor(areaId){
    if(areaId==='converter'){
      var ct=document.getElementById('pdf-tool-conversionType');
      return (ct && ct.value==='pdf-to-jpg') ? 'application/pdf' : 'image/*';
    }
    return 'application/pdf';
  }
  function openPicker(areaId, replace){
    var max=maxFor(areaId);
    if(max>0 && !replace && currentCount(areaId)>=max){ notifyLimit(max); return; }
    var input=document.createElement('input');
    input.type='file'; input.accept=acceptFor(areaId); input.multiple=true; input.style.display='none';
    document.body.appendChild(input);
    input.onchange=function(){
      var files=Array.prototype.slice.call(input.files||[]);
      try{ document.body.removeChild(input); }catch(e){}
      if(!files.length) return;
      files=filterFiles(areaId, files, replace);
      if(!files.length) return;
      callHandle(areaId, files, replace);
    };
    input.oncancel=function(){ try{ document.body.removeChild(input); }catch(e){} };
    input.click();
  }
  function areaIdFromBtn(btn){
    var area=btn.closest('.drag-drop-area');
    if(area && AREA_MAP[area.id]) return AREA_MAP[area.id];
    var id=btn.id||'';
    if(id.indexOf('Compress')>=0) return 'compress';
    if(id.indexOf('Converter')>=0) return 'converter';
    if(id.indexOf('Merge')>=0) return 'mergeSplit';
    if(id.indexOf('Security')>=0) return 'security';
    return null;
  }

  document.addEventListener('click', function(e){
    var btn=e.target.closest && e.target.closest('.small-btn, .add-more-btn, [id^="addMore"]');
    if(!btn) return;
    var areaId=areaIdFromBtn(btn);
    if(!areaId || !limitKey(areaId)) return;
    e.preventDefault(); e.stopImmediatePropagation();
    var isAddMore=btn.classList.contains('add-more-btn') || (btn.id&&btn.id.indexOf('addMore')===0);
    openPicker(areaId, !isAddMore);
  }, true);

  document.addEventListener('click', function(e){
    var btn=e.target.closest && e.target.closest('.reset-btn, [id*="reset"], [id*="Reset"]');
    if(!btn) return;
    setTimeout(clearAllTracked, 50);
    setTimeout(clearAllTracked, 400);
  }, true);

  document.addEventListener('dragover', function(e){
    var area=e.target.closest && e.target.closest('.drag-drop-area');
    if(!area || !AREA_MAP[area.id] || !limitKey(AREA_MAP[area.id])) return;
    e.preventDefault(); area.classList.add('drag-over');
  }, true);
  document.addEventListener('dragleave', function(e){
    var area=e.target.closest && e.target.closest('.drag-drop-area');
    if(area) area.classList.remove('drag-over');
  }, true);
  document.addEventListener('drop', function(e){
    var area=e.target.closest && e.target.closest('.drag-drop-area');
    if(!area || !AREA_MAP[area.id]) return;
    var areaId=AREA_MAP[area.id];
    if(!limitKey(areaId)) return;
    e.preventDefault(); e.stopImmediatePropagation(); area.classList.remove('drag-over');
    var max=maxFor(areaId);
    if(max>0 && currentCount(areaId)>=max){ notifyLimit(max); return; }
    var files=Array.prototype.slice.call((e.dataTransfer&&e.dataTransfer.files)||[]);
    if(!files.length) return;
    files=filterFiles(areaId, files, false);
    if(!files.length) return;
    callHandle(areaId, files, false);
  }, true);

  document.addEventListener('click', function(e){
    if(!(e.target.closest && e.target.closest('.remove-btn, .sec-file-remove'))) return;
    setTimeout(function(){
      ['compress','converter','mergeSplit','security'].forEach(function(id){ tracked[id]=countFromDom(id); });
    }, 200);
  }, true);

  window.cfPdfPremium = {
    isPremium: isPremium,
    unlock: function(k){ return tryUnlock(k); },
    lock: function(){ setPremium(false); },
    showUpgrade: showModal
  };

  function boot(){ updatePremiumBadge(); }
  if(document.readyState==='loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', boot);
  else boot();
  setTimeout(boot, 600);
})();
</script> -->
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
 
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  <script>

// this code block only used for upload file limit without payment

(function(){
  
  var LIMITS = {
    compress:      { desktop: 30, mobile: 10 },
    'jpg-to-pdf':  { desktop: 100, mobile: 50 },
    'pdf-to-jpg':  { desktop: 50, mobile: 30 },
    merge:         { desktop: 50, mobile: 30 },
    lock:          { desktop: 50, mobile: 10 },
    unlock:        { desktop: 50, mobile: 10 }
  };


  var AREA_MAP = {
    'pdf-tool-dropAreaCompress':   'compress',
    'pdf-tool-dropAreaConverter':  'converter',
    'pdf-tool-dropAreaMergeSplit': 'mergeSplit',
    'pdf-tool-dropAreaSecurity':   'security'
  };

  var tracked = { compress:0, converter:0, mergeSplit:0, security:0 };
  var lastAddAt = { compress:0, converter:0, mergeSplit:0, security:0 };

  function isMobile(){
    try{
      return /Android|webOS|iPhone|iPad|iPod|BlackBerry|IEMobile|Opera Mini/i.test(navigator.userAgent||'')
        || (window.matchMedia && matchMedia('(hover:none) and (pointer:coarse)').matches && window.innerWidth<=900);
    }catch(e){ return window.innerWidth<=700; }
  }

  function limitKey(areaId){
    if(areaId==='compress') return 'compress';
    if(areaId==='converter'){
      var ct=document.getElementById('pdf-tool-conversionType');
      return (ct && ct.value==='pdf-to-jpg') ? 'pdf-to-jpg' : 'jpg-to-pdf';
    }
    if(areaId==='mergeSplit'){
      var t=document.getElementById('pdf-tool-mergeSplitType');
      if(t && t.value==='merge') return 'merge';
      return null;
    }
    if(areaId==='security'){
      var st=document.getElementById('pdf-tool-securityType');
      return (st && st.value==='unlock') ? 'unlock' : 'lock';
    }
    return null;
  }

  function maxFor(areaId){
    var key=limitKey(areaId);
    if(!key || !LIMITS[key]) return 0;
    var n=parseInt(isMobile() ? LIMITS[key].mobile : LIMITS[key].desktop, 10);
    return (isNaN(n) || n<0) ? 0 : n;
  }

  function countFromDom(areaId){
    try{
      if(areaId==='security'){
        return document.querySelectorAll('#securityFileList .sec-file-row').length;
      }
      var idMap={
        compress:'file-name-compress',
        converter:'file-name-converter',
        mergeSplit:'file-name-mergeSplit'
      };
      var el=document.getElementById(idMap[areaId]||'');
      if(el){
        var badges=el.querySelectorAll('.file-badge');
        if(badges.length>1) return badges.length;
        if(badges.length===1){
          var tx=badges[0].textContent||'';
          var m=tx.match(/(\d+)\s*files?/i);
          if(m) return parseInt(m[1],10);
          if(tx.trim()) return 1;
        }
       
        if(!el.textContent.trim()) return 0;
      }
      if(areaId==='converter' || areaId==='compress' || areaId==='mergeSplit'){
        var preview=document.getElementById('pdf-tool-preview');
        if(preview && !preview.classList.contains('hidden-preview')){
          var items=preview.querySelectorAll('.preview-item');
          if(items.length) return items.length;
        }
      }
    }catch(e){}
    return 0;
  }

 
  function currentCount(areaId){
    var d=countFromDom(areaId);
    var now=Date.now();
    if(d>0){
      tracked[areaId]=d;
      return d;
    }
    
    if(lastAddAt[areaId] && (now - lastAddAt[areaId]) < 1500){
      return tracked[areaId]||0;
    }
    tracked[areaId]=0;
    return 0;
  }

  function notify(msg){
    if(typeof showError==='function'){ try{ showError(msg,true); return; }catch(e){} }
    try{ alert(msg); }catch(e){}
  }

  function filterFiles(areaId, files, replace){
    var max=maxFor(areaId);
    if(!max) return Array.prototype.slice.call(files||[]);
    var list=Array.prototype.slice.call(files||[]);
    var existing=replace ? 0 : currentCount(areaId);
    var room=Math.max(0, max-existing);
    if(list.length>room){
      notify('Maximum '+max+' files allowed'+(isMobile()?' on mobile':'')+'. Extra files were not added.');
      list=list.slice(0,room);
    }
    return list;
  }

  function remember(areaId, files, replace){
    var n=(files&&files.length)||0;
    if(replace) tracked[areaId]=n;
    else tracked[areaId]=(tracked[areaId]||0)+n;
    lastAddAt[areaId]=Date.now();
    setTimeout(function(){
      var d=countFromDom(areaId);
      tracked[areaId]=d;
    }, 500);
    setTimeout(function(){
      var d=countFromDom(areaId);
      tracked[areaId]=d;
    }, 1500);
  }

  function callHandle(areaId, files, replace){
    var fn=window.handleFiles;
    if(typeof fn!=='function'){
      notify('Upload handler not ready. Try again in a moment.');
      return;
    }
    remember(areaId, files, replace);
    return fn(areaId, files, replace);
  }

  function acceptFor(areaId){
    if(areaId==='converter'){
      var ct=document.getElementById('pdf-tool-conversionType');
      return (ct && ct.value==='pdf-to-jpg') ? 'application/pdf' : 'image/*';
    }
    return 'application/pdf';
  }

  function openPicker(areaId, replace){
    var max=maxFor(areaId);
    var have=currentCount(areaId);
    if(max>0 && !replace && have>=max){
      notify('Maximum '+max+' files already added. Remove a file to add more.');
      return;
    }
    var input=document.createElement('input');
    input.type='file';
    input.accept=acceptFor(areaId);
    input.multiple=true;
    input.style.display='none';
    document.body.appendChild(input);
    input.onchange=function(){
      var files=Array.prototype.slice.call(input.files||[]);
      try{ document.body.removeChild(input); }catch(e){}
      if(!files.length) return;
      files=filterFiles(areaId, files, replace);
      if(!files.length) return;
      callHandle(areaId, files, replace);
    };
    input.oncancel=function(){ try{ document.body.removeChild(input); }catch(e){} };
    input.click();
  }

  function areaIdFromBtn(btn){
    var area=btn.closest('.drag-drop-area');
    if(area && AREA_MAP[area.id]) return AREA_MAP[area.id];
    var id=btn.id||'';
    if(id.indexOf('Compress')>=0) return 'compress';
    if(id.indexOf('Converter')>=0) return 'converter';
    if(id.indexOf('Merge')>=0) return 'mergeSplit';
    if(id.indexOf('Security')>=0) return 'security';
    return null;
  }

  function clearAllTracked(){
    tracked.compress=0;
    tracked.converter=0;
    tracked.mergeSplit=0;
    tracked.security=0;
    lastAddAt.compress=0;
    lastAddAt.converter=0;
    lastAddAt.mergeSplit=0;
    lastAddAt.security=0;
  }

 
  document.addEventListener('click', function(e){
    var btn=e.target.closest && e.target.closest('.small-btn, .add-more-btn, [id^="addMore"]');
    if(!btn) return;
    var areaId=areaIdFromBtn(btn);
    if(!areaId) return;
    if(!limitKey(areaId)) return;

    e.preventDefault();
    e.stopImmediatePropagation();

    var isAddMore=btn.classList.contains('add-more-btn') || (btn.id&&btn.id.indexOf('addMore')===0);
    openPicker(areaId, !isAddMore);
  }, true);

  
  document.addEventListener('click', function(e){
    var btn=e.target.closest && e.target.closest('.reset-btn, [id*="reset"], [id*="Reset"]');
    if(!btn) return;
    setTimeout(clearAllTracked, 50);
    setTimeout(clearAllTracked, 300);
    setTimeout(clearAllTracked, 800);
  }, true);

 
  document.addEventListener('dragover', function(e){
    var area=e.target.closest && e.target.closest('.drag-drop-area');
    if(!area || !AREA_MAP[area.id]) return;
    if(!limitKey(AREA_MAP[area.id])) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    area.classList.add('drag-over');
  }, true);

  document.addEventListener('dragleave', function(e){
    var area=e.target.closest && e.target.closest('.drag-drop-area');
    if(area) area.classList.remove('drag-over');
  }, true);

 
  document.addEventListener('drop', function(e){
    var area=e.target.closest && e.target.closest('.drag-drop-area');
    if(!area || !AREA_MAP[area.id]) return;
    var areaId=AREA_MAP[area.id];
    if(!limitKey(areaId)) return;

    e.preventDefault();
    e.stopImmediatePropagation();
    area.classList.remove('drag-over');

    var max=maxFor(areaId);
    var have=currentCount(areaId);
    if(max>0 && have>=max){
      notify('Maximum '+max+' files already added. Remove a file to add more.');
      return;
    }

    var files=Array.prototype.slice.call((e.dataTransfer&&e.dataTransfer.files)||[]);
    if(!files.length) return;
    files=filterFiles(areaId, files, false);
    if(!files.length) return;
    callHandle(areaId, files, false);
  }, true);

 
  document.addEventListener('click', function(e){
    if(!(e.target.closest && e.target.closest('.remove-btn, .sec-file-remove'))) return;
    setTimeout(function(){
      ['compress','converter','mergeSplit','security'].forEach(function(id){
        tracked[id]=countFromDom(id);
      });
    }, 200);
  }, true);
})();

// this code block only used for upload file limit without payment

</script> 
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
   <script>
(function(){
  /* ===== JPG→PDF Advanced Settings Collapse (paste above </body>) ===== */
  function initJpgCollapse(){
    var box = document.getElementById('jpgToPdfSettings');
    if(!box || box.dataset.collapsedReady === '1') return;
    box.dataset.collapsedReady = '1';

    var grid = box.querySelector('.jpg-settings-grid');
    if(!grid) return;

    /* Create toggle button */
    var btn = document.createElement('button');
    btn.type = 'button';
    btn.id = 'jpgAdvancedToggle';
    btn.innerHTML = '<i class="fa-solid fa-chevron-right" id="jpgAdvancedChevron" style="font-size:11px;transition:transform .2s;margin-right:6px"></i><span>Advanced settings (Optional)</span>  ';
    
    
      btn.style.cssText = 'display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;background:none;border:none;color:#2563eb;font-weight:600;font-size:13px;opacity:0.5;cursor:pointer;padding:4px 0;margin:0;width:auto;max-width:100%;text-align:left;user-select:none;-webkit-tap-highlight-color:transparent';

    /* Create panel and move grid inside it */
    var panel = document.createElement('div');
    panel.id = 'jpgAdvancedPanel';
    panel.style.display = 'none';
    panel.dataset.open = '0';

    grid.parentNode.insertBefore(btn, grid);
    panel.appendChild(grid);
    btn.parentNode.insertBefore(panel, btn.nextSibling);

    /* Toggle logic */
    var chevron = document.getElementById('jpgAdvancedChevron');
    btn.addEventListener('click', function(){
      var open = panel.dataset.open === '1';
      panel.style.display = open ? 'none' : 'block';
      panel.dataset.open = open ? '0' : '1';
      if(chevron) chevron.style.transform = open ? '' : 'rotate(90deg)';
    });

    /* When switching to PDF→JPG, keep panel closed */
    var conv = document.getElementById('pdf-tool-conversionType');
    if(conv){
      conv.addEventListener('change', function(){
        panel.style.display = 'none';
        panel.dataset.open = '0';
        if(chevron) chevron.style.transform = '';
      });
    }
  }

  if(document.readyState === 'loading'){
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', initJpgCollapse);
  } else {
    initJpgCollapse();
  }
  /* late safety (in case other scripts re-render) */
  setTimeout(initJpgCollapse, 800);
})();
</script>
  
 
  
  
  
  
  
  
<script>
(function(){
  /* ===== Show "Merge all images" only when 2+ images (JPG→PDF) ===== */
  function updateMergeToggleVisibility(){
    var grp = document.getElementById('mergeImagesGroup');
    if(!grp) return;
    var conv = document.getElementById('pdf-tool-conversionType');
    var isJpgToPdf = conv && conv.value === 'jpg-to-pdf';
    var count = 0;
    try{
      if(window.selectedFiles && Array.isArray(window.selectedFiles.converter)){
        count = window.selectedFiles.converter.length;
      }
    }catch(e){}
    if(!count){
      var preview = document.getElementById('pdf-tool-preview');
      if(preview && !preview.classList.contains('hidden-preview')){
        count = preview.querySelectorAll('.preview-item').length;
      }
    }
    if(!count){
      var fn = document.getElementById('file-name-converter');
      if(fn){
        var multi = fn.querySelector('.file-badge.multi');
        if(multi){
          var m = (multi.textContent||'').match(/(\d+)\s*files?/i);
          if(m) count = parseInt(m[1],10)||0;
        }else if(fn.querySelector('.file-badge')){
          count = 1;
        }
      }
    }
    grp.style.display = (isJpgToPdf && count > 1) ? 'block' : 'none';
  }

  function boot(){
    updateMergeToggleVisibility();

    var conv = document.getElementById('pdf-tool-conversionType');
    if(conv) conv.addEventListener('change', updateMergeToggleVisibility);

    /* Remove / add / reset buttons */
    document.addEventListener('click', function(e){
      if(e.target.closest && (
        e.target.closest('.remove-btn') ||
        e.target.closest('#pdf-tool-resetConverterBtn') ||
        e.target.closest('#addMoreConverterBtn') ||
        e.target.closest('#pdf-tool-dropAreaConverter .small-btn')
      )){
        setTimeout(updateMergeToggleVisibility, 50);
        setTimeout(updateMergeToggleVisibility, 300);
        setTimeout(updateMergeToggleVisibility, 800);
      }
    }, true);

    /* When preview / file-name changes */
    var preview = document.getElementById('pdf-tool-preview');
    var fileName = document.getElementById('file-name-converter');
    if(window.MutationObserver){
      var obs = new MutationObserver(function(){ updateMergeToggleVisibility(); });
      if(preview) obs.observe(preview, {childList:true, subtree:true, attributes:true, attributeFilter:['class']});
      if(fileName) obs.observe(fileName, {childList:true, subtree:true, characterData:true});
    }

    /* Safety polls (covers drag-drop + async processing) */
    var n = 0;
    var t = setInterval(function(){
      updateMergeToggleVisibility();
      if(++n > 40) clearInterval(t);
    }, 500);
  }

  if(document.readyState === 'loading'){
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', boot);
  }else{
    boot();
  }
  setTimeout(boot, 1000);
})();
</script>  
  
  
  
   
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
<script>
(function(){
  'use strict';
  /* Keep Download / Lock / Unlock disabled until multi-file progress reaches n/n.
     Mirrors Compress-tab behaviour. No other features touched. */

  var BTN_IDS = {
    converter:  'pdf-tool-processConverterBtn',
    mergeSplit: 'pdf-tool-processMergeSplitBtn',
    security:   'pdf-tool-processSecurityBtn'
  };
  var NAME_IDS = {
    converter:  'file-name-converter',
    mergeSplit: 'file-name-mergeSplit'
  };

  function $(id){ return document.getElementById(id); }

  function parseProgress(text){
    if(!text) return null;
    var m = String(text).match(/(\d+)\s*\/\s*(\d+)/);
    if(!m) return null;
    var done = parseInt(m[1], 10) || 0;
    var total = parseInt(m[2], 10) || 0;
    if(total < 2) return null;          /* single-file → leave alone */
    return { done: done, total: total, complete: done >= total };
  }

  function progressFor(areaId){
    if(areaId === 'security'){
      var sum = document.querySelector('#securityFileList .sec-list-summary');
      if(sum){
        var p = parseProgress(sum.textContent || '');
        if(p) return p;
      }
      /* fallback: row count vs expect attribute if present */
      var rows = document.querySelectorAll('#securityFileList .sec-file-row').length;
      if(rows > 1){
        var exp = parseInt((sum && sum.dataset.cfTotal) || '0', 10);
        if(exp > 1) return { done: rows, total: exp, complete: rows >= exp };
      }
      return null;
    }
    var el = $(NAME_IDS[areaId]);
    if(!el) return null;
    var badge = el.querySelector('.file-badge.multi span:not(.fb-meta)');
    if(badge){
      var p2 = parseProgress(badge.textContent || '');
      if(p2) return p2;
      /* also check data attributes written by live-counter script */
      var d = parseInt(badge.dataset.cfDone || '', 10);
      var t = parseInt(badge.dataset.cfTotal || '', 10);
      if(t > 1 && !isNaN(d)) return { done: d, total: t, complete: d >= t };
    }
    return null;
  }

  function isStillProcessing(){
    var spin = $('pdf-tool-spinner');
    if(spin && spin.style.display === 'block') return true;
    var pt = $('pdf-tool-progressText');
    if(pt && pt.style.display === 'block' && (pt.textContent || '').trim()) return true;
    return false;
  }

  function enforceButtons(){
    try{
      Object.keys(BTN_IDS).forEach(function(areaId){
        var btn = $(BTN_IDS[areaId]);
        if(!btn) return;
        var prog = progressFor(areaId);
        if(!prog) return;               /* not multi-file → do nothing */
        if(!prog.complete || isStillProcessing()){
          btn.disabled = true;
        }
        /* when complete AND not processing, leave decision to original updateButtonStates */
      });
    }catch(e){}
  }

  /* Observe the places where progress text appears */
  var targets = [
    $('file-name-converter'),
    $('file-name-mergeSplit'),
    $('securityFileList'),
    $('pdf-tool-progressText'),
    $('pdf-tool-spinner'),
    $('pdf-tool-preview')
  ].filter(Boolean);

  if(window.MutationObserver){
    var mo = new MutationObserver(function(){ enforceButtons(); });
    targets.forEach(function(t){
      mo.observe(t, {
        childList: true,
        subtree: true,
        characterData: true,
        attributes: true,
        attributeFilter: ['style', 'class', 'data-cf-done', 'data-cf-total']
      });
    });
  }

  /* Catch original enable attempts (updateButtonStates / setProcessing) */
  var _origUBS = window.updateButtonStates;
  if(typeof _origUBS === 'function' && !_origUBS._cfGate){
    window.updateButtonStates = function(){
      _origUBS.apply(this, arguments);
      enforceButtons();
    };
    window.updateButtonStates._cfGate = true;
  }

  /* Safety poll – very light, only while multi-file progress exists */
  setInterval(function(){
    var need = false;
    Object.keys(BTN_IDS).forEach(function(id){
      if(progressFor(id)) need = true;
    });
    if(need) enforceButtons();
  }, 120);

  /* Reset / mode-switch already clear badges → our gate auto-releases */
  document.addEventListener('click', function(e){
    if(e.target.closest && e.target.closest('.reset-btn, [id*="reset"], [id*="Reset"]')){
      setTimeout(enforceButtons, 60);
      setTimeout(enforceButtons, 400);
    }
  }, true);

  /* Initial */
  enforceButtons();
})();
</script>  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  <script>
(function(){
  'use strict';
  /* Security (Lock/Unlock) only:
     - progress bar + text → just above #securityFileList
     - spinner hidden
     - everything else untouched */

  var list = document.getElementById('securityFileList');
  var bar  = document.getElementById('pdf-tool-progressBar');
  var text = document.getElementById('pdf-tool-progressText');
  var spin = document.getElementById('pdf-tool-spinner');
  if(!list || !bar || !text) return;

  /* placeholder so we can put elements back later */
  var placeholder = document.createElement('div');
  placeholder.id = 'cfSecProgPlaceholder';
  placeholder.style.display = 'none';
  if(bar.parentNode) bar.parentNode.insertBefore(placeholder, bar);

  var host = document.createElement('div');
  host.id = 'cfSecProgHost';
  host.style.cssText = 'display:none;margin:10px 0 6px;';
  list.parentNode.insertBefore(host, list);

  function isSecurityTab(){
    var t = document.querySelector('.pdf-tool-tab.active');
    return t && t.getAttribute('data-tab') === 'pdf-tool-security';
  }
  function isSecProcessing(){
    var pt = (text.textContent || '').trim();
    return /Encrypting|Unlocking/i.test(pt) ||
           (bar.style.display === 'block' && isSecurityTab());
  }

  function moveToHost(){
    if(host.contains(bar)) return;
    host.appendChild(text);
    host.appendChild(bar);
    host.style.display = 'block';
    if(spin) spin.style.display = 'none';
  }
  function restore(){
    if(!placeholder.parentNode) return;
    if(text.parentNode === host) placeholder.parentNode.insertBefore(text, placeholder);
    if(bar.parentNode === host)  placeholder.parentNode.insertBefore(bar, placeholder);
    host.style.display = 'none';
  }

  /* watch progress text + bar visibility */
  var mo = new MutationObserver(function(){
    if(!isSecurityTab()){ restore(); return; }
    if(isSecProcessing()){
      moveToHost();
      if(spin) spin.style.display = 'none';   /* force no spinner */
    } else {
      restore();
    }
  });
  mo.observe(text, { childList:true, characterData:true, subtree:true, attributes:true });
  mo.observe(bar,  { attributes:true, attributeFilter:['style'] });
  if(spin) mo.observe(spin, { attributes:true, attributeFilter:['style'] });

  /* also catch setProcessing / setProgressText calls */
  var _setProc = window.setProcessing;
  if(typeof _setProc === 'function' && !_setProc._cfSec){
    window.setProcessing = function(on){
      _setProc.apply(this, arguments);
      if(isSecurityTab()){
        if(on){ moveToHost(); if(spin) spin.style.display='none'; }
        else restore();
      }
    };
    window.setProcessing._cfSec = true;
  }

  /* tab switch → always restore */
  document.querySelectorAll('.pdf-tool-tab').forEach(function(tab){
    tab.addEventListener('click', function(){ setTimeout(restore, 30); });
  });
})();
</script>
 
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
<script>
(function(){
  /* Live 0/N → k/N → N/N
     Fixed for Lock/Unlock: Select PDF + Add More both give 0/10 → 1/10 → … → 10/10
     Remove already works — untouched */
  'use strict';

  var expect = { converter: 0, mergeSplit: 0, security: 0 };

  function $(id){ return document.getElementById(id); }

  function multiSpan(el){
    if(!el) return null;
    var b = el.querySelector('.file-badge.multi');
    if(!b) return null;
    for(var i=0;i<b.children.length;i++){
      var c = b.children[i];
      if(c.tagName==='SPAN' && !c.classList.contains('fb-meta')) return c;
    }
    return null;
  }

  function previewCount(){
    var p = $('pdf-tool-preview');
    if(!p || p.classList.contains('hidden-preview')) return 0;
    return p.querySelectorAll('.preview-item').length;
  }

  function writeBadge(areaId, done, total){
    if(!(total > 1)) return;
    var el = $('file-name-' + areaId);
    if(!el) return;
    done = Math.max(0, Math.min(+done || 0, total));
    var span = multiSpan(el);
    var txt = done + '/' + total + ' files';
    if(span){
      if(span.textContent !== txt){
        span.textContent = txt;
        span.dataset.cfDone = String(done);
        span.dataset.cfTotal = String(total);
      }
      return;
    }
    el.innerHTML =
      '<div class="file-badge multi">' +
        '<i class="fa-solid fa-layer-group"></i>' +
        '<span data-cf-done="'+done+'" data-cf-total="'+total+'">'+txt+'</span>' +
        '<span class="fb-meta"></span>' +
      '</div>';
  }

  function clearArea(areaId){
    expect[areaId] = 0;
    var el = $('file-name-' + areaId);
    if(el) el.innerHTML = '';
    if(areaId === 'security'){
      var sum = document.querySelector('#securityFileList .sec-list-summary');
      if(sum){
        sum.innerHTML = '';
        sum.dataset.cfDone = '';
        sum.dataset.cfTotal = '';
      }
      var list = $('securityFileList');
      if(list) list.dataset.cfPrevRows = '0';
    }
  }

  function existingFromDom(areaId){
    var el = $('file-name-' + areaId);
    var sp = multiSpan(el);
    if(sp && sp.dataset.cfTotal) return parseInt(sp.dataset.cfTotal, 10) || 0;
    if(sp){
      var m = (sp.textContent || '').match(/(\d+)\s*\/\s*(\d+)/);
      if(m) return parseInt(m[2], 10) || 0;
      m = (sp.textContent || '').match(/^(\d+)\s*files?$/i);
      if(m) return parseInt(m[1], 10) || 0;
    }
    if(el && el.querySelector('.file-badge:not(.multi)')) return 1;
    return 0;
  }

  /* Mode switch */
  var convSel = $('pdf-tool-conversionType');
  if(convSel) convSel.addEventListener('change', function(){ clearArea('converter'); });
  var mergeSel = $('pdf-tool-mergeSplitType');
  if(mergeSel) mergeSel.addEventListener('change', function(){ clearArea('mergeSplit'); });
  var secSel = $('pdf-tool-securityType');
  if(secSel) secSel.addEventListener('change', function(){ clearArea('security'); });

  /* Wrap handleFiles */
  function wrapFn(fn){
    if(typeof fn !== 'function' || fn._cfLive) return fn;
    var w = async function(areaId, files, replace){
      try{
        if(files && files.length && (areaId === 'converter' || areaId === 'mergeSplit')){
          var prev = replace ? 0 : Math.max(existingFromDom(areaId), expect[areaId] || 0);
          if(replace) prev = 0;
          var total = prev + files.length;
          expect[areaId] = total;
          if(total > 1) writeBadge(areaId, replace ? 0 : previewCount(), total);
        }
        if(files && files.length && areaId === 'security'){
          var prevS = replace ? 0 : document.querySelectorAll('#securityFileList .sec-file-row').length;
          if(replace) prevS = 0;
          expect.security = prevS + files.length;
          writeSec(0, expect.security);   // 0/10 immediately
        }
      }catch(err){}
      return await fn.apply(this, arguments);
    };
    w._cfLive = true;
    return w;
  }

  (function lockHandleFiles(){
    var raw = window.handleFiles;
    try{
      Object.defineProperty(window, 'handleFiles', {
        configurable: true, enumerable: true,
        get: function(){ return raw; },
        set: function(fn){ raw = wrapFn(fn); }
      });
      if(typeof raw === 'function') raw = wrapFn(raw);
    }catch(e){
      if(typeof window.handleFiles === 'function') window.handleFiles = wrapFn(window.handleFiles);
    }
    setInterval(function(){
      try{
        var f = window.handleFiles;
        if(typeof f === 'function' && !f._cfLive) raw = wrapFn(f);
      }catch(e){}
    }, 1000);
  })();

  /* Security summary writer */
  function writeSec(done, total){
    if(!(total > 0)) return;
    var list = $('securityFileList');
    if(!list) return;
    var sum = list.querySelector('.sec-list-summary');
    done = Math.max(0, Math.min(+done || 0, total));
    var sizePart = '';
    if(sum){
      var raw = sum.textContent || '';
      if(raw.indexOf('|') >= 0) sizePart = ' |' + raw.split('|').slice(1).join('|');
    }
    var html = '<i class="fa-solid fa-layer-group"></i> ' + done + '/' + total +
      ' file' + (total === 1 ? '' : 's') + sizePart;
    if(!sum){
      sum = document.createElement('div');
      sum.className = 'sec-list-summary';
      list.insertBefore(sum, list.firstChild);
      list.classList.add('has-files');
    }
    if(sum.dataset.cfDone === String(done) && sum.dataset.cfTotal === String(total)) return;
    sum.innerHTML = html;
    sum.dataset.cfDone = String(done);
    sum.dataset.cfTotal = String(total);
  }

  /* Converter / Merge plain text → progress */
  function onFileNameMut(areaId){
    var el = $('file-name-' + areaId);
    if(!el) return;
    if(!(el.innerHTML || '').trim()){ expect[areaId] = 0; return; }
    var sp = multiSpan(el);
      if(!sp){
      if(el.querySelector('.file-badge:not(.multi)')) expect[areaId] = 0;
      return;
    }
    var tx = (sp.textContent || '').trim();
    var m = tx.match(/^(\d+)\s*files?$/i);
    if(m){
      var n = parseInt(m[1], 10) || 0;
      if(n > 1){
        expect[areaId] = n;
        writeBadge(areaId, Math.min(previewCount(), n), n);
      }
      return;
    }
    m = tx.match(/(\d+)\s*\/\s*(\d+)/);
    if(m) expect[areaId] = parseInt(m[2], 10) || expect[areaId];
  }

  ['converter', 'mergeSplit'].forEach(function(areaId){
    var el = $('file-name-' + areaId);
    if(!el) return;
    new MutationObserver(function(){ onFileNameMut(areaId); })
      .observe(el, { childList: true, subtree: true, characterData: true });
  });

  var preview = $('pdf-tool-preview');
  if(preview){
    new MutationObserver(function(){ tick(); }).observe(preview, { childList: true });
  }

  /* ========== SECURITY OBSERVER (key fix) ========== */
  var secList = $('securityFileList');
  if(secList){
    new MutationObserver(function(){
      var rows = secList.querySelectorAll('.sec-file-row').length;
      var prevRows = parseInt(secList.dataset.cfPrevRows || '0', 10) || 0;
      secList.dataset.cfPrevRows = String(rows);

      /* IMPORTANT: rows===0 mat zero karo.
         Select PDF full rebuild temporarily empties the list.
         expect.security ko high rehne do. */
      if(rows === 0) return;

      /* Progressive add → total badhao agar zarurat ho */
      if(rows > (expect.security || 0)){
        expect.security = rows;
      }
      /* Real remove (rows kam hue) → total bhi kam karo */
      else if(rows < prevRows){
        expect.security = rows;
      }

      writeSec(rows, Math.max(expect.security || 0, rows));
    }).observe(secList, { childList: true, subtree: true, characterData: true });
  }

  /* Encrypting / Unlocking progress text */
  var prog = $('pdf-tool-progressText');
  if(prog){
    new MutationObserver(function(){
      var pt = prog.textContent || '';
      var m = pt.match(/(?:Encrypting|Unlocking)\s+(\d+)\s+(?:of|\/)\s+(\d+)/i);
      if(m){
        expect.security = parseInt(m[2], 10);
        writeSec(parseInt(m[1], 10), expect.security);
      }
    }).observe(prog, { childList: true, subtree: true, characterData: true });
  }

  /* Live tick */
  function tick(){
    try{
      var n = previewCount();
      if(expect.converter > 1){
        var elC = $('file-name-converter');
        
        
                    if(elC && !(elC.innerHTML || '').trim() && n === 0){ expect.converter = 0; window._cfPrevConvN = 0; }
        else {
          var prevN = window._cfPrevConvN || 0;
          /* Sirf REMOVE par total kam; upload par 0/10 → 1/10 → … → 10/10 */
          if(n < prevN && n < expect.converter) expect.converter = n;
          window._cfPrevConvN = n;
          if(expect.converter > 1) writeBadge('converter', Math.min(n, expect.converter), expect.converter);
        }
        
        
      }
      if(expect.mergeSplit > 1){
        var elM = $('file-name-mergeSplit');
        if(elM && !(elM.innerHTML || '').trim() && n === 0) expect.mergeSplit = 0;
        else writeBadge('mergeSplit', Math.min(n, expect.mergeSplit), expect.mergeSplit);
      }
      if(expect.security > 0){
        var rows = document.querySelectorAll('#securityFileList .sec-file-row').length;
        if(rows > 0){
          writeSec(rows, Math.max(expect.security, rows));
        }
        /* rows===0 pe expect mat zero karo — rebuild ke dauran temporary hota hai */
      }
    }catch(e){}
  }
  setInterval(tick, 50);

  /* Reset buttons */
  document.addEventListener('click', function(e){
    var btn = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('.reset-btn, [id*="reset"], [id*="Reset"]');
    if(!btn) return;
    var id = (btn.id || '').toLowerCase();
    if(id.indexOf('converter') >= 0) clearArea('converter');
    if(id.indexOf('merge') >= 0) clearArea('mergeSplit');
    if(id.indexOf('security') >= 0) clearArea('security');
  }, true);
})();
</script>  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  <script>
    //Advanced settings pe click → direction, orientation, margins, border khule for layout tab
(function(){
  function initLayoutAdvanced(){
    var btn = document.getElementById('layoutAdvancedToggle');
    var panel = document.getElementById('layoutAdvancedPanel');
    var chevron = document.getElementById('layoutAdvancedChevron');
    if(!btn || !panel || btn.dataset.ready === '1') return;
    btn.dataset.ready = '1';
    btn.addEventListener('click', function(e){
      e.preventDefault();
      e.stopPropagation();
      var open = panel.dataset.open === '1';
      panel.style.display = open ? 'none' : 'block';
      panel.dataset.open = open ? '0' : '1';
      if(chevron) chevron.style.transform = open ? '' : 'rotate(90deg)';
    });
  }
  if(document.readyState === 'loading'){
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', initLayoutAdvanced);
  } else {
    initLayoutAdvanced();
  }
  setTimeout(initLayoutAdvanced, 500);
})();
</script>
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
<script>
(function(){
  'use strict';
  /* JPG→PDF Output Size — fast exact size, no stuck Calculating on multi-remove.
     Paste just above </body>. No side-effects. */

  var _lastBytes = 0;
  var _timer = null;
  var _estToken = 0;
  var _booted = false;
  var WRAP_ID = 'cfJpgSizeWrap';
  var BADGE_MARGIN = '6px 0 0 5px';

  function $(id){ return document.getElementById(id); }

  function isJpgToPdf(){
    var ct = $('pdf-tool-conversionType');
    return !!(ct && ct.value === 'jpg-to-pdf');
  }

  function isMergeOn(){
    var el = $('mergeImagesCheck');
    return !el || el.checked !== false;
  }

  function getQuality(){
    var el = $('jpgOutputQuality');
    return parseFloat(el && el.value) || 0.72;
  }

  function formatSize(b){
    if (!b || b <= 0) return '';
    if (b >= 1048576) return (b / 1048576).toFixed(2) + ' MB';
    return (b / 1024).toFixed(1) + ' KB';
  }

  function ensureWrap(){
    var nameEl = $('file-name-converter');
    if (!nameEl || !nameEl.parentNode) return null;
    var wrap = $(WRAP_ID);
    if (!wrap){
      wrap = document.createElement('div');
      wrap.id = WRAP_ID;
      wrap.style.cssText = 'display:none;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px;margin:2px 0 8px;line-height:1.2;vertical-align:middle;';
      if (nameEl.nextSibling) nameEl.parentNode.insertBefore(wrap, nameEl.nextSibling);
      else nameEl.parentNode.appendChild(wrap);
    }
    return wrap;
  }

  function hideSize(){
    var wrap = $(WRAP_ID);
    if (wrap){ wrap.style.display = 'none'; wrap.innerHTML = ''; }
  }

  function showCalculating(){
    if (!isJpgToPdf() || !isMergeOn()){ hideSize(); return; }
    var nameEl = $('file-name-converter');
    if (!nameEl || !nameEl.innerHTML.trim()){ hideSize(); return; }
    var wrap = ensureWrap();
    if (!wrap) return;
    wrap.style.display = 'inline-flex';
    wrap.innerHTML =
      '<div class="file-badge" style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#f8fafc,#f1f5f9);border:1px solid #e2e8f0;color:#64748b;margin:' + BADGE_MARGIN + ';">' +
        '<i class="fa-solid fa-spinner fa-spin" style="color:#94a3b8"></i>' +
        '<span>Calculating PDF size\u2026</span>' +
      '</div>';
  }

  function showSize(bytes){
    if (!isJpgToPdf() || !isMergeOn()){ hideSize(); return; }
    if (!(bytes > 0)){ hideSize(); return; }
    _lastBytes = bytes;
    var nameEl = $('file-name-converter');
    if (!nameEl || !nameEl.innerHTML.trim()){ hideSize(); return; }
    var wrap = ensureWrap();
    if (!wrap) return;
    wrap.style.display = 'inline-flex';
    wrap.innerHTML =
      '<div class="file-badge jpg-pdf-out-size" style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#fff1f2,#ffe4e6);border:1px solid #fb7185;color:#9f1239;margin:' + BADGE_MARGIN + ';">' +
        '<i class="fa-solid fa-file-pdf" style="color:#e11d48"></i>' +
        '<span>Output PDF</span>' +
        '<span class="fb-meta" style="color:#9f1239;font-weight:700">\u00a0' + formatSize(bytes) + '</span>' +
      '</div>';
  }

  function clearAll(){
    _lastBytes = 0;
    _estToken++; /* cancel any in-flight measure */
    clearTimeout(_timer);
    hideSize();
    document.querySelectorAll('#pdf-tool-preview .est-size').forEach(function(n){ n.remove(); });
  }

  function layoutRow(){
    var nameEl = $('file-name-converter');
    var wrap = $(WRAP_ID);
    if (!nameEl) return;
    nameEl.style.display = 'inline-flex';
    nameEl.style.alignItems = 'center';
    nameEl.style.flexWrap = 'wrap';
    nameEl.style.gap = '8px';
    nameEl.style.marginBottom = '0';
    nameEl.style.minHeight = '0';
    if (wrap) wrap.style.display = wrap.innerHTML ? 'inline-flex' : 'none';
  }

  function loadImage(src){
    return new Promise(function(res, rej){
      var im = new Image();
      im.onload = function(){ res(im); };
      im.onerror = function(){ rej(new Error('img')); };
      im.src = src;
    });
  }

  function dataUrlToBytes(dataUrl){
    var pure = (dataUrl.split(',')[1] || '');
    var bin = atob(pure);
    var out = new Uint8Array(bin.length);
    for (var i = 0; i < bin.length; i++) out[i] = bin.charCodeAt(i);
    return out;
  }

  /* Parallel compress — same rules as tool */
  function compressForEmbed(dataUrl, q){
    return (async function(){
      if (q >= 0.95){
        try{
          var buf = await fetch(dataUrl).then(function(r){ return r.arrayBuffer(); });
          return new Uint8Array(buf);
        }catch(e){
          return dataUrlToBytes(dataUrl);
        }
      }
      var img = await loadImage(dataUrl);
      var maxDim = (q <= 0.75) ? 1800 : 99999;
      var sc = Math.min(1, maxDim / Math.max(img.naturalWidth, img.naturalHeight));
      var c = document.createElement('canvas');
      c.width = Math.max(1, Math.round(img.naturalWidth * sc));
      c.height = Math.max(1, Math.round(img.naturalHeight * sc));
      c.getContext('2d').drawImage(img, 0, 0, c.width, c.height);
      var qUrl = c.toDataURL('image/jpeg', q);
      c.width = 0; c.height = 0;
      return dataUrlToBytes(qUrl);
    })();
  }

  /* Fast + exact: compress all in parallel, one PDFLib save */
  async function measureExactPdfBytes(token){
    if (typeof PDFLib === 'undefined' || !PDFLib.PDFDocument) return 0;
    var imgs = document.querySelectorAll('#pdf-tool-preview .preview-item img');
    if (!imgs.length) return 0;
    if (token !== _estToken) return -1; /* cancelled */

    var q = getQuality();
    var sources = [];
    for (var i = 0; i < imgs.length; i++) sources.push(imgs[i].currentSrc || imgs[i].src);

    /* parallel compress */
    var compressed = await Promise.all(sources.map(function(src){
      return compressForEmbed(src, q).catch(function(){ return new Uint8Array(0); });
    }));
    if (token !== _estToken) return -1;

    /* still have same image count? */
    var nowCount = document.querySelectorAll('#pdf-tool-preview .preview-item img').length;
    if (nowCount !== sources.length) return -1;

    var pdfDoc = await PDFLib.PDFDocument.create();
    for (var k = 0; k < compressed.length; k++){
      if (token !== _estToken) return -1;
      if (!compressed[k] || !compressed[k].length) continue;
      var jpg;
      try { jpg = await pdfDoc.embedJpg(compressed[k]); }
      catch (e) {
        try { jpg = await pdfDoc.embedPng(compressed[k]); }
        catch (e2) { continue; }
      }
      var page = pdfDoc.addPage([jpg.width, jpg.height]);
      page.drawImage(jpg, { x: 0, y: 0, width: jpg.width, height: jpg.height });
    }
    if (token !== _estToken) return -1;

    var saved = await pdfDoc.save();
    if (token !== _estToken) return -1;
    return saved ? saved.byteLength : 0;
  }

  async function updatePerCardExact(token){
    if (typeof PDFLib === 'undefined') return;
    var items = document.querySelectorAll('#pdf-tool-preview .preview-item');
    var q = getQuality();
    var jobs = [];
    Array.prototype.forEach.call(items, function(item){
      var meta = item.querySelector('.file-meta');
      var img = item.querySelector('img');
      if (!meta || !img) return;
      var src = img.currentSrc || img.src;
      jobs.push((async function(){
        try{
          if (token !== _estToken) return;
          var embedBytes = await compressForEmbed(src, q);
          if (token !== _estToken) return;
          var pdfDoc = await PDFLib.PDFDocument.create();
          var jpg;
          try { jpg = await pdfDoc.embedJpg(embedBytes); }
          catch (e) { jpg = await pdfDoc.embedPng(embedBytes); }
          var page = pdfDoc.addPage([jpg.width, jpg.height]);
          page.drawImage(jpg, { x: 0, y: 0, width: jpg.width, height: jpg.height });
          var saved = await pdfDoc.save();
          if (token !== _estToken) return;
          if (!item.isConnected) return;
          var line = meta.querySelector('.est-size');
          if (!line){ line = document.createElement('div'); line.className = 'est-size'; meta.appendChild(line); }
          line.innerHTML = '\u2193 Output PDF: <b>' + formatSize(saved.byteLength) + '</b>';
        }catch(e){}
      })());
    });
    await Promise.all(jobs);
  }

  function refresh(opts){
    opts = opts || {};
    if (!isJpgToPdf()){ clearAll(); return; }

    var preview = $('pdf-tool-preview');
    var hasItems = preview && !preview.classList.contains('hidden-preview') && preview.querySelector('.preview-item');
    if (!hasItems){ clearAll(); return; }

    clearTimeout(_timer);

    /* show calculating only when we must recompute */
    if (opts.forceCalc || !_lastBytes){
      if (isMergeOn()) showCalculating();
      else hideSize();
    } else if (isMergeOn() && _lastBytes){
      showSize(_lastBytes);
    }
    layoutRow();

    /* debounce: multi-remove settles before measure */
    var delay = opts.immediate ? 80 : 320;
    _timer = setTimeout(function(){
      if (!isJpgToPdf()) return;
      var p = $('pdf-tool-preview');
      var n = p ? p.querySelectorAll('.preview-item').length : 0;
      if (!p || p.classList.contains('hidden-preview') || n === 0){
        clearAll();
        return;
      }

      var token = ++_estToken;

      if (isMergeOn()){
        measureExactPdfBytes(token).then(function(bytes){
          if (token !== _estToken) return; /* cancelled by newer refresh */
          if (!isJpgToPdf()) return;
          /* images gone during measure? */
          var still = document.querySelectorAll('#pdf-tool-preview .preview-item').length;
          if (still === 0){ clearAll(); return; }
          if (bytes < 0){
            /* cancelled mid-way — schedule one more pass */
            refresh({ forceCalc: true });
            return;
          }
          if (bytes > 0) showSize(bytes);
          else hideSize();
          layoutRow();
        }).catch(function(){
          if (token !== _estToken) return;
          /* fail-safe: never leave stuck on Calculating */
          if (_lastBytes > 0) showSize(_lastBytes);
          else hideSize();
        });
      } else {
        hideSize();
        updatePerCardExact(token);
      }
    }, delay);
  }

  function onReplaceUpload(){
    _lastBytes = 0;
    _estToken++;
    clearTimeout(_timer);
    if (isJpgToPdf()) showCalculating();
  }

  function boot(){
    if (_booted) return;
    _booted = true;

    if (!$('cfJpgSizeCSS')){
      var s = document.createElement('style');
      s.id = 'cfJpgSizeCSS';
      s.textContent =
        '#file-name-converter{display:inline-flex!important;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px;margin-bottom:0!important;min-height:0!important;}' +
        '#' + WRAP_ID + '{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px;margin:2px 0 8px;vertical-align:middle;}' +
        '#file-name-converter .file-badge{margin:0!important;}' +
        '@media(max-width:480px){#' + WRAP_ID + '{margin-top:4px;}}';
      document.head.appendChild(s);
    }

    /* ONLY Output Quality */
    var qEl = $('jpgOutputQuality');
    if (qEl) qEl.addEventListener('change', function(){
      _lastBytes = 0;
      refresh({ forceCalc: true, immediate: true });
    });

    var mergeEl = $('mergeImagesCheck');
    if (mergeEl) mergeEl.addEventListener('change', function(){
      _lastBytes = 0;
      refresh({ forceCalc: true, immediate: true });
    });

    var convEl = $('pdf-tool-conversionType');
    if (convEl) convEl.addEventListener('change', function(){
      clearAll();
      setTimeout(function(){ refresh({ forceCalc: true, immediate: true }); }, 100);
    });

    document.addEventListener('click', function(e){
      var btn = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('#pdf-tool-dropAreaConverter .small-btn, #addMoreConverterBtn');
      if (!btn) return;
      var isAddMore = btn.id === 'addMoreConverterBtn' || btn.classList.contains('add-more-btn');
      if (!isAddMore) onReplaceUpload();
      else if (isJpgToPdf() && isMergeOn()) showCalculating();
    }, true);

    document.addEventListener('click', function(e){
      if (e.target.closest && e.target.closest('#pdf-tool-resetConverterBtn')) clearAll();
    }, true);

    /* image add/remove — debounced so multi-remove does not stick */
    var preview = $('pdf-tool-preview');
    if (preview && window.MutationObserver){
      var prevCount = 0;
      var mo = new MutationObserver(function(){
        if (!isJpgToPdf()) return;
        var n = preview.querySelectorAll('.preview-item').length;
        if (n === 0){ clearAll(); prevCount = 0; return; }
        if (n !== prevCount){
          _lastBytes = 0;
          /* forceCalc + normal debounce so rapid removes settle */
          refresh({ forceCalc: true, immediate: false });
        }
        prevCount = n;
      });
      mo.observe(preview, { childList: true });
    }

    var nameEl = $('file-name-converter');
    if (nameEl && window.MutationObserver){
      var nmo = new MutationObserver(function(){
        if (!isJpgToPdf()) return;
        layoutRow();
        if (!isMergeOn()) return;
        if (_lastBytes > 0) showSize(_lastBytes);
        else if (nameEl.innerHTML.trim() && preview && preview.querySelector('.preview-item'))
          showCalculating();
      });
      nmo.observe(nameEl, { childList: true, subtree: true });
    }

    
    
    
    
     window.cfRefreshJpgPdfSize = function(){
      _lastBytes = 0;
      refresh({ forceCalc: true, immediate: true });
    };
   
    
    
    
    
    setTimeout(function(){ refresh({ forceCalc: true }); }, 500);
  }

  if (document.readyState === 'loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', boot);
  else boot();
  setTimeout(boot, 800);
})();
</script>
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
<script>
(function () {
  'use strict';
  /* PDF → JPG slim progress
     Scale from window.cfGetPdfToJpgScale (tool) — do NOT edit scale here
     Only PDF→JPG Download | No success msg | No side effects */

  var convFiles = [];
  var running = false;
  var HOST_ID = 'cfPdfJpgProgHost';

  function $(id) { return document.getElementById(id); }

  function isPdfToJpg() {
    var ct = $('pdf-tool-conversionType');
    return !!(ct && ct.value === 'pdf-to-jpg');
  }

  function isMobile() {
    try {
      return /Android|iPhone|iPad|iPod|Mobile/i.test(navigator.userAgent || '')
        || (window.matchMedia && matchMedia('(hover:none) and (pointer:coarse)').matches);
    } catch (e) { return false; }
  }

  function getQ() {
    var el = $('pdfToJpgQuality');
    return el ? (parseFloat(el.value) || 0.75) : 0.75;
  }

  /* Reads scale from TOOL — fallback only if tool function missing */
  function getScaleAndJpegQ() {
    var q = getQ();
    if (typeof window.cfGetPdfToJpgScale === 'function') {
      return window.cfGetPdfToJpgScale(q);
    }
    var scale = isMobile()
      ? (q >= 0.90 ? 3.5 : 2.5)
      : (q >= 0.90 ? 6 : 4);
    return { scale: scale, jpegQ: q >= 0.90 ? 0.93 : q };
  }

  function fmtEta(ms) {
    if (!isFinite(ms) || ms < 0) return '';
    var s = Math.ceil(ms / 1000);
    if (s < 5) return 'few sec left';
    if (s < 60) return s + ' sec left';
    var m = Math.floor(s / 60);
    var r = s % 60;
    return m + 'm ' + r + 's left';
  }

  function ensureCSS() {
    if ($('cfPdfJpgProgCSS')) return;
    var s = document.createElement('style');
    s.id = 'cfPdfJpgProgCSS';
    s.textContent =
      '#' + HOST_ID + '{display:none;margin:8px 0 6px;padding:7px 10px;border-radius:8px;' +
      'background:#eff6ff;border:1px solid #bfdbfe;font-family:inherit;line-height:1.25}' +
      '#' + HOST_ID + '.show{display:block}' +
      '#' + HOST_ID + ' .cf-top{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;gap:8px;margin-bottom:4px}' +
      '#' + HOST_ID + ' .cf-left{font-size:12px;font-weight:700;color:#1e40af}' +
      '#' + HOST_ID + ' .cf-pct{font-size:12px;font-weight:800;color:#059669;font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums}' +
      '#' + HOST_ID + ' .cf-bar{height:6px;border-radius:999px;background:#e2e8f0;overflow:hidden}' +
      '#' + HOST_ID + ' .cf-fill{height:100%;width:0%;border-radius:999px;' +
      'background:linear-gradient(90deg,#2563eb,#0ea5e9);transition:width .25s ease}' +
      '#' + HOST_ID + ' .cf-sub{margin-top:4px;font-size:11px;font-weight:600;color:#64748b}' +
      '.pdf-tool-container.dark-mode #' + HOST_ID + '{background:#1e293b;border-color:#334155}' +
      '.pdf-tool-container.dark-mode #' + HOST_ID + ' .cf-left{color:#93c5fd}' +
      '.pdf-tool-container.dark-mode #' + HOST_ID + ' .cf-sub{color:#94a3b8}' +
      '.pdf-tool-container.dark-mode #' + HOST_ID + ' .cf-bar{background:#334155}';
    document.head.appendChild(s);
  }

  function ensureHost() {
    ensureCSS();
    var host = $(HOST_ID);
    if (host) return host;
    host = document.createElement('div');
    host.id = HOST_ID;
    host.innerHTML =
      '<div class="cf-top">' +
        '<span class="cf-left" id="cfPdfJpgLeft">PDF to JPG</span>' +
        '<span class="cf-pct" id="cfPdfJpgPct">0%</span>' +
      '</div>' +
      '<div class="cf-bar"><div class="cf-fill" id="cfPdfJpgFill"></div></div>' +
      '<div class="cf-sub" id="cfPdfJpgSub">Starting...</div>';

    var btnGroup = document.querySelector('#pdf-tool-converter .pdf-tool-button-group');
    if (btnGroup && btnGroup.parentNode) {
      btnGroup.parentNode.insertBefore(host, btnGroup);
    } else {
      var tab = $('pdf-tool-converter');
      if (tab) tab.appendChild(host);
      else document.body.appendChild(host);
    }
    return host;
  }

  function showProg(on) {
    if (on && !isPdfToJpg()) return;
    var host = ensureHost();
    if (on) host.classList.add('show');
    else {
      host.classList.remove('show');
      var fill = $('cfPdfJpgFill');
      if (fill) fill.style.width = '0%';
    }
  }

  function updateUI(done, total, fileIdx, fileTotal, pageIdx, pageTotal, etaMs) {
    var pct = total > 0 ? Math.min(100, Math.round((done / total) * 100)) : 0;
    var fill = $('cfPdfJpgFill');
    var pctEl = $('cfPdfJpgPct');
    var left = $('cfPdfJpgLeft');
    var sub = $('cfPdfJpgSub');
    if (fill) fill.style.width = pct + '%';
    if (pctEl) pctEl.textContent = pct + '%';
    if (left) {
      left.textContent = 'Page ' + pageIdx + '/' + pageTotal +
        (fileTotal > 1 ? ('  |  File ' + fileIdx + '/' + fileTotal) : '');
    }
    if (sub) {
      if (pct >= 100) sub.textContent = 'Almost done...';
      else if (done < 1) sub.textContent = 'Starting...';
      else {
        var eta = fmtEta(etaMs);
        sub.textContent = eta
          //? (done + '/' + total + ' pages  |  ' + eta)
          //: (done + '/' + total + ' pages');
        
        
        ? ('Total pages ' + done + '/' + total + '  |  ' + eta)
          : ('Total pages ' + done + '/' + total);
        
      }
    }
  }

  function downloadBlob(blob, name) {
    var finalName = name || 'download.bin';
    try {
      var base = finalName.replace(/\.[^.]+$/, '');
      var ext = (finalName.match(/\.[^.]+$/) || [''])[0];
      var asked = prompt('Enter file name (without extension):', base);
      if (asked === null) return;
      asked = (asked || '').trim() || base;
      asked = asked.replace(/[\\/:*?"<>|]/g, '_');
      if (ext && !asked.toLowerCase().endsWith(ext.toLowerCase())) asked += ext;
      finalName = asked;
    } catch (e) {}
    var a = document.createElement('a');
    a.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    a.download = finalName;
    document.body.appendChild(a);
    a.click();
    document.body.removeChild(a);
  }

  async function renderPage(page, scale, jpegQ) {
    var vp = page.getViewport({ scale: scale });
    var canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
    var ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
    canvas.width = vp.width;
    canvas.height = vp.height;
    await page.render({ canvasContext: ctx, viewport: vp }).promise;
    var dataUrl = canvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg', jpegQ);
    canvas.width = 0;
    canvas.height = 0;
    return dataUrl;
  }

  async function countPages(files) {
    var total = 0;
    for (var i = 0; i < files.length; i++) {
      var ab = await files[i].arrayBuffer();
      var doc = await window.pdfjsLib.getDocument({ data: ab }).promise;
      total += doc.numPages;
      try { doc.destroy(); } catch (e) {}
    }
    return Math.max(1, total);
  }

  async function runConversion(files) {
    if (!files || !files.length) throw new Error('No PDF selected');
    if (typeof JSZip === 'undefined') throw new Error('JSZip not loaded');
    if (!window.pdfjsLib) throw new Error('PDF.js not loaded');

    var sq = getScaleAndJpegQ();
    showProg(true);
    updateUI(0, 1, 0, files.length, 0, 1, 0);

    var totalPages = await countPages(files);
    var done = 0;
    var times = [];
    var tPrev = performance.now();
    var zip = new JSZip();

    for (var fi = 0; fi < files.length; fi++) {
      var file = files[fi];
      var folderName = (file.name || ('file_' + (fi + 1))).replace(/\.pdf$/i, '') || ('file_' + (fi + 1));
      var folder = zip.folder(folderName);
      var ab = await file.arrayBuffer();
      var doc = await window.pdfjsLib.getDocument({ data: ab }).promise;
      var num = doc.numPages;

      for (var p = 1; p <= num; p++) {
        var page = await doc.getPage(p);
        var dataUrl = await renderPage(page, sq.scale, sq.jpegQ);
        folder.file('page_' + p + '.jpg', dataUrl.split(',')[1], { base64: true });

        done++;
        var now = performance.now();
        times.push(now - tPrev);
        tPrev = now;
        var slice = times.slice(-5);
        var avg = slice.reduce(function (a, b) { return a + b; }, 0) / slice.length;
        var eta = avg * (totalPages - done);
        updateUI(done, totalPages, fi + 1, files.length, p, num, eta);
      }
      try { doc.destroy(); } catch (e) {}
    }

    updateUI(totalPages, totalPages, files.length, files.length, 1, 1, 0);

    var blob = await zip.generateAsync({ type: 'blob', compression: 'DEFLATE' });

    if (files.length === 1) {
      var names = Object.keys(zip.files).filter(function (n) { return !zip.files[n].dir; });
      if (names.length === 1) {
        var u8 = await zip.file(names[0]).async('uint8array');
        downloadBlob(new Blob([u8], { type: 'image/jpeg' }),
          (files[0].name || 'page').replace(/\.pdf$/i, '') + '.jpg');
      } else {
        downloadBlob(blob, (files[0].name || 'PDF').replace(/\.pdf$/i, '') + '_JPG.zip');
      }
    } else {
      downloadBlob(blob, 'ComputerFy_PDFs_to_JPG.zip');
    }
  }

  function wrapHandleFiles() {
    var fn = window.handleFiles;
    if (typeof fn !== 'function' || fn._cfJpgProg) return;
    window.handleFiles = async function (areaId, files, replace) {
      try {
        if (areaId === 'converter' && isPdfToJpg() && files && files.length) {
          var list = Array.prototype.slice.call(files);
          if (replace) convFiles = list;
          else convFiles = convFiles.concat(list);
        }
      } catch (e) {}
      return fn.apply(this, arguments);
    };
    window.handleFiles._cfJpgProg = true;
  }

  function syncRemoves() {
    document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
      if (!isPdfToJpg()) return;
      var rm = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('#pdf-tool-preview .remove-btn');
      if (!rm) return;
      var item = rm.closest('.preview-item');
      if (!item) return;
      var idx = parseInt(item.dataset.index, 10);
      if (!isNaN(idx) && idx >= 0 && idx < convFiles.length) convFiles.splice(idx, 1);
    }, true);
  }

  function bindReset() {
    document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
      if (!(e.target.closest && e.target.closest('#pdf-tool-resetConverterBtn'))) return;
      convFiles = [];
      showProg(false);
    }, true);
  }

  function bindTypeChange() {
    var ct = $('pdf-tool-conversionType');
    if (!ct || ct._cfJpgProg) return;
    ct.addEventListener('change', function () {
      convFiles = [];
      showProg(false);
    });
    ct._cfJpgProg = true;
  }

  function showErr(msg) {
    var box = $('pdf-tool-error');
    if (!box) { try { alert(msg); } catch (e) {} return; }
    box.style.display = 'block';
    box.className = 'error';
    box.innerHTML = msg + '<span class="close">\u00d7</span>';
    var c = box.querySelector('.close');
    if (c) c.onclick = function () { box.style.display = 'none'; };
  }

  function wrapDownloadBtn() {
    var btn = $('pdf-tool-processConverterBtn');
    if (!btn || btn._cfJpgProg) return false;
    var orig = btn.onclick;
    btn.onclick = async function (ev) {
      if (!isPdfToJpg()) {
        if (typeof orig === 'function') return orig.call(this, ev);
        return;
      }
      if (running) return;
      if (!convFiles.length) {
        showErr('Please select a PDF first');
        return;
      }
      running = true;
      var prevHtml = btn.innerHTML;
      btn.disabled = true;
      btn.textContent = 'Processing...';
      try {
        await runConversion(convFiles.slice());
      } catch (err) {
        showErr('Conversion error: ' + ((err && err.message) ? err.message : String(err)));
      } finally {
        running = false;
        showProg(false);
        btn.disabled = false;
        btn.innerHTML = prevHtml || '<i class="fa-solid fa-download"></i> Download';
      }
    };
    btn._cfJpgProg = true;
    return true;
  }

  function boot() {
    wrapHandleFiles();
    bindTypeChange();
    syncRemoves();
    bindReset();
    ensureHost();
    showProg(false);
    if (!wrapDownloadBtn()) {
      var n = 0;
      var t = setInterval(function () {
        wrapHandleFiles();
        if (wrapDownloadBtn() || ++n > 40) clearInterval(t);
      }, 250);
    }
    setInterval(wrapHandleFiles, 1500);
  }

  if (document.readyState === 'loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', boot);
  else boot();
  setTimeout(boot, 600);
  setTimeout(boot, 1500);
})();
</script>
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
<script>
(function () {
  'use strict';
  /* ============================================================
     Compress tab ONLY — live 0/N badge (safe, no handleFiles wrap)
     Add More flicker fix | Zero side-effects on rest of tool
     Paste just above </body>
     ============================================================ */

  var lockedTotal = 0;
  var lockedDone  = 0;
  var prevItems   = 0;

  var NAME_ID    = 'file-name-compress';
  var PREVIEW_ID = 'pdf-tool-preview';
  var RESET_ID   = 'pdf-tool-resetCompressBtn';
  var PROG_ID    = 'pdf-tool-progressText';

  function $(id) { return document.getElementById(id); }

  function isCompressTab() {
    var t = document.querySelector('.pdf-tool-tab.active');
    return !!(t && t.getAttribute('data-tab') === 'pdf-tool-compress');
  }

  function itemCount() {
    var p = $(PREVIEW_ID);
    if (!p || p.classList.contains('hidden-preview')) return 0;
    return p.querySelectorAll('.preview-item').length;
  }

  function multiSpan() {
    var el = $(NAME_ID);
    if (!el) return null;
    var b = el.querySelector('.file-badge.multi');
    if (!b) return null;
    for (var i = 0; i < b.children.length; i++) {
      var c = b.children[i];
      if (c.tagName === 'SPAN' && !c.classList.contains('fb-meta')) return c;
    }
    return null;
  }

  function write(done, total) {
    if (!(total > 1)) return;
    done = Math.max(0, Math.min(done, total));
    var el = $(NAME_ID);
    if (!el) return;

    var span = multiSpan();
    var txt  = done + '/' + total + ' files';

    if (span) {
      if (span.textContent !== txt) {
        span.textContent = txt;
        span.dataset.cfDone  = String(done);
        span.dataset.cfTotal = String(total);
      }
      return;
    }

    /* Keep existing total-size meta if present */
    var meta = '';
    var oldMeta = el.querySelector('.file-badge .fb-meta');
    if (oldMeta) meta = (oldMeta.textContent || '').trim();

    el.innerHTML =
      '<div class="file-badge multi">' +
        '<i class="fa-solid fa-layer-group"></i>' +
        '<span data-cf-done="' + done + '" data-cf-total="' + total + '">' + txt + '</span>' +
        '<span class="fb-meta">' + meta + '</span>' +
      '</div>';
  }

  function clear() {
    lockedTotal = 0;
    lockedDone  = 0;
    prevItems   = 0;
  }

  function apply(done, total) {
    if (!(total > 1) && !(lockedTotal > 1)) return;

    /* Total only grows (Add More); shrink only when items really removed */
    if (total > lockedTotal) lockedTotal = total;
    if (lockedTotal < 2) lockedTotal = total;

    var n = itemCount();

    /* Never drop done on "Compressing 0 / N" during Add More */
    var safeDone = Math.max(done, n, lockedDone);
    if (safeDone > lockedTotal) safeDone = lockedTotal;

    lockedDone = safeDone;
    write(safeDone, lockedTotal);
  }

  function syncFromBadgeText() {
    var span = multiSpan();
    if (!span) return;
    var tx = (span.textContent || '').trim();

    /* Tool wrote plain "8 files" → promote to k/8 using current preview count */
    var mPlain = tx.match(/^(\d+)\s*files?$/i);
    if (mPlain) {
      var t = parseInt(mPlain[1], 10) || 0;
      if (t > 1) apply(itemCount(), t);
      return;
    }

    /* Already k/N — adopt total if larger */
    var mKN = tx.match(/^(\d+)\s*\/\s*(\d+)/);
    if (mKN) {
      var d = parseInt(mKN[1], 10) || 0;
      var t2 = parseInt(mKN[2], 10) || 0;
      if (t2 > 1) apply(d, t2);
    }
  }

  function syncFromProgress() {
    var prog = $(PROG_ID);
    if (!prog) return;
    var pt = (prog.textContent || '').trim();
    var m = pt.match(/(?:Compressing|Done)\s+(\d+)\s*\/\s*(\d+)/i);
    if (!m) return;
    var done  = parseInt(m[1], 10) || 0;
    var total = parseInt(m[2], 10) || 0;
    if (total < 2) return;

    /* Add More flash fix: tool starts with "Compressing 0 / N" */
    var n = itemCount();
    if (done === 0 && n > 0) done = n;

    apply(done, total);
  }

  function onPreviewChange() {
    if (!isCompressTab()) return;
    var n = itemCount();

    /* Real remove */
    if (n < prevItems && lockedTotal > 1) {
      lockedTotal = Math.max(n, 0);
      lockedDone  = n;
      if (lockedTotal > 1) write(n, lockedTotal);
      else clear();
      prevItems = n;
      return;
    }

    prevItems = n;

    if (lockedTotal > 1) {
      apply(Math.max(n, lockedDone), lockedTotal);
    } else {
      syncFromBadgeText();
    }
  }

  /* ---------- observers only (no function wrapping) ---------- */

  function watch(el, fn) {
    if (!el || el._cfCompSafe) return;
    el._cfCompSafe = true;
    new MutationObserver(fn).observe(el, {
      childList: true,
      subtree: true,
      characterData: true
    });
  }

  function boot() {
    watch($(NAME_ID), function () {
      if (isCompressTab()) syncFromBadgeText();
    });
    watch($(PREVIEW_ID), function () {
      if (isCompressTab()) onPreviewChange();
    });
    watch($(PROG_ID), function () {
      if (isCompressTab()) syncFromProgress();
    });

    /* light poll — only while compress tab active & multi */
    setInterval(function () {
      if (!isCompressTab()) return;
      if (lockedTotal > 1 || multiSpan()) {
        syncFromProgress();
        onPreviewChange();
        syncFromBadgeText();
      }
    }, 200);
  }

  /* Reset */
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var btn = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('#' + RESET_ID + ', .reset-btn');
    if (!btn) return;
    var id = (btn.id || '').toLowerCase();
    if (id.indexOf('compress') >= 0 || (isCompressTab() && btn.classList.contains('reset-btn'))) {
      setTimeout(clear, 50);
      setTimeout(clear, 400);
    }
  }, true);

  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', boot);
  } else {
    boot();
  }
  setTimeout(boot, 800);
})();
</script>  
  
  
</body>
</html>
